### [47] [Toward Trustworthy Autonomous Science: A Two-Year Community Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12113)
*Rafael Ferreira da Silva,Milad Abolhasani,Peter Beaucage,Laura Biven,Michael Bussmann,Kyle Chard,Ryan Coffee,Stephen DeWitt,Sagar Dolas,Carrie Eckert,David Elbert,Ian Foster,Tirthankar Ghosal,Anna Giannakou,Tom Gibbs,Leslie Hamilton,Glenn Lockwood,Theresa Mayer,Ben Mintz,Raffi Nazikian,Sal Nimer,Amanda Randles,Woong Shin,Sreenivas Rangan Sukumar,Frédéric Suter,Mitra Taheri,Michela Taufer,Draguna Vrabie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 这是一份由橡树岭国家实验室（ORNL）发布的两年期社区路线图报告，针对自主科学（autonomous science）领域提出了更新后的发展框架。该报告是2024年AISLE路线图的延续，鉴于过去一年间领域快速发展（如多智能体系统、自驱动实验室、推理基础模型等的进步以及Genesis Mission的推动），同时面临重大挑战（重大发现结果被修正、智能体在开放式研究中完成率低、引用造假等问题），报告将原始的五个关键维度扩展为七个维度，重点将"信任、验证与可复现性"以及"安全、安全性与治理"提升为独立的核心维度，并新增四个里程碑（M15-M18），制定了两年的实施路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科学领域（如自驱动实验室、多智能体系统）正快速发展，但目前各自主实验室仍以"孤岛"模式运作，缺乏互操作性。同时，领域面临核心矛盾：生成候选发现已不再是难题，但验证发现的可靠性却成为瓶颈，包括错误的旗舰级发现结果、智能体在开放式研究中的低完成率、以及学术会议上的虚假引用等问题，迫切需要建立可信、可验证、可治理的自主科学生态系统。

Method: 基于社区共识的路线图方法：由多元化利益相关者（联邦机构、学术界、产业界）共同参与，对原始AISLE路线图的五个维度进行重新评估，将其中两个横切性议题（信任/验证/可复现性、安全/安全性/治理）提升为独立维度，形成七个维度的框架。对原有M1-M14里程碑进行状态评估（已实现/部分实现/重构/开放），并新增M15-M18四个里程碑，以两年为时间尺度分阶段（第1年：接口、协议采用、验证脚手架；第2年：联邦化、零信任协调、治理）。

Result: 提出了七个核心维度的更新路线图，涵盖原始五个维度（自驱动实验室、互操作性、基础模型与智能体、社区网络、教育与劳动力）的演进，以及两个新独立维度（信任/验证/可复现性；安全/安全性/治理）。评估了原14个里程碑的实施状态并新增4个里程碑。确定了"生产候选发现容易，验证发现困难"是该领域发展的核心张力，并提出以草根网络作为互操作性基础架构，连接联邦项目、国际倡议和商业平台，避免重新形成孤岛。

Conclusion: 自主科学领域已进入新阶段，关键挑战从模型能力转向验证能力。该报告通过将信任、验证与治理提升为一类议题，制定两年期可执行路线图，呼吁学界、产业界和政府协同合作，以草根网络为互操作性织构，构建可信赖的自主科学生态系统，支撑联邦级科学战略并连接多元化参与者。

Abstract: One year ago, the AISLE roadmap argued that autonomous laboratories operated as isolated islands and proposed a grassroots network organized around five critical dimensions. The field has since moved faster than anticipated. Multi-agent systems have produced experimentally validated hypotheses, self-driving laboratories have grown more interoperable and orchestrated, reasoning-trained and domain foundation models have raised the capability ceiling, and the Genesis Mission has placed autonomous experimentation at the center of U.S. federal science strategy, with industry emerging as a primary actor. Progress has met a sobering counter-current, including a corrected flagship discovery result, benchmarks showing that agents which rival experts on closed-ended questions still complete only a fraction of open-ended research, and fabricated citations surfacing at leading venues. We read this as the defining tension of the field. Producing a candidate discovery is no longer the hard part, but verifying it is, and this asymmetry now limits autonomous science more than raw model capability. We update the roadmap around seven dimensions, revisiting the original five and elevating two former cross-cutting concerns, trust, verification, and reproducibility, and safety, security, and governance, to first-class status. We assess the original milestones (M1 through M14) as achieved, partially achieved, reframed, or open, add four new milestones (M15 through M18), and scope the path forward to a two-year horizon. The first year concentrates on interfaces, protocol adoption, and the scaffolding of verification, and the second targets federation, zero-trust coordination, and governance. Throughout, we position the grassroots network as the interoperability fabric that lets national programs, international initiatives, and commercial platforms connect rather than re-silo.

</details>


### [48] [HARP-ME: Closure-Driven Exact Induced Motif Enumeration on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12074)
*Ashwina Kumar,Rupesh Nasre*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HARP-ME 是一个用于精确枚举连通 4 节点诱导子图模体的 GPU 框架，核心思想是"以闭合关系驱动的锚点选择"替代完全的显式遍历：通过选择一组较小的子结构（锚点）进行显式枚举，再利用精确的闭合方程（代数恒等式）推导其余模体计数，同时结合诱导签名复用和分区所有权规则，保证在超显存图上的精确计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确诱导模体枚举是图挖掘基础原语，但在大规模图上存在三大 GPU 难题：(1) 候选扩展高度不规则且按度数倾斜；(2) 重复集合交集主导运行时；(3) 诱导计数需要同时检验边的存在与缺失，且图可能超出显存需要分区。现有 GPU 系统分别解决了部分问题，但缺乏一个统一的精确诱导模体流水线。更深层动机是区分"遍历高效型"与"推导高效型"枚举——现有系统主要优化"如何枚举选定模式"，而 HARP-ME 首次将"推导收益（derivation yield）"作为一等编译目标，即选择锚点使其充分统计量能通过精确闭合关系确定多个剩余模体计数。

Method: 方法包含六阶段流水线：(1) 锚点选择目标函数：将锚点基的选择建模为联合优化问题，最小化显式枚举成本、集合交集成本、分区 halo 放大成本，最大化状态复用与代数闭合收益，由 λ 参数加权平衡。(2) 闭合驱动的锚点合成：在偏序扩展 DAG 上对候选锚点按四个准则评分（枚举成本、复用率、闭合收益、halo 放大），选择最优锚点基（4 节点场景下锚点包括有序楔形与三角形）。(3) 诱导签名复用 σ(φ)=(j, C(φ), M⁺(φ), M⁻(φ))：将候选前沿 C、邻接约束 M⁺ 与非邻接约束 M⁻ 一并编码，仅当完成语义完全一致时才复用状态，避免将具有不同禁止边约束的状态错误合并。(4) GPU 执行模型：使用排序 CSR 邻接表与规范序约束，低成本锚点采用 warp 级执行，重锚点提升到 CTA 级；候选生成采用有序集合交集、压缩与块内聚合。(5) 所有权保留的分区推导：基于全局有序锚点元组定义规范所有者证书，跨 halo 分区的模体仅由锚点所有者分区贡献，避免重复计数。(6) 闭合方程：针对 4 节点模体给出精确分解——三角形锚点按外部邻接数 j=1,2,3 推导 paw/diamond/4-clique；边锚点通过 Aₑ、Bₑ 集合中候选对是否成边推导 4-path 与 4-cycle；星形通过邻居子图的边数与三角形数推导。

Result: 在 Tesla V100 上对 6 个社交、网页、生物与合成图评测：HARP-ME 相比 Pangolin 获得最高 2.11× 加速（Pokec），相对分区 PBE 最高 1.83×，相对 CPU 基线最高 10.73×（RMAT-24），且为所有数据集上最快方法。微架构层面缓存命中率 64%–76%，有效带宽最高达 671 GB/s（RMAT-24），占用率达 84%，warp 效率 88%。主机-设备传输开销显著低于 PBE 分区策略：在 Pokec 从 18.2% 降至 6.4%，LiveJournal 从 21.5% 降至 7.1%，Web-BerkStan 从 16.7% 降至 5.8%，RMAT-24 从 19.8% 降至 6.9%。生物学小图 Bio-CElegans 因并行度不足与重复前沿结构较少，相对加速比较小（1.36× vs Pangolin）。

Conclusion: HARP-ME 证明将"闭合收益"作为锚点选择的编译目标可以有效补充现有面向遍历与复用的 GPU 优化，闭合驱动的锚点选择、诱导签名复用与所有权保留的分区执行三者协同减少了显式扩展与重复诱导检查。其所有权规则兼具正确性（防止重复推导）与系统效率（限制冗余分区数据移动）双重作用。未来工作将扩展至 5 节点及以上更高阶诱导模体，研究自动闭合合成、自适应锚点选择与可扩展多 GPU 执行。

Abstract: Exact induced motif enumeration is a fundamental operation in graph mining, but it remains challenging on GPUs because candidate expansion is irregular, repeated set intersections dominate execution, and induced counting must consider both the presence and absence of edges. We present HARP-ME, which stands for Hierarchical Anchor-Reuse Partitioned Motif Enumeration. It is a GPU framework for the exact enumeration of connected induced four-node motifs. HARP-ME introduces closure-aware compilation, which selects a set of anchors for explicit enumeration by considering traversal cost, algebraic derivation benefits, expected state reuse, and the additional halo overhead caused by graph partitioning. HARP-ME also introduces induced-signature reuse. This technique identifies reusable completion states using candidate-frontier information together with compact constraints representing both adjacency and non-adjacency relationships. For graphs that exceed GPU memory, a canonical anchor-owner rule ensures exact counting across partitions with overlapping halo regions. We do not claim that the individual graphlet closure identities are new. Instead, our contribution is their integration into a GPU execution framework that reduces both explicit candidate expansion and repeated induced-edge checks. Across six social, web, biological, and synthetic graphs, HARP-ME is the fastest among the evaluated methods. It achieves up to 2.11 times speedup over Pangolin, up to 1.83 times speedup over partitioned PBE, and up to 10.73 times speedup over the evaluated CPU baseline. Detailed measurements show cache-hit rates between 64% and 76%, along with substantially lower host-to-device data transfer overhead than PBE-style partitioning. These results demonstrate that optimizing anchor selection for algebraic derivation benefits can complement traversal-based and reuse-based GPU motif enumeration techniques.

</details>


### [49] [FlashDiff: Efficient Regional Execution and Scheduling for Diffusion Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12121)
*Yaqi Qiao,Ping He,Songrun Xie,Ayush Barik,Chensong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashDiff是一个面向扩散模型(图像/视频/音频生成)的推理服务系统，提出"语义补丁并行"(semantic patch parallelism)新范式，通过将潜在表示分解为语义一致的区域，并基于自注意力活动度选择性跳过低价值区域的去噪步骤，从而消除24-66%的计算量，实现端到端延迟降低30-97%、吞吐量提升1.2-2.2倍，且不损失生成质量。系统包含三个核心组件：Patch Partitioner(分区)、Patch Gate(门控)和Patch Scheduler(调度)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在每一步去噪中需处理整个高维潜在空间(全区域执行)，导致单步计算开销极大(GPU利用率可达98.9%)。现有的多GPU并行策略(张量并行、序列并行)虽然能降低单步计算，但因扩散模型激活值-参数量比例的特殊性，通信开销往往抵消甚至超过并行收益(在SD3模型上通信占据76%的单步延迟)。与此同时，扩散过程存在两大内在特性：(1)空间异质性——不同区域精炼速率差异显著(如均匀背景vs.精细纹理)；(2)时间亲和性——相邻步骤的潜在状态高度相关。然而现有系统将去噪视为整体刚性迭代过程，未能利用语义异质性，存在分区税、误差传播、执行气泡三大挑战。

Method: FlashDiff基于反向扩散ODE的概率流框架，统一回答"在哪里计算"和"何时计算"两个问题，包含三大组件：

1. **Patch Partitioner(语义感知分区)**：利用去噪前几步的交叉注意力图识别显著token，聚合为显著性图S，采用基于Otsu方法的递归显著性分区机制，平衡焦点/上下文token数量产生负载均衡的语义一致patch；通过有界二分搜索在线自适应调整patch数量R。

2. **Patch Gate(选择性精炼门控)**：基于自注意力的"精炼重要性分数"R_i^(t)及其相对变化量Δ_i，构建归一化权重w_i^(t)；当w_i^(t)<ε时跳过该patch；设置最大连续跳过数K实现强制重新激活；理论证明(Gronwall不等式)最终误差受O(ε·∑D_n + γ)控制。

3. **Patch Scheduler(负载均衡调度)**：将每个patch封装为单调任务(monotask)加入全局优先队列，支持步骤边界处的抢占式切换；采用局部性感知的最小最大负载分配策略min_g(max L_g + w_j)，实现O(|g|)复杂度的近最优GPU负载均衡。

系统基于NVIDIA TensorRT实现，约5.5K行代码，支持跨模态(T2I/T2V/T2A)和跨架构(DiT系列)。

Result: 在8×H100(T2I/T2V)和4×A100(T2A)集群、超过数万真实请求的在线部署中评估：

- **端到端性能**：相对xDiT和DistriFusion等SOTA基线，延迟降低30-97%，吞吐量提升1.2-2.2×，计算量减少24-66%(StableAudioOpen跳过66%，SD3-Medium 44%，Wan2.1 36%，FLUX.1-dev 24%)
- **质量保持**：PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS/HPSv3等指标与基线持平或略优(表2显示FlashDiff在FLUX上的PSNR=20.98 vs DistriFusion=20.85)
- **开销极小**：自身控制平面调度开销仅0.4-3.7%
- **组件贡献**：Patch Gate和Patch Scheduler均为关键组件，单独使用也优于DistriFusion
- **敏感性**：在GPU数量(2-8)、去噪步数、图像分辨率(512²-2048²)、门控阈值ε等参数变化下均保持优势；在1.5×负载下请求完成速度最高提升25×
- **稳定性**：存在突发负载时仍能维持高吞吐量，而基线迅速饱和

Conclusion: FlashDiff重新定义了扩散模型的执行范式，将刚性整体式去噪转化为语义感知的并行子任务调度。通过三大组件(语义分区、自适应门控、负载均衡调度)的协同设计，在不修改模型和采样器的前提下，显著降低了扩散推理的延迟和资源消耗。理论分析证明了质量误差的可控性边界，且系统在图像、视频、音频三种模态上均验证有效。语义补丁并行可视为对现有LLM服务范式在扩散场景下的重要补充，为大规模生成式AI部署提供了新的效率-质量前沿(pareto frontier)。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the central backbone for modern image, video, and audio generation, but their efficient service remains a challenge. Unlike autoregressive decoding, diffusion inference repeatedly updates high-dimensional spatial or temporal latents over many denoising steps. This all-region execution pattern makes generation latency high and limits serving throughput. Existing multi-GPU parallelization methods can reduce per-step computation, but often introduce substantial activation exchange overhead, causing communication to offset or even outweigh the benefits of parallel execution. This paper presents FlashDiff, a diffusion serving system that improves inference efficiency through adaptive regional execution and scheduling. FlashDiff is based on the observation that diffusion refinement is not uniform across latent regions or denoising steps: different regions often stabilize at different rates, while neighboring steps exhibit strong temporal correlation. FlashDiff leverages these properties to selectively execute only regions that require further refinement and to reallocate the resulting compute slack across concurrent serving requests. FlashDiff consists of three mechanisms. First, it decomposes the latent representation into coherent execution regions using early-stage attention signals, preserving semantic structure while exposing fine-grained parallelism. Second, it uses a lightweight runtime controller to estimate region activity and bypass low-impact updates when further refinement is unlikely to affect output quality. Third, it applies an affinity-aware online scheduler that co-locates dependent regions, balances residual load across GPUs, and reuses reclaimed compute capacity to improve serving efficiency. Across real-world image, video, and audio workloads, FlashDiff reduces end-to-end serving latency by 30-97% and improves throughput by 1.2-2.2x.

</details>


### [50] [Decentralized Gradient Descent: Bottleneck Regimes and Budget Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12172)
*Nicolò Michelusi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了去中心化梯度下降（DGD）在分布式网络中的通信-计算预算复杂度问题。作者提出了一个"瓶颈中心"框架，将优化过程分解为多个阶段，每个阶段由不同因素主导：初始化、目标异质性与网络连通性、梯度噪声、通信噪声。通过引入两个关键量化指标——梯度多样性-网络连通性比（DNR）和梯度-通信噪声比（GCR），本文推导了各瓶颈主导运行区域的预算最优步长选择和显式预算复杂度界限，揭示了总体预算如何分解为各连续瓶颈的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化优化中，虽然DGD的收敛性已被广泛研究，但达到目标精度所需的通信和计算资源（即预算复杂度）尚未得到充分表征。特别是在大规模、资源受限的系统中，通信延迟、传输能耗和计算成本可能主导整体优化过程。不同误差尺度下，不同因素（目标异质性、网络连通性、梯度噪声、通信噪声）依次成为瓶颈，但现有分析通常只关注单一损伤或特定算法增强，缺乏对预算资源如何在不同瓶颈之间分配的系统性理解。

Method: 1. **多阶段公式化**：将优化轨迹划分为S个阶段，每个阶段内步长固定，阶段间根据误差尺度更新参数。误差每阶段减少因子Φ。
2. **两个基本量化指标**：
   - DNR（梯度多样性-网络连通性比）：捕获目标异质性与网络连通性之间的张力
   - GCR（梯度-通信噪声比）：比较梯度噪声与通信噪声的相对影响
3. **单阶段瓶颈分析**：分别分析Local SGD（仅计算）和Full DGD（计算+通信）模式，识别初始化主导、目标异质性主导、梯度噪声主导、通信噪声主导等运行区域。
4. **多阶段预算复杂度**：通过累积各连续瓶颈主导区域的阶段预算，推导出总预算的显式表达式，将总体预算分解为各瓶颈贡献之和。
5. **四参数设置分类**：基于DNR-to-gradient-noise比和GCR，将DNR-GCR参数空间划分为四个区域，每个区域对应不同的瓶颈序列。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：
1. **单阶段预算最优步长**（引理3）：Full DGD的学习步长η_(s)和共识步长γ_(s)的闭式表达式，揭示四种运行区域：(a)通信噪声主导、(c)梯度噪声主导、(e) DNR主导、(f)初始化主导。
2. **阶段间过渡规则**（引理4）：当误差远大于E^loc时Local SGD更优；否则Full DGD更优。Full DGD的初始化主导区域永远不会被激活。
3. **总预算分解**：例如在High-GCR、中等DNR-to-gradient-noise比设置下：
   - B_dgd ≲ b_dgd(5.8 + 2.9ln(E^loc/ε) + 32.8(L²/σ_g²)DNR + 2.5(σ_g²/μ²)(GCR/DNR) + 3276κ²σ_c²DNR/ε⁴)
4. **误差衰减率**：各区域呈现不同衰减行为——初始化主导区几何衰减O(e^(-ct))，DNR主导区O(1/t)，梯度噪声主导区O(1/√t)，通信噪声主导区O(1/⁴√t)。
5. **数值验证**：在N=50个代理的环形网络上验证了四阶段瓶颈序列与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了去中心化优化的资源感知、瓶颈中心框架，揭示了不同误差尺度下不同因素的依次主导机制。提出的两个量化指标DNR和GCR有效刻画了优化动态的瓶颈序列及其预算最优运行策略。预算复杂度界限的显式表达揭示了目标异质性、网络连通性、梯度噪声与通信噪声之间的基本权衡，并展示了在通信噪声成为主导瓶颈时，预算随1/ε⁴增长——即实现高精度愈发昂贵。该框架为资源感知去中心化优化开辟了新方向，即算法需在优化过程中主动适应当前活跃瓶颈。

Abstract: Decentralized gradient descent (DGD) is widely used for solving distributed optimization problems over networks of agents. While its convergence properties are well understood, less is known about the communication and computation resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. In this paper, we study DGD from a resource-aware perspective and characterize the communication-computation budget required to attain a target error level. We develop a bottleneck-centric framework in which different factors dominate the optimization dynamics at different error scales. Specifically, we identify operating regimes governed by initialization, objective heterogeneity and network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise. To capture these effects, we introduce two fundamental quantities: the gradient-Diversity-to-Network-connectivity Ratio (DNR) and the Gradient-to-Communication-noise Ratio (GCR). We show that these quantities determine the sequence of bottlenecks encountered during optimization and the corresponding budget-optimal operating strategy. Using a multi-stage analysis, we derive optimal stepsize selections and explicit budget-complexity bounds that quantify the budget resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. The resulting expressions reveal how the overall budget decomposes into contributions associated with successive bottlenecks and provide insight into the fundamental tradeoffs among objective heterogeneity, network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise.

</details>


### [51] [Overcoming Orchestration Bottlenecks at Exascale: A Decentralized, Policy-Driven Approach for Sim-AI Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12211)
*Harikrishna Tummalapalli,Christine M. Simpson,Riccardo Balin,Vitali A. Morozov,Thang D. Pham,Murat Keceli,Thomas D. Uram*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EnsembleLauncher是一个递归层次化、完全去中心化的工作流编排器，专为百亿级(exascale)系统上异构仿真-AI(Sim-AI)集成设计，通过可编程调度策略接口解决大规模任务编排瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学计算正从单一整体应用转向耦合仿真-AI工作流，这些工作流包含高度异构的任务(从亚秒级串行推理到多节点MPI仿真)。当扩展到百亿级系统(如Aurora超级计算机)时，传统的集中式编排器和系统级调度器(如Slurm)产生严重的吞吐瓶颈：集中式控制平面无法应对百万级并发任务，而现有去中心化工具要么缺乏MPI支持要么学习曲线陡峭。此外，并行任务系统的调度是NP难问题，但现有工具很少暴露可编程调度接口，使领域科学家无法注入定制策略。

Method: 1. 设计原则：通过Dask的对照缩放实验证明控制平面拓扑(而非框架实现)是吞吐量的主要决定因素，扁平拓扑在256节点后失效。2. 架构：EnsembleLauncher采用递归层次化树结构，由Manager(管理子树)和Launcher(执行任务)组成，支持Push(静态分区)和Pull(动态负载均衡)混合模式。3. 核心组件：包含执行器、调度器、资源管理器、通信器(基于ZeroMQ)和检查点器；调度器在Manager级(粗粒度任务路由)和Launcher级(细粒度任务排序)两层均暴露可编程策略接口。4. 执行器抽象：支持进程池、线程池、MPI进程池和直接MPI执行四种后端，实现串行与MPI任务协同调度。5. 容错：通过心跳机制检测故障，子树级检查点-重启。

Result: 1. 缩放性能：在Aurora超级计算机上扩展至8,192节点(最高分配限制)，处理800万串行任务，性能超过Parsl和Dask等最先进工具4倍以上；在2,048节点时，1秒任务的开销比竞品低一个数量级。2. 单节点基准：单Launcher配置3ms中位延迟优于Dask的10ms；102个Launcher配置时吞吐量接近原生ProcessPool。3. 拓扑敏感性：两层层次化(Depth=2)在128节点时维持近满资源利用率，而单层配置资源利用率不超过10%，执行时间长5倍。4. 调度策略影响：高方差任务集合下，Longest First和Largest First策略显著优于FIFO；MOFA工作流原型中，Adaptive负载感知路由策略GPU利用率比Partitioned策略高约2倍。5. 框架对比：Parsl和Dask在同一单层层次化配置下表现出几乎相同的缩放行为，证实拓扑而非实现决定吞吐性能。

Conclusion: EnsembleLauncher通过递归去中心化控制平面消除了限制现有工具的单节点串行化瓶颈，将可扩展性从约10³节点推进到8,192节点以上。工作证明控制平面拓扑(而非具体框架实现)是百亿级任务吞吐量的主要决定因素。可编程调度策略接口允许领域科学家根据工作负载特征定制行为，对调度不当的异构Sim-AI流水线可造成高达2倍的资源利用率损失。未来工作包括实现MCP接口以支持LLM驱动的智能体工作流，以及利用AI驱动的自适应调度策略从观测工作负载行为中实时学习。

Abstract: Scientific computing is increasingly shifting from monolithic applications to coupled simulation-AI workflows composed of highly heterogeneous tasks with diverse hardware, scale, and runtime requirements. As these workflows scale to leadership-class systems, the resulting extreme ensemble sizes and task variability can create orchestration bottlenecks. System-level schedulers are often configured for limited throughput, while workflow tools face scalability issues due to rigid control-plane topologies and static scheduling heuristics. We introduce EnsembleLauncher, a recursively hierarchical workflow orchestrator for exascale systems, featuring a fully decentralized control plane and a programmable scheduling policy interface. On the Aurora supercomputer, EnsembleLauncher successfully scales to the entire machine with up to eight million serial tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art tools by more than four times. Additionally, we implement a programmable scheduling interface and demonstrate a significant impact of scheduling policies on resource utilization for high-variance ensembles and active learning pipelines representative of modern coupled simulation-AI workflows.

</details>


### [52] [Profiling and Scheduling Complex O-RAN Applications Across the 5G Edge and Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12230)
*Yoonjae Hwang,Bhaskar Krishnamachari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 O-DAG 框架,将 O-RAN 切片调度器建模为有向无环图(DAG),通过 DagProfiler 工具进行剖析,利用扩展 SAGA 调度器在远边缘、近边缘、云三层分散基础设施上调度,并在 MintEDGE 模拟器中验证。实验比较了 HEFT、MCT、MinMin、MaxMin、Duplex 五种调度算法,在 5K–50K UE、2–20 小区、2–10 切片的配置下评估。结果表明 HEFT 在所有配置下均取得最短 makespan,但调度器排名具有工作负载依赖性;SAGA-仿真差距可作为计算密集型与通信密集型工作模式的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN 将智能 RAN 控制分解为相互依赖的 AI/ML 功能流水线(如流量预测、信道质量估计、切片调度),这些功能需在远边缘、近边缘和云端组成的分散计算连续体上执行,面临异构的时延和带宽约束。虽然这些流水线天然适合用 DAG 表达,但目前缺乏一种集成方法论来:剖析其执行成本、通过调度启发式将其映射到分散基础设施,并在 5G 蜂窝条件下验证布局结果。现有工具(HEFT、SAGA、MintEDGE、DIME 等)各自只解决了问题的一个片段。

Method: O-DAG 框架包含四个紧密耦合阶段:(1) DagProfiler——开源剖析工具,对 O-RAN 切片调度器进行插桩,提取每个任务的指令数和每条边的通信量;(2) 参数化三层网络拓扑——编码远边缘(DU、RIC)、近边缘(edge)和云节点,具有真实的链路带宽;(3) 扩展 SAGA 调度框架——增加节点固定约束,支持 HEFT 等算法;(4) 基于 MintEDGE 的自定义 DAG 仿真模块。将一个具体的 RAN 切片调度器建模为 8 任务 DAG(T0–T7),包含两个并行 ML 推理分支并在 T5 处汇合的 fork-join 结构。

Result: 评估了五种调度算法(HEFT、MCT、MinMin、MaxMin、Duplex)在三种实验维度(UE 数 5K–50K、小区数 2–20、切片数 2–10)下的表现。主要发现:(1) HEFT 在所有配置下均取得最低 SAGA makespan,优势通常<1%,但在 10 切片时对 MinMin 优势达 4.87%;(2) 调度器排名具有工作负载依赖性——MinMin/MCT 在高切片数和高小区数下退化,MaxMin 反而改善;(3) SAGA-仿真差距充当计算密集型与通信密集型模式的诊断:负差距(低至-1.72%)标识计算密集型配置,而+0.64%的正差距(2 切片)暴露通信受限模式。计算与通信比(CCR)在所有配置下接近 1.0,表明调度器选择需依赖任务成本与链路带宽的精细交互。

Conclusion: O-DAG 是首个将 O-RAN 应用剖析、异构 DAG 调度、节点固定和 5G 蜂窝仿真统一为单一端到端流水线的框架,所有制品均开源以支持可重复性。未来的工作包括:扩展 DagProfiler 以捕获工作负载相关的成本分布;将节点固定约束泛化为带迁移惩罚的软约束;在硬件测试平台上交叉验证;将该框架应用于其他 O-RAN 应用以测试通用性。

Abstract: The O-RAN paradigm decomposes intelligent RAN control into pipelines of interdependent AI/ML functions, including traffic prediction, signal quality estimation, and slice scheduling, that must execute across a dispersed continuum of far-edge, near-edge, and cloud resources under heterogeneous latency and bandwidth constraints. Despite the natural expression of these pipelines as Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs), no integrated methodology exists to profile their execution costs, map them onto dispersed infrastructure via scheduling heuristics, and validate the resulting placement under 5G cellular conditions. We present O-DAG, an end-to-end framework that closes this gap through four tightly coupled stages: (1) DagProfiler, a new open-source tool that instruments O-RAN Slice Scheduler and extracts per-task instruction counts and per-edge communication volumes; (2) a parameterized three-tier network topology encoding far-edge (DU, RIC), near-edge (edge), and cloud nodes with realistic link bandwidths; (3) an extension of the SAGA scheduling framework and (4) a custom DAG simulation module built on the MintEDGE simulator. We evaluate five scheduling algorithms (HEFT, MCT, MinMin, MaxMin, Duplex) for a slice scheduling application across various configurations spanning 5K--50K UEs, 2--20 cells, and 2--10 network slices. HEFT achieves the lowest makespan in all configurations, but scheduler rankings are workload-dependent. The SAGA--simulation gap serves as a regime diagnostic: negative gaps (up to -1.72%) identify compute-dominated configurations where HEFT overestimates conservatively, while a positive gap (+0.64%) at low slice counts exposes a communication-bound regime where bandwidth contention exceeds the scheduling model's assumptions. All artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [53] [Parallelizing Legacy Mesh Generation Software: Lessons Learned from a Pseudo-Constrained Parallel Data Refinement Approach for Advancing Front Local Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12253)
*Kevin Garner,David Marcum,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将NASA工业级顺序网格生成软件AFLR（Advancing Front Local Reconnection）作为黑盒进行并行化的经验教训。作者提出了Pseudo-Constrained Parallel Data Refinement（PsC.AFLR）方法，将AFLR集成到PDR框架中并结合PREMA运行时系统。在16核CPU上实现了约11倍的加速比，且输出网格质量良好。但由于AFLR黑盒的输入边界约束，方法无法完全复制串行版本的网格密度和可扩展性。研究表明，对遗留黑盒代码采用"功能优先"（functionality-first）的并行化策略并不实用，应转向"可扩展性优先"（scalability-first）的设计思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NASA CFD 2030愿景要求高性能网格生成代码能够运行在大规模并行架构上。AFLR是由NASA、DoD、DoE及多家航空航天研究机构使用的工业级顺序四面体网格生成器，具有30年的发展积累。将其并行化以满足高保真模拟需求具有重要意义。然而，AFLR作为复杂遗留软件（超过10万行代码），其并行化面临"功能优先"与"可扩展性优先"两种路径的权衡。理解将此类黑盒软件并行化的可行性与局限性，为未来网格生成软件的并行设计提供经验。

Method: （1）数据分解：采用八叉树（octree）对四面体体积网格进行分解，形成多个子域（leaves），每个子域配置缓冲区（level 1和level 2邻居）以消除并发点插入的数据依赖。（2）初始网格生成：使用TetGen生成满足AFLR密度要求的初始体积网格，从中提取子域边界。（3）并行细化：在PREMA 2.0运行时系统上采用主-从模式，主线程管理子域细化列表和锁定机制，工作线程并行调用AFLR对各子域执行推进波前点插入与局部重连操作。（4）子域接口冻结：细化过程中保持子域内部接口面冻结，完成后合并所有子域进行一次全局最终优化。（5）两种实现策略对比：超子域（super-subdomain）局部重连 vs. 无超子域重连，后者因开销更低、网格质量相当而被采用。（6）针对AFLR的输入边界要求（光滑、单连通的三角化表面），增加了边界提取、重复点处理、松散连接分区检测与重分配等辅助操作。

Result: （1）加速性能：在角形灯泡（horn bulb）几何（106万表面单元）上，使用16核CPU实现约11倍加速比（224秒 vs. 16101秒），细化速度从7,212提升至65,900 elements/sec；火箭几何上也获得约15倍加速比。（2）网格质量：满足CFD求解器（FUN3D、SU2）的运行要求，最小二面角3.52°（角形灯泡）和2.27°（火箭），满足弱可重现性（weak reproducibility）。（3）网格密度差异：PsC.AFLR生成的网格元素数量远少于串行AFLR（角形灯泡约1440万 vs. 1.16亿，火箭约3800万 vs. 1.46亿），原因是子域边界从粗初始网格提取，导致点分布约束。（4）开销分析：边界提取（Boundary Extraction）和分区交换（Partition Swap）是PsC PDR中最耗时的操作；超过32核后由于子域数量固定（4096个）以及跨节点通信开销，性能反而下降。（5）扩展性限制：黑盒边界要求引入显著预处理开销，并限制了理论可扩展性。

Conclusion: 将AFLR等复杂遗留网格生成软件作为黑盒并行化并不实用。其约束来自：子域边界必须冻结导致内部元素细化受限、点分布无法独立控制、预处理开销显著、无法保证输出与串行版本一致。30年积累的复杂代码需要大规模重构才能消除这些约束。建议未来工作应：（1）采用"可扩展性优先"方法，从底层设计为并行架构而生；（2）若继续功能优先路线，需对AFLR源码进行深度修改，使其能处理纯体积输入而无需输入表面；（3）优化分解技术以减少界面元素对质量的约束；（4）通过先处理子域界面元素再处理内部元素的方式减少通信开销。本研究为遗留科学计算代码的并行化策略选择提供了重要参考。

Abstract: This paper presents lessons learned from parallelizing the legacy software known as Advancing Front Local Reconnection (AFLR) as a black box. The parallel procedure utilizes (i) a data decomposition scheme where each subdomain is refined in parallel using the sequential mesh generation code and (ii) a runtime system for work-load balancing. Results on the mesh refinement operation show that the parallel method's stability (output mesh quality) is good and that the parallel method outperforms the serial AFLR by about 11 times when utilizing 16 CPU cores. However, full stability (i.e., generating the same quality as the serial method) and potential scalability is limited due to the constraints set by the black box's input boundary requirement. Satisfying this requirement for each subdomain not only adds overhead but also causes the parallel method to generate a different output mesh volume than that generated by the serial method. The complexity of such a state-of-the-art code requires that it be modified to a non-trivial extent in order to remove these constraints. These results suggest that the parallelization of black-box legacy codes like AFLR may not be practical and instead encourages an approach that is originally designed without such constraints to efficiently leverage the concurrency offered by large-scale architectures.

</details>


### [54] [Scaling Synthetic-Image Pre-Training for Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12583)
*Qianpiao Ma,Xiaozhu Song,Junlong Zhou,Yue Zeng,Jianchun Liu,Huaqing Tu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出FeDiSyn框架，将合成图像预训练与联邦微调(FedFT)统一优化，针对大视觉模型(LVM)在联邦学习中的资源约束、系统异构性和非IID数据三大挑战，通过扩展定律确定最优合成图像数量、扩散模型捕获设备特定特征分布、贡献感知的LoRA配置与带宽分配算法，在真实测试平台上实现完成时间减少52.5%以上、通信成本减少97.2%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调大视觉模型面临三大挑战：(1)资源约束（LVM参数量巨大，设备算力/带宽有限）；(2)系统异构性（设备算力和网络条件差异大，拖慢者问题严重）；(3)非IID数据（标签分布和特征分布均存在偏斜）。现有方法要么在合成图像预训练时只使用文本条件生成（无法捕获设备特征分布），要么在FedFT时仅按设备能力分配LoRA深度（忽视弱设备的关键贡献），更关键的是将预训练和FedFT视为独立阶段，缺乏整体优化视角，无法平衡生成/预训练开销与FedFT收益。

Method: FeDiSyn采用"一个定律、两个优化"的设计：(1)预训练阶段：各设备用轻量VAE将本地图像编码为潜变量，提取聚类中心与类均值后加噪上传；服务器结合LLM扩展的文本提示和CLIP文本编码，用Stable Diffusion生成既保持设备风格特征又具有语义多样性的合成图像。(2)扩展定律：提出H(λ,Acc) = h₀λ + (1/(κ₁λ^κ₂))e^(κ₃Acc) + κ₄ 来描述总完成时间与缩放因子λ、目标精度的关系，通过小规模实验拟合κ₁-κ₄，求导得到最优λ*。(3)FedFT阶段：提出CLCBA算法（贡献感知LoRA配置与带宽分配），以每轮性能增益R^t = ΣG_i^t/H^t为指标，贪心地为贡献大的设备增加LoRA深度；并用基于注水算法的WFBA子算法在带宽预算约束下平衡各设备完成时间。还给出了针对LoRA低秩矩阵A和B的收敛性分析。

Result: 在真实异构测试平台（RTX 4090服务器 + 10台Jetson设备，含AGX Xavier/Orin NX/Orin/Thor）上，以ViT-B/16和ViT-L/16在CIFAR-10、Caltech-101、Food-101数据集上进行IID（Dirichlet α=0.3的non-IID）实验，对比GPT-FL、FGL、SFLF、CAFF四个基线：(1)训练时间：FeDiSyn在Food-101 IID下达到80%精度仅需17584s，比CAFF、SFLF、FGL、GPT-FL分别快44.9%、58.0%、60.7%、61.3%；非IID下优势更明显，比CAFF快62.0%；(2)通信开销：Food-101 non-IID下仅7.4MB，比CAFF(262.1MB)减少97.2%；(3)最优λ*验证：λ*附近λ值时总时间最短，验证了扩展定律的有效性；(4)消融实验：各模块（FG、DM、CLC、WFBA）均贡献显著，模型规模越大预训练模块相对重要性越高。

Conclusion: FeDiSyn是首个系统性地将合成图像预训练与联邦微调统一考虑的工作，通过扩展定律实现了生成/预训练成本与FedFT收敛加速的最优权衡；特征引导的扩散模型生成方法同时缓解了标签和特征分布偏斜；贡献感知的LoRA配置和注水带宽分配有效应对了系统异构性并保留弱设备的关键信息。实验证明该框架在精度相当的前提下大幅缩短训练时间并降低通信开销，为大视觉模型在隐私敏感的边缘分布式场景中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning (FedFT) enables adapting pre-trained large vision models (LVMs) on distributed, privacy-sensitive devices, while its practical deployment is hindered by three critical challenges: resource constraints, system heterogeneity, and non-IID data. While prior studies partially address these issues, e.g., by pre-training initial models on synthetic images to mitigate the adverse effects of non-IID data, or leveraging parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to reduce resource consumption, they remain inadequate and fragmented. Specifically, existing synthetic image generation methods fail to capture device-specific feature distributions, while current PEFT-based FedFT methods often undervalue weaker devices that may provide critical information. More importantly, the separate optimization of pre-training and FedFT neglects their inherent connection, lacking a holistic perspective to maximize training efficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose FeDiSyn, a unified framework that holistically considers the interplay between pre-training and FedFT to minimize the overall LVM training time. Specifically, FeDiSyn introduces: (i) a scaling law for FedFT pre-training to determine the optimal number of synthetic images, balancing pre-training benefit against generation/pre-training cost, (ii) diffusion-based synthetic image generation that captures device-specific feature distributions for pre-training to tackle non-IID data, and (iii) a contribution-aware LoRA configuration and bandwidth allocation algorithm for FedFT to ensure that informative devices are effectively utilized while addressing system heterogeneity. Experimental results on the real-world testbed demonstrate that FeDiSyn reduces completion time by over 52.5% and communication cost by over 97.2%, while achieving comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [55] [HeteroMosaic: Exposing and Exploiting Heterogeneous Execution Opportunities for Energy-Efficient Edge LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12839)
*Gregory Hyegang Jun,Wesley Pang,Eddie Richter,Mehdi Saeedi,Aporva Amarnath,Pallavi Ferrao,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HeteroMosaic是一个面向边缘端LLM推理的"异构优先"调度框架，针对CPU+iGPU+NPU的统一内存SoC，通过异构roofline建模、因果保留的微批处理暴露跨加速器并行机会，并结合trace-guided关键区间协同优化，在AMD Ryzen AI平台上实现了最高2.35×的加速比和最高45.3%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前边缘SoC已普遍集成CPU、iGPU和NPU，但现有LLM推理框架通常只做粗粒度的设备级决策，或仅在操作算子层面做局部优化，忽略了任务图层面的跨加速器协同调度。这一不足在统一内存平台上尤为突出，因为性能不仅取决于操作放在哪个设备上，还取决于任务图中的执行协调。论文质疑：异构性本身是否能成为提升端到端LLM推理性能的关键策略，而非仅仅作为模型重构、稀疏化或量化等优化手段的副产品？

Method: HeteroMosaic包含三个核心组件：(1) 异构Roofline分析模型——扩展经典roofline模型，引入效率因子η和α以表征DVFS、内存争用和NPU运行时开销等实际系统效应，预测iGPU与NPU组合的理论性能上界；(2) 因果微批处理（causal micro-batching）——将prefill阶段分解为保留依赖关系的微批，使后续微批可通过KV缓存对前面已完成的微批进行注意力计算，从而暴露原本不可见的跨阶段并行机会；(3) 运行时感知的延迟整形（latency shaping）与Trace引导的关键区间协同优化——将异构分配视为控制节点完成时间的工具，对关键路径上的节点采用激进的张量并行切分，对非关键节点采用单设备执行或主动插入气泡（bubble），并通过离线trace-guided搜索（约8小时）针对每种模型/设备配置找到最优调度方案。此外还实现了非对称微批处理策略以应对长上下文场景中注意力复杂度二次增长的问题。

Result: 在三个AMD Ryzen AI平台（NPU-heavy AI 7 350、balanced AI 9 HX 370、iGPU-heavy AI Max+ 395）上，HeteroMosaic在balanced平台上对iGPU基线获得最高1.73×加速、对NPU基线1.78×加速、对llama.cpp最高2.05×加速，整体相比先前异构边缘AI方案提升最高2.35×，平均1.25×。能耗最高降低45.3%（即tokens per watt显著提升）。Prefill阶段在NPU-heavy平台平均2.30×加速，balanced平台1.50×，iGPU-heavy平台1.17×，趋势与roofline预测一致；decode阶段因受内存带宽限制，异构增益较为有限。消融实验表明：张量并行、关键区间调度、非对称微批、DVFS感知调度和NPU调度各组件协同贡献性能。

Conclusion: HeteroMosaic将边缘LLM推理重新定义为调度问题而非单纯的算子放置问题，证明在TDP受限的统一内存SoC上，通过细粒度的异构调度可以在不增加峰值功耗的前提下完成更多工作，从而同时提升速度和能效。该框架的方法论虽以AMD Ryzen AI为具体实现平台，但其核心调度原理——异构roofline分析、因果微批暴露并行、关键区间协同优化——可推广至其他统一内存异构系统以及视觉-语言-动作模型、扩散模型等基于Transformer的工作负载。未来可扩展至数据中心级异构系统，但需将通信开销作为一等调度约束纳入考量。

Abstract: Modern edge system-on-chips (SoCs) combine CPUs, integrated GPUs (iGPUs), and neural processing units (NPUs), yet existing LLM runtimes typically make coarse device-level decisions or optimize operators in isolation. As a result, they underutilize heterogeneous resources, particularly on unified-memory platforms where performance depends on both device placement and task-graph coordination. We present HeteroMosaic, a heterogeneity-first scheduling framework for edge LLM inference. HeteroMosaic first uses a heterogeneous roofline model to identify when combining iGPU and NPU execution is beneficial. It then decomposes inference into dependency-preserving micro-batches that expose cross-accelerator overlap and applies trace-guided co-optimization of scheduling and device allocation under practical effects such as memory contention, DVFS, device variation, and NPU runtime overheads. We implement HeteroMosaic in PyTorch C++ and evaluate it on three AMD Ryzen AI platforms spanning NPU-heavy, balanced, and iGPU-heavy designs. On the balanced platform, HeteroMosaic achieves up to 1.73X speedup over an iGPU baseline, 1.78X over an NPU baseline, and 2.05X over frameworks such as \texttt{ this http URL }, while reducing energy by up to 45.3%. It also improves performance over prior heterogeneous edge AI solutions by up to 2.35X.

</details>

*Chong Li,Corinne Ancourt,Gaétan Hains*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 这是 HLPP 2026（第19届国际高层级并行编程与应用研讨会）的会议论文集前言，介绍了该会议在巴黎举行的概况，收录了10篇经过同行评审的论文，并扩展了主题范围以涵盖人工智能相关的并行编程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍 HLPP 2026 研讨会的会议背景、论文收录情况以及主题范围，特别强调2026年版将人工智能纳入高层级并行编程的研究范畴，回应了AI与并行计算交叉融合的学术趋势。

Method: 采用单盲评审机制（single-blind review），每篇投稿由3位程序委员会成员进行评审；论文按照研讨会会议日程的顺序编排。

Result: 论文集共收录10篇经过同行评审的论文，主题涵盖多核、GPU及分布式平台上的编程模型、语言、库、算法骨架、编译器及运行时系统，并新增了AI系统的并行编程与性能、以及AI辅助并行代码生成等方向，还设有专门的并行性与AI开场会议。

Conclusion: HLPP 2026 在延续传统高层级并行编程研究主题的同时，扩展至AI领域，反映了该学科与人工智能技术日益交叉融合的发展趋势，为相关研究者提供了学术交流平台。

Abstract: This volume contains the ten peer-reviewed papers presented at HLPP 2026, the 19th International Symposium on High-Level Parallel Programming and Applications, held on 9-10 July 2026 at the Institut Henri Poincare in Paris, France. The symposium covers high-level approaches to parallel programming: programming models, languages, libraries, algorithmic skeletons, compilers, and runtime systems for multi-core, GPU, and distributed platforms. The 2026 edition extended this scope towards artificial intelligence, with new topics on the parallel programming and performance of AI systems and on AI-assisted generation of parallel code, and opened with a dedicated session on parallelism and AI. Papers were selected through a single-blind review process, with three Program Committee reviews per submission, and appear in the order of the symposium program. Edited by Chong Li, Corinne Ancourt, and Gaetan Hains.

</details>

### [57] [AutoTrace: From Patches to Triggers via Agentic Interprocedural Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12058)
*Arastoo Zibaeirad,Marco Vieira,Thomas Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoTrace 是一个用于漏洞触发点定位(trigger localization)的智能体流程,通过代码属性图(CPG)与大语言模型智能体的协同,在函数间(interprocedural)级别精确识别漏洞触发的具体语句,并构建了 SinkTrace-Bench 这一新型基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞分析方法存在明显不足:基于规则的静态工具无法覆盖项目特定的封装函数;单函数行排序器无法跨越调用边界追踪污点;VulTrigger 等先前系统通过句法规则匹配混淆了共现与因果关系。给定一个修复性提交(VFC),真正需要回答的是:哪个具体语句将漏洞程序状态转化为实际的不安全操作?这一触发语句往往距离补丁函数数个调用层,需要跨过程因果推理,而这是传统方法难以胜任的。

Method: AutoTrace 采用五阶段流程:(1)关键变量提取——智能体从 diff 中识别补丁相关的关键变量及其 CWE 类别;(2)补丁作用域 CPG 与局部切片——按需构建代码属性图,在每个函数内独立切片并跨调用边界拼接证据;(3)智能体跨过程探索——采用最佳优先搜索,由 LLM 智能体决定探索方向,确定性门控决定是否接受触发点,优先级评分综合语义相关性与深度惩罚;(4)多层验证——L1/L4 硬门控(数据流与上下文一致性)必须通过,L1b 控制依赖回退用于循环边界触发,L2/L3 软证据(守卫检查、补丁差异)用于校准置信度,验证智能体(Agent 3)仅在门控通过者中行使最终判定权(资源泄漏类除外);(5)构建 SinkTrace-Bench——为每个已验证触发点生成匹配的 vulnerable/safe 样本对,暴露从源到汇的完整因果链。威胁模型将 LLM 后端视为不可信推理者,所有接受决策由 CPG 门控保障。

Result: 在 InterPVD 完整基准(744 个 CVE)上,AutoTrace 达到 75.0% VulnHit 和 80.8% FuncHit,超越 VulTrigger 的 69.8% VulnHit(同一行级包含指标)。在 593 个共同覆盖的 CVE 上,精确触发点定位率近乎为 LLM+RAG 基线的两倍(71.0% vs 37.8%)。所有深度学习检测器在跨过程样本上均出现退化(LineVul 下降 11.2 个百分点最显著)。消融实验表明反思重试贡献最大(-32 pp),其次情景记忆(-16)、关键变量细化(-12)、长期记忆(-8);确定性探索与智能体探索的对比显示,后者在深度≥2 时将 VulnHit 从 4.7% 提升至 18.6%。SinkTrace-Bench 包含 1,542 个样本(771 个匹配对),涵盖 16 个 CWE 类,七种前沿大模型零样本评估中无一显著超越随机水平(最高 59.0%,Gemini-3-Flash),最强模型在安全样本上崩溃(TNR 仅 7–31%),表明存在根本性的因果推理鸿沟。平均成本为每个 CVE 0.65 美元,23.2 次 LLM 调用,中位数耗时 42 分钟(P90 约 13.8 小时)。

Conclusion: AutoTrace 证明了跨过程触发点定位可通过智能体引导的 CPG 探索实现,其确定性门控机制确保了所有报告均有显式图证据支撑,克服了先前方法将句法共现误判为因果连接的局限。其 5.8 个百分点的 FuncHit/VulnHit 差距反映的是保守弃权而非自信错误,主要限制来自 CPG 完整性而非验证器严格性。SinkTrace-Bench 揭示了当前大模型在区分匹配漏洞/安全对上的因果推理鸿沟。下一步工作是在调用路径上预先构建 CPG,并在模型截止日期之后的实时仓库快照上运行 AutoTrace,以实现零日触发点发现——此时无任何公开记录可被模型记忆,且 CPG 门控保证候选均有证据支撑。

Abstract: Given a vulnerability-fixing commit, trigger localization asks which specific statement turns the vulnerable program state into a concrete unsafe operation. This question is harder than binary vulnerability detection because the answer demands interprocedural, causal reasoning: in a substantial fraction of real-world CVEs the triggering statement lies several call layers outside the patched function, beyond the reach of static rule sets and pattern-matching language models alike. We present AutoTrace, an agentic pipeline that localizes vulnerability triggers by exploring a code property graph layer by layer, with LLM agents deciding where to look next and deterministic admissibility gates deciding what evidence is required before a trigger can be reported. Agents never accept a trigger on their own authority; every reported trigger is backed by explicit evidence drawn from the graph, so the pipeline covers both intra- and interprocedural vulnerabilities without relying on ungrounded model judgment. On the full InterPVD benchmark, AutoTrace reaches 75.0% VulnHit and 80.8% FuncHit, surpassing the prior state of the art on the same corpus. Building on the same machinery, we construct SinkTrace-Bench, a dataset that exposes each vulnerability as a source-to-sink (S2S) causal chain from attacker-controlled input through propagation to the dangerous operation, drawn from matched vulnerable and patched program states. It comprises 1,542 verifier-confirmed, perfectly balanced vulnerable/safe samples whose label fidelity we audit against expert annotations. Benchmarking frontier LLMs on it, we find that even the strongest struggle to separate the matched pairs, exposing the causal-reasoning gap that trigger localization targets. Artifact available at this https URL .

</details>


### [58] [Code-MUE: Measuring Code LLMs' Uncertainty through Execution-based Semantic Interaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12273)
*Xiaoning Ren,Yinxing Xue,Lei Ma,Yuheng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Code-MUE 是一个纯黑盒的不确定性量化框架，通过对代码大语言模型采样多个候选程序，在混合测试输入上执行它们以获取运行时行为，进而构建"语义交互图"，并计算其 Von Neumann 熵作为不确定性度量。该方法在 8 个 SOTA LLM 和 4 个代码基准测试中实现了与功能正确性高达 -0.98 的 Spearman 负相关，显著优于基于词汇和嵌入的基线方法，并支持选择性预测和无监督重排序等实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码 LLM 因其固有的随机性在关键软件工程场景中存在重大风险。现有不确定性估计方法存在三大局限：(1) 白盒/灰盒方法依赖 logits 或内部状态，无法应用于闭源商业模型（GPT-4、Claude、Gemini 等）；(2) Token 级预测概率仅反映局部语法不确定性，与全局逻辑正确性存在"粒度失配"；(3) NLP 中的语义熵方法对代码失效，因为代码具有"语法-语义鸿沟"——单一 token 改变即可颠覆功能，但表面相似度或嵌入距离难以捕捉此类离散性差异。核心动机是：代码具有可执行性这一独有属性，可作为量化语义不确定性的可靠锚点。

Method: Code-MUE 采用三阶段管道：(1) **概率采样与混合输入合成**：通过温度 τ=1.0 的随机解码采样 N=100 个候选程序；构建 K=20 的探针集（K/2 基于静态类型分析的边界用例 + K/2 由 LLM 生成的语义感知标称输入，无需期望输出）；(2) **语义交互图构建**：在沙盒中执行所有程序，将结果归类为返回值、异常类型或超时，形成执行签名向量；构建 N×N 相似度矩阵 W，元素为两程序在探针上行为一致的比例（语法错误隔离、超时保守处理为零相似度）；(3) **图基不确定性量化**：将相似度矩阵迹归一化为密度矩阵 ρ=W/Tr(W)，对其进行特征分解，把谱 Λ 解释为潜在语义模式的概率分布，计算 Von Neumann 熵 H_spec=-Σλ_k ln(λ_k)，并用 ln(N) 归一化至 [0,1] 区间。该方法将"两个程序是否看起来相似"转变为"两个程序是否行为一致"。

Result: 在 8 个 LLM（GPT-4.1-mini/nano、Claude-3-Haiku、Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite、Llama 3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B、Qwen2.5-7B）和 4 个基准（HumanEval、MBPP、QuixBugs、CodeNet）上的大规模评估表明：(1) **正确性对齐**：Spearman 相关性达 -0.987（GPT-4.1-mini on HumanEval）和 -0.985（Gemini on HumanEval），AUROC 在 32 个场景中 26 个最优；(2) **选择性预测**：风险-覆盖率曲线显著优于基线，AURC 最低；(3) **无监督重排序**：基于特征向量中心性选择最优解，准确率超 80%，GPT-4.1-mini 与 Gemini 超 90%；(4) **样本敏感性**：N 从 100 降至 25 性能损失极小（ρ 从 -0.831 降至 -0.804），K=4 时仍提供有效信号（ρ=-0.682）；(5) **计算开销**：动态执行阶段占比较低（N×K 次 CPU 计算），候选生成阶段主导成本。案例研究揭示两类失败模式：高熵=软失败（模型振荡），低熵但低正确率=硬失败（系统性偏差，如频率偏置导致的过度自信错误）。

Conclusion: Code-MUE 通过将不确定性量化从模糊的文本/嵌入空间转移到可观测的运行时执行空间，弥合了代码的语法-语义鸿沟。其基于语义交互图的 Von Neumann 熵不仅是一个可靠的正确性代理指标，还具备双重诊断能力：高不确定性指示模型处于"无知"状态，低不确定性但功能错误指示模型处于"系统性偏差"状态。该框架在黑盒设置下实现了无需 logits、无需测试预言的实用不确定性估计，为代码 LLM 的安全部署、选择性预测和响应选择提供了可操作的工程工具，未来工作可探索更大规模代码场景、与其他不确定性维度（如认知 vs 偶然）的结合以及更强的输入生成策略（如模糊测试）。

Abstract: As Code Large Language Models (LLMs) become central to modern software engineering, their inherent stochasticity poses significant real-world risks, where even minor errors can lead to severe functional, security, or safety consequences. Reliable automation, therefore, demands the ability to distinguish between confident, well-supported predictions and stochastic guessing. However, existing uncertainty estimation methods face a critical gap: white and grey-box techniques are often inapplicable to closed-source models, while standard "black-box" text metrics fail to capture the unique fragility of code, where syntactic variation does not always imply semantic divergence. To bridge this syntax-semantics gap, we introduce Code-MUE, a purely black-box framework that measures uncertainty through execution-based Semantic Interaction Graphs. Unlike prior approaches that rely on superficial textual similarity, Code-MUE grounds uncertainty in observable runtime behavior, calculating the Von Neumann entropy of the solution space to quantify global semantic diversity. A large-scale empirical study across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrates that Code-MUE achieves a strong negative correlation with functional correctness (Spearman's correlation up to -0.98), significantly outperforming lexical and embedding-based baselines while enabling robust risk detection and selective prediction in practical workflows.

</details>


### [59] [Agent-Safety Evaluations as Load-Bearing Evidence: A Vendor-Neutral, Cross-Harness Reconstructability Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12469)
*Oleg Solozobov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向智能体安全评估的可重构性（reconstructability）度量方法，包含八类决策属性的属性级评分、证据充分性卡（Evidence Sufficiency Cards）、反事实重放干预协议以及声明-证据过度声明差距定义，旨在解决安全评估结果缺乏可重建证据支撑的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体安全评估结果并非"承重证据"：相同的名义结果（任务成功、攻击成功、监控分数）可能建立在实质不同的证据基础之上。缺乏厂商中立、可运行的工具来衡量证据的可重构性，即捕获的证据能否重建某项声明所依赖的决策，制约了安全评估的有效性。

Method: 1. 提出覆盖八类决策属性的属性级可重构性度量；2. 设计跨评估框架的适配器，为每个决策生成证据充分性卡（Evidence Sufficiency Cards）；3. 定义每次运行的监控覆盖发布检查；4. 规定反事实重放干预协议及其可重放性前置条件探针；5. 定义声明-证据过度声明差距（claim-evidence overclaim gap）。

Result: 在公开和捆绑的追踪数据上（无需新模型运行），四个共享表面读数的输入的十二字段充分性跨度为0.458–0.833；所有评分追踪的可重放前置条件均未满足。在合成发布门控对中，充分性门控阻止了原始变体（0.542）并放行了有仪器记录的变体（0.667）。

Conclusion: 安全评估声明应附带其可重构性向量；论文提供了可复现的软件包以重新生成所有报告数值，呼吁将证据可重构性作为评估有效性的标准度量纳入安全评估实践。

Abstract: Many agent-safety evaluation results are not yet load-bearing evidence: identical nominal outcomes (task success, attack success, monitor scores) may sit atop materially different evidence regimes. No vendor-neutral, runnable instrument scores reconstructability as an evaluation-validity metric: whether captured evidence can reconstruct the decision a claim depends on. This paper introduces a property-level reconstructability metric over eight decision-property classes and a cross-harness adapter emitting per-decision Evidence Sufficiency Cards backing a per-run monitor-coverage release check. It specifies a counterfactual-replay intervention protocol, implements its replayability-precondition probe, and defines a claim-evidence overclaim gap. On public and bundled traces, without new model runs, twelve-field sufficiency spans 0.458-0.833 across four inputs sharing a surface reading; replay preconditions are unmet in every scored trace. In a synthetic release-gate pair, the sufficiency gate blocks the raw variant (0.542) and passes the instrumented (0.667). Safety-evaluation claims should travel with their reconstructability vector; a reproducibility package regenerates every reported number.

</details>


### [60] [Multi-Perspective Agentic Program Repair via Code Property Graphs and Temporal Execution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12605)
*Zhili Huang,Ling Xu,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CT-Repair提出了一种基于代理（agentic）的自动化程序修复（APR）框架，通过将静态与动态证据结构化为可查询的代码属性图（CPG）和时序执行图（TEG），结合三阶段过滤管道和多视角（静态/动态/混合）有限状态机引导的代理分析，生成基于证据的修复策略，从而提高修复效果并减少对大量补丁采样的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动化程序修复方法存在两大局限：（1）原始执行轨迹过大且冗余，难以作为有效的模型上下文；（2）重复采样补丁往往产生不同实现但缺乏不同的根因假设或修复策略，导致修复效率受限。

Method: 1. 将静态证据表示为可查询的代码属性图（CPG），将动态证据表示为时序执行图（TEG）；2. 采用三阶段过滤管道（执行过滤、行为过滤等）构建紧凑的TEG；3. 三个由有限状态机（FSM）引导的代理分别从静态、动态和混合视角分析bug，独立产出基于证据的修复策略；4. 通过策略引导的生成过程，将策略实例化为候选补丁，并利用验证反馈优化最有前景的策略。

Result: 在Defects4J v3.0的854个Java bug上评估：混合模型配置下正确修复489个bug；在受控的GPT-5.4-mini配置下修复388个bug，比ReinFix多19个、比RepairAgent多30个；三种证据视角的并集比最强单一视角多修复99个bug；执行过滤平均将候选方法范围缩小94.85%，行为过滤进一步减少55.97%的运行时记录。

Conclusion: 结构化的运行时证据（TEG）和多视角推理（静态/动态/混合）能够有效提升自动化程序修复的效果，无需单纯依赖更大的补丁生成预算，验证了证据结构化与策略引导生成在APR任务中的价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have improved automated program repair (APR), but two limitations remain. First, raw execution traces are often too large and repetitive to serve as effective model context. Second, repeated patch sampling may produce different implementations without yielding distinct root-cause hypotheses or repair strategies. We present CT-Repair, an agentic APR framework representing static and dynamic evidence as queryable Code Property Graph (CPG) and Temporal Execution Graph (TEG). CT-Repair applies a three-stage filtering pipeline to construct compact TEGs. Three finite-state-machine-guided agents analyze each bug from static, dynamic, and hybrid perspectives and independently produce evidence-grounded repair strategies. A strategy-guided generation procedure instantiates these strategies as candidate patches and uses validation feedback to refine the most promising strategy. We evaluate CT-Repair on 854 Java bugs from Defects4J v3.0. In the mixed-model configuration, CT-Repair correctly repairs 489 bugs. Under a controlled GPT-5.4-mini configuration, it repairs 388 bugs, 19 and 30 more than ReinFix and RepairAgent, respectively. The union of the three evidence perspectives repairs 99 more bugs than the strongest individual perspective. The filtering pipeline also compacts runtime evidence, with execution filtering narrowing the candidate method scope by 94.85% on average and behavior filtering further reducing retained runtime records by 55.97%. These results show that structured runtime evidence and multi-perspective reasoning can improve repair effectiveness without relying solely on a larger patch-generation budget.

</details>


### [61] [Line-Anchored Feedback Cuts Token Costs and Improves Correctness in AI Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12713)
*William Franz Lamberti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过配对实验证明，将代码审查反馈从整体式提示（holistic prompt）改为行锚定（line-anchored）格式（FileMark 导出的结构化格式），可显著降低前沿模型的 token 生成成本（Claude Opus -22.5%、Claude Sonnet -57.5%，百行以上文件可达 24%–80%），并提升弱本地模型的正确率（5 个本地模型中 3 个提升 5–7 个百分点）。在探索性的 function-patch 实验中，当工具端自动应用编辑时，本地模型在大文件上的正确率几乎翻三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 生成式 AI 代码编辑中，生成 token 直接驱动推理成本、延迟与能耗，但反馈格式对成本的影响尚未被系统研究。
2. 现代代码审查天然是行级、定位化的，而 GAI 助手通常接收的是单一整体式提示——这一形式差距未被实验隔离过。
3. Green AI 倡导将效率作为一等评估指标，AI 研究中复现性不足也是长期痛点。
4. 因此需要一项内容匹配、控制单一变量的对照实验，量化"行锚定"作为反馈格式杠杆的价值。

Method: 1. 配对、内容匹配设计：从同一 changes.json 派生出控制组（整体式纯文本提示）与处理组（FileMark .feedback.md 行锚定导出），仅交付格式不同；后置审计确认 6,552 次运行提示的变更条目完全一致。
2. 任务库：参数化模板生成的 36 个 R 脚本任务，分入 6 个文件规模等级（T1<10 行至 T6≥1000 行），每任务注入 2–5 个缺陷。
3. 模型：5 个本地模型（Ollama 量化运行的 llama3.1、qwen2.5-coder:14b、gemma4:26b、gemma3:27b、qwen3-coder:30b）+ 2 个前沿模型（Claude Opus 4-8、Claude Sonnet 5）。
4. 输出契约：小等级（T1–T3）要求返回完整脚本；大等级（T4–T6）要求以 SEARCH/REPLACE 块自主编辑；探索性附录 B 采用 function-patch 契约（工具端按函数名替换）。
5. 双主结局：token 效率（输出 token 数）与正确性（沙箱执行 + 验证器评分，含 partial-credit 与二元正确性）。
6. 统计设计：13 次重复/任务，共 6,552 次主实验运行；按功效分析取每格 92 对；配对 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验与 McNemar 检验为主，Holm–Bonferroni 校正多重比较；ANOVA 检验 arm × tier 交互。

Result: 1. Token 效率：4/7 模型经 Holm 校正后显著减少 token——Claude Sonnet -57.5%（1282→545，p≈5.6×10⁻⁵⁸）、Claude Opus -22.5%、qwen3-coder:30b -18.3%、gemma3:27b -11.7%；两个模型反而显著增加（qwen2.5-coder:14b +30.9%、llama3.1 +6.0%）。百行以上文件中 Sonnet 可省 74.7%–80.3%、Opus 可省 24.3%–38.4%。
2. 正确性：合并提升 +2.0 个百分点（0.630→0.650，McNemar p=8.7×10⁻⁶）；qwen3-coder:30b +7.3、qwen2.5-coder:14b +6.0、llama3.1 +5.1 三个本地模型显著提升；Claude Opus 双臂满分；Claude Sonnet 因过度精简牺牲 4.5 个百分点（0.994→0.949）。
3. Arm × Tier 交互：T4（100–500 行）显著正效应（partial-credit +0.048，Holm 校正 p=3.1×10⁻⁵），T6 微弱负效应（-0.012，主因 Sonnet）；T1–T3 双臂近天花板。
4. 契约合规：Claude 完美合规；两个 coder 模型仅 T4 合规；Gemma 与 llama3.1 在大等级几乎无法产生可用 edit block——这是模型能力瓶颈而非锚定效应。
5. 失败模式转变：FileMark 将 coder 模型的"runs_fail"（静默错误）比率从 0.073/0.043 降至 0/0.002，把静默错误转化为可被察觉的崩错或成功。
6. 探索性 function-patch 实验：本地模型在大文件正确率从 0.097 提升至 0.279（约三倍），T4 分数效应扩大至 +0.191；Sonnet 正确性代价消失。

Conclusion: 1. 行锚定反馈是一根同时作用于成本与质量的杠杆：前沿模型省 token（22%–58%，百行以上 24%–80%），本地模型提正确率（+5 至 +7 分），效应落在各自有提升空间的维度上。
2. 行级反馈量化了"人在环路中"的价值：人工定位的判断以低成本记录为行内锚点，可同时改善正确率与运行成本。
3. T4（100–500 行）是 sweet spot：缺陷定位难但编辑仍在能力范围内；告知模型"在哪里改"优于让模型搜索。
4. 模型异质性关键在于"格式能力"：能否产生有效 SEARCH/REPLACE 块决定了 agentic 编辑路径下的收益能否兑现；用工具端补丁契约绕过此瓶颈后，本地模型大文件正确率可三倍提升。
5. 实践建议：本地模型将 FileMark 与自动应用编辑的工具搭配是本研究识别的最高价值配置；大规模生产中行锚定是有效的成本控制手段。
6. 局限与未来工作：任务为合成 R 代码、单轮交互；本地模型受限于 24GB GPU 量化；未来扩展到多轮、真实仓库、Python/C/Julia 以及 FileMark 日常实践的用户研究。

Abstract: Generated tokens are a direct driver of the cost, latency, and energy of generative AI (GAI) code editing. We show the format of feedback is a lever on all three. We compare two deliveries of the same requested changes: a holistic prompt (control) versus the structured, line-anchored export of FileMark (treatment). FileMark is a VSCodium extension for inline comments on any file. In a paired experiment line anchoring cut generated tokens by 22% (Claude Opus) and 58% (Claude Sonnet), reaching 24%-80% on files of 100 lines or more, with four of seven models generating significantly fewer tokens after multiple-testing correction. Correctness rose where models had headroom: +2.0 points pooled and +5 to +7 points for three of five local models. An exploratory experiment in which the harness, not the GAI model, applies function-level patches shows the correctness benefit grows further when the edit-application burden is lifted: local-model correctness on 100+ line files roughly triples under anchoring. Line-anchored feedback reduces what stronger models spend and improves what weaker models get right.

</details>


### [62] [Form, Not Content? A Preregistered, Placebo-Controlled Evaluation of Learned Error-Conditioned Self-Repair Through Prompts and Weights in Frozen Small Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12962)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PoPE（Popperian安慰剂对照评估）方法学，测试冻结小型代码LLM（0.5-1.5B参数）能否操作性地使用反驳其自身输出的执行证据。在40单元1.5B边缘抗性测试集上，通过提示通道（ELF）和权重通道（ELW，QLoRA适配器）进行双盲安慰剂对照实验，结果显示错误内容在两个通道中均未超越形式匹配的安慰剂，提示通道Sh=12 > On=10（mechanism-null），权重通道Ct=Fr=8，SHA-错乱安慰剂Sf=10（train-null）。研究将已编译的批评信息丧失外部认知角色的现象诠释为"批评已转化为条件化"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自修复文献评估LLM能否利用执行反馈纠正失败代码时缺乏安慰剂控制，导致无法区分增益来源于：(1) 学习的错误内容 vs. 重试的形式结构 vs. 生成器规模。具体而言存在四个未解决的混淆问题：形式混淆（机制与呈现形式捆绑）、预算与顺序统计量混淆（适应性分配与额外采样混淆）、通道混淆（提示与权重通道未联合测量）、规模混淆（单尺度结果被泛化为"关于方法"的结论）。在冻结0.5-1.5B代码模型、零通过池层、几百样本数据预算的特定部署区间内，此前研究未进行系统表征。

Method: PoPE采用双门决策规则：(1) 信号门Gs：内容臂必须优于干预自由基线Fr或盲采样；(2) 内容门Gc：内容臂必须优于通道特异的形式匹配安慰剂。提示通道（ELF）使用四臂设计：On（实时学习的错误晶格控制器）、Fr（基线）、Sh（内容消融安慰剂，保留脚手架但移除错误内容）、De（供体错误事实，破坏任务-错误映射）。权重通道（ELW）使用三臂设计：Ct（QLoRA适配器，在1964个真实任务-错误原子对上训练）、Sf（SHA错乱安慰剂LoRA，相同训练配置但任务-内容映射被错乱）、Fr（无适配器基线）。所有比较在匹配输出生成预算R=4下进行，使用精确单侧McNemar检验，Holm校正控制在通道家族内。测试单元为40个1.5B边缘抗性单元的Up公开层筛选终点，隐藏层确认按设计推迟。在程序层面，引入AEG-BANDIT自适应分配器、JEPA错误集架构（ERA）等机制阶梯组件，并执行自审计链（匹配绘制预算B=8重放）以撤回自身表面阳性结果。

Result: 提示通道：On-Sh对比净效应-2（p=1.0），Sh=12 > On=10 > De=8 > Fr=7，机制零（mechanism-null）。权重通道：Ct-Fr对比净效应0（b01=b10=4，p=1.0），Ct=Fr=8 < Sf=10，训练零（train-null）。两个适配器训练损失均降至约0.002（99.67-99.73%收敛），但无内容可归因的迁移优越性。描述性规模观察：任意组合公开层解锁率从0.5B的27.5%升至1.5B的60%。AEG-BANDIT初始+6.0pp结果（B=12，p=0.01375）经匹配绘制预算B=8自审计撤回，得出replay-null结论（适应性-最佳固定+0.02209，p=0.2022；适应性-随机-0.00227，p=0.6176）。程序唯一确认的阳性为LG P0单元×组合交互效应（T_int=0.016667，置换p=0.0001，n=80）。ERA有效性测试按设计推迟，JEPA头不崩塌但无下游信号（岭R²≈0.0096，解码器R²≈-133）。

Conclusion: PoPE本身是可重测的反思性测量标准而非修复算法，研究不主张等价性或非劣效（均未单独检验）。四项规律性：(1) 双通道归因失败边界（"形式墙"）；(2) 1.5B方向的两点规模方向（不可外推为规模律）；(3) 匹配预算下适应性-随机差异约零，提示最佳B采样的顺序统计量饱和；(4) 小数据权重边界——训练损失下降未带来内容可归因的迁移。核心诠释为：在波普尔意义上，批评在反驳时刻（执行）起作用，编译进生成状态时则转化为先验信念。已编译的批评未消失为信息，而是丧失了在独立测试新猜想中的外部认知角色——"测试已被条件化所取代"。四项后续研究建议：任务多样化语料、7B生成器评估、ERA有效性补做、分配器重新定位为首次抽取前先验。

Abstract: Frozen small code LLMs are deployed locally, yet the information guiding a retry after a failed attempt is still measured without placebo controls in the self-repair literature. We treat a failed program as a conjecture and an execution counterexample as an oracle-relative refutation, and introduce PoPE (Popperian Placebo-controlled Evaluation): a methodology for measuring whether evidence that falsifies LLM-generated code can be used operationally by that same model. In PoPE, error content is paired with channel-specific placebos that keep the predeclared scaffold while ablating task-relevant content or deranging the task-error assignment. Frozen small code models (0.5-1.5B) are evaluated under preregistered rules through a prompt channel and a weight channel (small-data adapter training), with four generations per arm-unit pair. In the prompt channel, public-tier screening unlocked 12 units under the content-ablated form placebo versus 10 under the live error-pattern arm on a 40-unit resistant band; the result was recorded as mechanism-null. In the weight channel, an 8-8 tie was observed between the error-content adapter and the intervention-free baseline (p=1.0), while the SHA-deranged placebo adapter stayed ahead with 10 unlocks; content-attributable superiority was not confirmed. These results do not constitute evidence of equivalence or non-inferiority. Equivalence was not tested separately. Findings are restricted to the public-tier screening endpoint; hidden-tier confirmation was deferred by design. We read this not as compiled criticism disappearing as information, but as the loss of its external role in testing a new conjecture: when a representation learned from the oracle is written back into the generation state, testing is replaced by conditioning. No working JEPA-RL controller is claimed. PoPE is presented as a placebo-controlled, retestable measurement standard.

</details>


### [63] [Predicting Acceptance and Review Effort in Human and Agent Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12057)
*Kartik Ghanshyambhai Pansuriya,Ehsan Ghorbani,Deepak Singh,Eman Abdullah AlOmar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究在 PR 提交时(无审查、CI 或合并信息可参考)能否预测其可接受性与审查工作量,基于 AIDev 数据集构建了仅使用提交期特征的防泄漏预测管道,涵盖 Logistic 回归、随机森林、梯度提升、Extra Trees 与 MLP 等经典模型,并在混合、仅人类、仅 Agent 与均衡贡献者四种视角下进行 5 折交叉验证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 编码 Agent 开始与人类开发者共用同一审查流水线提交 PR,仓库维护者面临新挑战:在 PR 刚开启时就识别哪些易于接受、哪些将耗费大量审查资源。已有 PR 预测工作基本默认人类贡献者,难以直接迁移到人—Agent 混合场景,亟需一种仅依赖提交期信息的早期分诊模型,以辅助维护者合理分配审查注意力。

Method: 1) 数据源采用 AIDev 语料库,聚合为 PR 级建模表;2) 特征工程限定在提交时刻可见信息,分为四类——PR 文本结构(标题/正文长度、URL、代码块等)、仓库与时间上下文(stars、forks、语言、星期/小时)、diff 结构(增删行、文件数、churn、文件类型比例)以及基于关键词的轻量任务意图标签;3) RQ1 定义为 merged/closed 二分类,RQ2 定义为审查评论数与合并时间的回归,严格排除后置审查信号作为输入以防泄漏;4) 模型涵盖 LR、RF、GBC、ET、MLP(含 majority 与启发式基线)以及 RFR、GBR 回归器;5) 在 Pooled/Human-only/Agent-only/Balanced 四种视图下做 5 折分层交叉验证,配合配对统计检验、SHAP 与特征族消融进行可解释性与误差分析。

Result: RQ1 方面:树模型(RF/GBC/ET/MLP)F1 达到 0.957~0.958,显著优于 Majority 与启发式基线,但 AUC 仅约 0.65~0.68,表明虽有分诊价值但不应作为完美排序器;PR 文本与元数据(正文/标题长度等)是最重要预测因子,在均衡贡献者视图下 LR 显著提升而 RF 表现稳健;Agent PR 因格式更规整而略具更强可分性。RQ2 方面:评论数回归 MAE=1.00、R²=0.20,合并时间回归 MAE=24 小时、R²=0.12,仅能解释有限方差,说明工作量同时受审查者可用性、CI 与项目流程等提交期不可见因素主导;diff/churn 特征对工作量更关键。

Conclusion: 提交期信号足以支撑 PR 接受的早期分诊,但不足以完整刻画审查工作量;模型应定位为决策辅助工具而非自动化审批者;Agent PR 虽引发更谨慎的审查动态,但在均衡分布下依然可被早期特征预测;未来工作需融合语义代码表示、审稿者—作者交互历史、CI 状态以及更广泛的项目工作流上下文,以提升工作量预测能力。

Abstract: Pull requests (PRs) are a central mechanism for reviewing and integrating code changes in modern software repositories. As AI coding agents begin to submit more code changes alongside human developers, maintainers face a new challenge: deciding which PRs are likely to be accepted and which ones may require substantial review effort. This paper studies whether such outcomes can be estimated at the time a PR is opened, before reviewer discussion, CI feedback, or merge decisions are available. Using the AIDev dataset, we construct a leakage-aware prediction pipeline for human- and agent-authored PRs. The feature set is limited to submission-time information, including PR text characteristics, metadata, repository context, temporal signals, and lightweight diff statistics. We evaluate classical machine-learning models, including Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosting, Extra Trees, and MLPs, across pooled, human-only, agent-only, and balanced contributor views. Our results show that acceptance prediction is feasible from early signals: tree-based models achieve F1 scores above 0.95, with textual clarity and metadata among the most influential predictors. Review-effort prediction is more difficult. Comment counts and time-to-merge are only modestly explained by submission-time features, suggesting that reviewer availability, project workflow, and team-specific review practices play a major role. These findings indicate that early PR models can support triage and reviewer prioritization, but should be used as advisory tools rather than automated decision-makers.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Test Presence: Assessing the Quality and Robustness of Agent-Generated Tests in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12068)
*Preet Jhanglani,Zeel Kaushal Desai,Vidhi Kansara,Eman Abdullah AlOmar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对AI编码代理自动生成测试用例的质量问题，对204,673个测试文件（含24,941个人工编写文件和179,732个AI生成文件，来自AIDev数据集）进行大规模实证比较。基于AST静态分析，从三个维度（断言强度、边界用例覆盖、脆弱性风险）评估测试质量，发现AI代理生成的测试在边界条件覆盖上优于人类，但存在更高的脆弱性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI生成测试的评估基准（如SWE-bench）主要关注通过率而非测试的内在质量，这可能导致"隐性技术债务"——测试套件虽然通过但缺乏全面的覆盖或语义价值。因此需要一种评估方法来判断AI代理生成的测试在断言强度、边界覆盖和稳定性方面的真实质量。

Method: 采用基于Python AST模块的"白盒"静态分析框架，设计三条独立的分析管道：1）管道A通过预定义的强/弱断言分类法评估断言强度；2）管道B通过启发式字面量匹配（None、0、-1、空字符串等）评估边界用例覆盖的多样性；3）管道C通过检测time.sleep、文件I/O、非确定性函数等反模式来评估脆弱性候选，并结合动态分析（每测试执行100次）确认脆弱性。

Result: 研究发现三个核心结论：1）断言强度方面，人类略优（人类强断言占比88.08% vs AI的85.37%），但AI生成的"未知"断言模式高达11.58%，远高于人类的1.46%；2）边界用例覆盖方面，AI显著优于人类（多样性得分0.62 vs 0.32，零值测试27.7% vs 11.2%，空值测试13.4% vs 8.3%）；3）脆弱性方面，AI生成的测试风险更高（候选率0.41 vs 0.30），主要源于对文件I/O和非确定性API的依赖。

Conclusion: 研究表明AI代理不应被视为人类测试员的替代品，而更适合作为需要人工监督的"测试生成器"。它们在扩展测试覆盖范围（广度）方面表现出色，但在测试稳定性（深度）方面存在不足。未来工作应聚焦于环境感知建模、混合断言生成（RAG + 规则）、自动化Mock生成等方向，以实现"生成-执行-精化"的工作流范式。

Abstract: The integration of AI-powered coding agents into Continuous Integration/Continuous Delivery (CI/CD) pipelines has fundamentally altered how software verification is conducted. While these agents successfully automate the test generation, current evaluation benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench) largely focus on pass-rates rather than the intrinsic quality of the generated tests. This raises the possibility of "stealth technical debt", in which test suites pass execution but do not offer comprehensive coverage or semantic value. We address this methodological gap through a large-scale, empirical comparison of 204,673 test artifacts which comprises of 24,941 human-authored files and 179,732 agent-generated files; sourced from the AIDev dataset. Using the Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) parsing with Python's naive ast module, we implemented a "white-box" static analysis framework to evaluate three quality dimensions: Assertion Strength (RQ1), Edge-Case Coverage (RQ2), and Flakiness Potential (RQ3). Our results present a nuanced inversion of traditional assumptions. AI agents performed better than humans in Edge-Case Coverage, with almost twice the variety of boundary checks (Variety Score: 0.62 vs 0.32) and a higher frequency of null-safety testing (13.40% vs. 8.3%), even though human developers had a slight advantage in Assertion Strength (88.1% strong assertions vs. 85.37% for agents). But this thoroughness comes at a price: due mostly to their reliance on file I/O and non-deterministic logic, agent-generated tests exhibited a higher risk of flakiness (Candidate Rate: 0.41 vs. 0.30). These findings suggest that while AI agents excel at rigorous boundary testing, they lack the "environmental awareness" needed to write stable, hermetic tests.

</details>


### [65] [Fault Injection in OpenAPI Specifications for Evaluating Black-Box Testing Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12101)
*Hamza Bin Mazhar,Yuqing Wang,Mika V. Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统地向OpenAPI规范中注入六类文献驱动的故障，并在两个微服务基准系统（TrainTicket和SocialNetwork）上使用三种主流黑盒测试工具（EvoMaster、RESTler、Schemathesis）进行评估，揭示了规范故障会以异质方式显著影响测试效果，并识别出四种独特的退化模式，证明了仅依赖覆盖率指标会低估规范故障的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统（MSS）的黑盒测试工具高度依赖OpenAPI规范作为输入来生成和评估测试用例。然而，已有研究表明现实中的OpenAPI规范常常存在不完整、不一致或错误的问题，但既有工具评估研究几乎都假设规范是正确的并以此评估工具性能，忽略了规范质量问题对测试效果的影响。这构成了一个关键的研究空白：缺乏系统性研究来揭示规范故障如何、在何种程度上削弱黑盒API测试的有效性。

Method: 研究采用故障注入（Fault Injection）方法。1) 构建文献驱动的六类OpenAPI故障分类：REQUEST_STRICT（收紧请求约束）、REQUEST_LOOSE（放松请求约束）、METHOD_SEM（HTTP方法语义重映射）、RESPONSE_ORACLE（响应预言修改）、PARSE_BROKEN_REF（$ref引用损坏）、COMPOSITE（复合故障）。2) 在五个严重程度级别（P15至P75，表示受影响操作的比例）下注入故障。3) 在两个微服务基准系统上评估：TrainTicket（211个操作的复杂系统）和SocialNetwork（6个操作的简单确认性系统）。4) 使用三种黑盒测试工具：EvoMaster（基于搜索）、RESTler（基于有状态模糊测试）、Schemathesis（基于属性测试）。5) 采用四个度量类别评估影响：代码覆盖率、规范覆盖率、请求/响应质量（2xx/4xx/5xx率）、行为多样性（唯一日志模板和唯一方法-路径对）。共生成超过50个规范变体，执行超过450次运行。

Result: 研究发现：1) **方法语义故障（METHOD_SEM）导致广泛退化**——在五分之六的系统-工具组合中使所有四个度量类别都严重退化，最大退化可达代码覆盖率19.2个百分点、规范覆盖率33.3、请求/响应质量47.9、行为多样性84.0。2) **请求约束放松（REQUEST_LOOSE）导致隐蔽退化**——代码和规范覆盖率保持接近基线，但请求/响应质量严重退化（最高达47.90），这种退化在仅看覆盖率时完全不可见。3) **请求收紧和响应预言故障（REQUEST_STRICT和RESPONSE_ORACLE）呈平坦或反转模式**——整体退化接近零，部分指标甚至低于基线。4) **复合故障（COMPOSITE）呈累积但稀释模式**——多个故障效应合并后产生中等退化，从未超过最强单一故障。工具层面，三个工具在覆盖率指标上高度一致，但在请求/响应质量和行为多样性上分歧显著：EvoMaster和Schemathesis呈现隐蔽退化，RESTler因有状态依赖链架构呈现度量反转。退化严重程度随故障比例增加而单调加深。

Conclusion: OpenAPI规范质量直接塑造黑盒API测试的有效性。研究结论包括：1) 仅依赖代码和规范覆盖率的评估方法会严重低估规范故障的影响，因为放松请求约束导致的隐蔽退化无法被覆盖率指标捕获；2) 评估黑盒API测试工具时应同时纳入请求/响应质量和行为多样性度量；3) 实践者应将OpenAPI规范视为可测试的工件而非固定输入，通过跟踪请求/响应质量和行为多样性相对于基线的变化来检测规范故障；4) 约束类故障可通过规范增强方法解决，但语义类故障（如HTTP方法误用）需要在规范源头修复并借助反模式检测方法定位。

Abstract: OpenAPI specifications are the primary input for black-box testing tools in microservice systems (MSS), yet prior work shows these specifications are often incomplete, inconsistent, or incorrect. Despite this, most studies on OpenAPI-based black-box testing assume correct specifications and evaluate tool performance. We address this gap by introducing a literature-grounded taxonomy of six OpenAPI specification fault classes. We inject faults at five severity levels, and evaluate the resulting mutated specifications on two microservice benchmarks, TrainTicket and SocialNetwork, using three testing tools: EvoMaster, RESTler, and Schemathesis. We measure the impact of these faults using code coverage, specification coverage, request/response quality, and behavioral diversity. Our results show that specification faults cause strong and heterogeneous degradation patterns across testing tools and systems. Faults in method semantics cause broad degradation across all metrics, while others, such as modifications to response codes, remain weak. Relaxations of schema constraints cause hidden degradation, with no impact on code and specification coverage but a large impact on request/response quality. These findings demonstrate that specification quality directly shapes black-box API testing effectiveness. Also, code and specification coverage-only evaluations can understate the impact of specification faults on black-box testing in MSS and should be complemented by request/response quality and behavioral diversity.

</details>


### [66] [TraceSynth: Generating Production-Quality Kernel Traces with Constraint-Guided Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12104)
*Yuvraj Sehgal,Sneh Patel,Mahsa Panahandeh,Naser Ezzati-Jivan,Francois Tetreault*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceSynth提出了一种基于扩散模型（DDPM）的合成内核执行轨迹生成框架，通过Transformer去噪网络联合建模6个轨迹属性（事件类型、时间戳、CPU亲和性、线程ID、进程命令、返回值），并结合从真实轨迹中学习的约束进行后置修复，以在数据稀缺的生产可观测性流水线中增强有限的真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业生产系统中，收集内核级执行轨迹面临运行时开销（1.5–1.6倍）、存储成本和隐私合规（如Ciena等电信公司无法共享客户网络拓扑数据）三大障碍，导致ML诊断模型训练数据稀缺且多样性不足。现有合成方法（马尔可夫链、规则生成器、GAN）无法同时保证统计多样性和结构有效性，无法满足AIOps和系统诊断对高质量训练数据的需求。

Method: 方法包含四个阶段：(1) 预处理——将LTTng原始轨迹解码为多通道事件表示（6个通道），通过全局词汇表编码、log(1+Δt)归一化、滑动窗口（L∈{256,1024,4096}）构造固定长度序列并存储为NPZ格式；(2) 扩散模型——基于Transformer编码器（d_model=256, 4–8层/头, T=1000步线性β调度）的DDPM，离散通道用学习嵌入表、连续时间差用MLP投影，损失函数结合噪声预测L2损失和通道级交叉熵/MSE重构损失；(3) 约束学习与后置修复——从真实轨迹中挖掘4类不变量（事件转移有向图、时间边界、CPU亲和集、属性有效性），通过4种距离指标量化违规，并以贪心替换方式进行后置修复；(4) 下游评估——采用下一事件预测任务（384类，L_pred=128）作为代理指标，在50%真实+50%合成数据上训练Transformer分类器，评估macro-F1等指标。实验在单个NVIDIA H100 GPU上对6个Phoronix基准（ffmpeg、iozone、pybench、scimark2、stream、unpack-linux）进行评估。

Result: 关键结果：(1) RQ1——合成数据增强效果强烈依赖于工作负载类型，scimark2在L=4096时达到87.2% macro-F1（仅比纯真实基线89.8%低2.6个百分点），而I/O密集型stream/iozone退化25–36%；(2) RQ2——约束引导修复在80%的基准-上下文组合中带来0.3–4.3%的提升，收益在短上下文时最大（如L=256时scimark2+4.3%、pybench+4.1%），L=4096时几乎中性；(3) RQ3——上下文长度是主导质量因素，L=4096相对L=256带来+104%的平均相对改进（+29.9个百分点），呈现边际递减；(4) RQ4——轻量级2通道模型（仅事件+时间）保留全6通道模型97–99%的性能，计算成本约减半。

Conclusion: TraceSynth为生产可观测性流水线中的内核轨迹提供了一种成本有效的增强方案。三个核心工业实践洞见：(1) 上下文主导质量，应最大化L=4096；(2) 简洁性可扩展，2通道模型在多数场景下足够；(3) 约束修复提供鲁棒性安全网。对于确定性、计算密集型工作负载，合成增强可达到近真实数据性能；但异步I/O密集型系统仍是挑战。未来工作将聚焦于：Ciena控制平面生产轨迹的工业验证、引入系统状态显式表示以捕获I/O非确定性、采用线性注意力/状态空间模型扩展上下文、以及通过差分隐私提供形式化隐私保证。

Abstract: Machine learning models for system diagnostics rely on kernel execution traces to capture fine-grained system behavior, but collecting production traces in industrial systems is costly due to runtime overhead, storage demands, and privacy constraints. We present TraceSynth, a diffusion-based framework for generating synthetic kernel traces that augment limited real data for downstream ML tasks. TraceSynth models traces as multi-channel sequences (event types, timestamps, CPU affinity, thread identifiers, and process metadata) using a Transformer-based denoising diffusion process with constraint-guided repair to enforce system invariants. Across six benchmarks, results show strong workload dependence. For deterministic, compute-heavy workloads (scimark2), synthetic augmentation achieves 87.2% F1-Macro at context length L=4096, only 2.6 percentage points below real-only baselines. Context length is the dominant quality factor, with L=4096 yielding a +104% relative improvement over L=256, while constraint-guided repair improves synthetic data quality by up to 4.3%. Ablation studies show that lightweight 2-channel models retain 97-99% of the performance of full 6-channel models at roughly half the computational cost. TraceSynth supports cost-effective augmentation of kernel execution traces in production observability pipelines and helps identify when synthetic data can substitute for limited real traces.

</details>


### [67] [TestMiner: Software Testing Analysis for GitHub Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12223)
*Andre Hora,José Miguel Rojas,Romain Robbes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestMiner是一个基于浏览器的零安装Web工具，用于分析GitHub仓库的软件测试实践。它提供测试统计、测试位置、跨版本测试历史及测试依赖等四大分析视图，并支持仓库、Owner和Topic三种粒度的浏览。作者在本科生软件测试课程中让50名学生使用TestMiner探索真实项目，学生反馈积极，成功识别出测试组织、测试演进、测试夹具、Mock和边界测试等多种实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同软件项目在测试策略上差异显著（单元测试 vs e2e测试、Python/Java的独立测试目录 vs Go/Rust的同位测试等），开发者或学生需要花费大量手工探索才能理解项目的测试实践。现有工具如通用仓库挖掘框架缺乏测试专用功能，Codecov/SonarQube需要CI集成，TSDetect/PyNose等测试异味检测工具仅针对本地单元测试。因此需要一个零安装、面向测试、浏览器端的综合分析工具来降低理解真实项目测试实践的门槛。

Method: TestMiner是一个单页应用（SPA），使用React和Vite构建，调用GitHub API和jsDelivr API获取仓库元数据与文件级信息。它通过文件名和路径规则将文件分类为10类（tests、test-helpers、fixtures、e2e、snapshots、benchmarks、mocks、ci-tests、smoke、source code），提供四类报告：（1）Test Overview（统计）；（2）Test Location（文件树可视化，受Distribution Map启发）；（3）Test History（首版、中间版和最新版的测试指标对比）；（4）Test Dependencies（基于GitHub SBOM提取）。此外支持Owner和Topic视图进行跨仓库对比。评估方法是在本科软件测试课程中让50名学生选择真实GitHub仓库，使用TestMiner探索并以开放式问答解释测试实践，通过主题分析对50份回复进行定性归类。

Result: 学生反馈总体积极，50份回复中识别出9类测试实践：测试组织（10次）、测试演进（9次）、测试内容（6次）、Fixtures（5次）、Mocking（5次）、测试类型（5次）、文档测试（4次）、边界用例（3次）和测试依赖（3次）。代表性案例包括发现Streamlit测试集中于e2e/lib/frontend、Newtonsoft.Json从7个测试文件增长到200个、FastAPI的测试兼作文档降低过时风险、NestJS因模块架构而重e2e测试等。定性分析表明TestMiner有效降低了参与不熟悉代码库的门槛，具有教学与实践双重价值。

Conclusion: TestMiner作为一款零安装、浏览器端的测试专用分析工具，能有效帮助开发者和学生理解开源项目的测试实践。局限性包括GitHub API速率限制（可通过token提升至5000次/小时）、API与代码库同步不一致、大仓库返回信息可能不完整。未来工作包括集成tree-sitter解析器进行源码分析，以及利用LLM生成自然语言摘要报告，使分析结果更易理解。

Abstract: Software systems have unique testing characteristics. Some projects can emphasize unit tests, while others may focus on end-to-end testing. Test organization may vary across ecosystems: in languages like Python and Java, tests are typically placed in dedicated folders, whereas Go and Rust projects commonly co-locate tests with source code. These distinctions make it harder to understand how a project approaches testing. In this paper, we present TestMiner, a tool for exploring software testing in GitHub repositories. TestMiner provides an overview of a project's testing practices, including test statistics, test location, test metrics across releases, and dependencies related to testing. We used TestMiner in an undergraduate Software Testing course, where 50 students explored the testing practices of real-world GitHub repositories. Overall, students expressed positive feedback regarding TestMiner. They were able to critically explore a variety of testing practices, including test organization, test evolution, test fixtures, mocking, and edge-case testing. TestMiner is available at: this https URL . Screencast: this https URL .

</details>


### [68] [WIP: Software Engineering Competencies in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12259)
*Lynn Vonderhaar,Massood Towhidnejad,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: A university education is meant to fully prepare graduates to enter and succeed in the workforce in their field. Accreditation bodies and professional organizations help to achieve this by publishing curriculum content, skills and competency models to outline the basics that universities must teach within a degree program. Although these curriculum guidelines and competency models are periodically updated to align university curricula with current industry needs, the needs of industry evolve rapidly and are often not properly reflected within the model, a situation which is occurring now with the rise of Artificial Intelligence (AI) use in industry. This paper reviews literature describing the AI needs within industry to motivate two new AI competencies, AI Literacy and AI development, within the Software Engineering Competency Model (SWECOM). This work then analyzes the industry needs described in the literature to propose the necessary skill areas for these new competencies.

Abstract: A university education is meant to fully prepare graduates to enter and succeed in the workforce in their field. Accreditation bodies and professional organizations help to achieve this by publishing curriculum content, skills and competency models to outline the basics that universities must teach within a degree program. Although these curriculum guidelines and competency models are periodically updated to align university curricula with current industry needs, the needs of industry evolve rapidly and are often not properly reflected within the model, a situation which is occurring now with the rise of Artificial Intelligence (AI) use in industry.
