This work then analyzes the industry needs described in the literature to propose the necessary skill areas for these new competencies.

</details>


### [69] [XScientist: A Git-Like Research Protocol for Long-Running Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12301)
*Jixiang Luo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: XScientist 提出了一种类似 Git 的研究协议与操作系统，用于长时间运行的自主科学发现。其核心创新是 Agent-Native Research Artifact (ARA) 协议，将每次研究运行视为可移植、可审计、可分叉的研究工件（而非仅生成 PDF），通过探索 DAG、声明-证据锚定、内容哈希与来源追踪，实现失败分支、修复过程与论文声明的可追溯性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究系统多以"一次性论文生成器"的范式进行评估，仅产出稿件与少量实验日志，掩盖了科学研究在长时间运行、多分支、易失败、需人机可审计交接方面的核心运行难题。一份看似合理的论文并不等于可信赖的研究基础设施，关键问题在于如何管理证据、失败、修订与跨时间的交接。因此，需要一种将研究过程本身视为可观察、可分叉、可审计工件的新范式。

Method: XScientist 由四层设计原则驱动：可观察、可分叉、声明可溯源、不确定性显式化。系统整合了七类核心组件：(1) 构思与规划，将主题与源队列转化为带验收检查的研究计划；(2) 实验执行，采用树搜索循环记录代码、输出、指标与失败状态；(3) 写作与评审，生成 LaTeX 稿件并执行多策略（新颖性、严谨性、清晰度、可复现性）评审；(4) 索引与管理；(5) 核心 ARA 协议层，采用 manifest.json + exploration_graph.json 的目录契约，记录节点代码、日志、指标、声明锚点、验证报告与 provenance；(6) 质量与完整性控制层，包括自评-修复回路、真值契约（Truth Contracts，将计划转化为可校验的客观事实、可比性约束、工件绑定规则）、样本门（Sample Gates，防止将缺陷计划放大为完整论文）、确定性完整性取证（确定性跨账本与一致性检查）；(7) 长时间运行的守护进程层，支持源队列、健康跟踪、仪表盘、暂停/恢复、反馈闭环与策略调度。配套 CLI 工具 run_ara_fork.py 提供 inspect、exec、fork、freeze、validate、verify、diff、log、refs 等操作动词。

Result: 本文为系统报告而非基准对比，不声称 XScientist 优于其他自主研究系统。其评估策略以工件为中心，判断一次运行是否产出：(1) 包含 DAG 探索图的合法 ARA；(2) 节点级代码、指标、日志与环境描述符；(3) 声明锚点与证据敏感语句的覆盖率摘要；(4) 解释稿件修改过程的评审与修复工件；(5) 区分硬性/软性发现的确定性完整性报告；(6) 足够支持他人分叉、验证或拒收的 provenance。论文完整实现了该系统并在 GitHub 公开仓库（smileformylove/XScientist）开源，包含实现、协议 schema、命令行工具、示例与文档。论文相对一次性生成器在主输出、失败处理、延续方式、声明锚定、评审记录与运维等维度均给出对比分析。

Conclusion: XScientist 将自主科学研究从一次性论文生成推向可复现、可评审、可分叉的研究基础设施。其 ARA 协议把每篇论文转化为科学探索树，使失败分支、修复实验、消融与稿件声明始终与产生它们的节点保持关联。结合自评修复回路、样本门、真值契约、确定性完整性取证与守护进程调度，该设计显著提高了"沉默证据丢失"的成本并使后续评审更具操作性。然而，系统并不使自主研究自动正确——人审仍是科学有效性、伦理判断与最终投稿决策的必要条件；完整性取证虽确定性但不完备；ARA 是协议接口而非通用标准；长时间自主运行仍需显式预算、停机条件与运维监督。未来工作应比较 ARA 支持的延续方式与冷启动基线、衡量重执行成本、研究声明锚定评审是否能改善人类错误检测。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are often evaluated as one-shot paper generators: given a topic, they produce a manuscript and a small set of experiment logs. This framing hides the operational problem that makes such systems difficult to trust: research is long-running, branching, failure-prone, and dependent on auditable handoffs between agents and humans. XScientist is a git-like research protocol and operating system for this setting. It orchestrates idea generation, experiment execution, manuscript drafting, self-review, repair, quality gating, daemon scheduling, and reproducibility artifacts as one continuously observable pipeline. The central design choice is to treat each run as a portable research artifact rather than only as a PDF. XScientist exports an Agent-Native Research Artifact (ARA), a protocol that records an exploration DAG, per-node code and outputs, claim-to-evidence anchors, content hashes, provenance, and re-execution hooks. This makes each generated paper inspectable as a science exploration tree: failed branches, repaired experiments, ablations, and manuscript claims remain connected to the nodes that produced them. The system also includes deterministic integrity forensics, sample gates, truth contracts, reviewer-oriented repair loops, and long-running daemon controls. This paper describes the current XScientist architecture, the ARA protocol surface, and the practical safeguards needed to move autonomous science from single-run demos toward reproducible, reviewable, and forkable research infrastructure. The implementation and manuscript source are maintained in the public GitHub repository at this https URL .

</details>


### [70] [How Many Interviews Are Enough in a Software Engineering Study? Preliminary Findings on Sample Size and Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12326)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Mauricio Rodrigues,Cleyton Magalhaes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2016–2025年发表在ESEM、EASE、ICSE、FSE和EMSE五大软件工程会议/期刊上的429篇使用访谈的论文，探讨了软件工程实证研究中访谈样本量充分性和饱和度的操作化方式。研究发现样本量差异显著（1–100+不等），最常见的范围为13–24人，但样本量充分性和饱和度的明确讨论在软件工程研究中仍然不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 访谈研究在软件工程实证研究中广泛应用，但样本量充分性和饱和度的报告缺乏一致性。现有方法论指南虽强调透明度和充分性，但未给出具体的访谈数量建议；同时，跨学科定性研究已更直接地讨论了访谈数量、停止标准和饱和度范围。软件工程领域缺乏对这些实践操作方式的系统性实证考察，导致同行评审中经常因样本量问题否定定性研究的合理性。本研究旨在通过大规模系统映射填补这一空白。

Method: 采用类似系统映射研究的方法，手动检索2016–2025年间ESEM、EASE、ICSE、FSE四大会议和EMSE期刊的论文（共6,834篇），通过自动化脚本根据标题、关键词、摘要筛选可能涉及定性研究的论文（608篇），再由两位作者手动审阅全文确认实际使用访谈的研究（最终429篇）。使用描述性统计对样本量分布进行量化分析，并根据定性方法文献将样本量划分为若干特征范围（&lt;5、5–12、13–24、25–40、41–60、&gt;60）。此外，从各范围中分层随机抽取14篇论文进行初步的定性主题分析，研究样本量论证和饱和度讨论。

Result: 429篇论文共涉及7,444次访谈，平均17.35人/研究。样本量分布差异显著：13–24人最常见（152篇），其次为5–12人（144篇）、25–40人（64篇）、&lt;5人（46篇）、41–60人（14篇）、&gt;60人（9篇）。半结构化访谈是最主流形式（407篇）。在14篇初步分析的论文中，样本量论证多为情境性、实用性理由（如组织访问限制、参与者专业性），而非方法论依据；明确的饱和度讨论仅出现在4篇中（其中2篇为扎根理论研究）。研究指出样本量高度依赖研究目标、方法论、分析深度、参与者可及性和组织范围等因素，而非固定数值标准。

Conclusion: 软件工程访谈研究的样本量充分性高度依赖情境而非统一数值标准，13–24人是最常见的实证范围。明确的方法论论证和饱和度讨论在该领域仍不充分，尤其在扎根理论研究之外。建议提高样本量决策的透明度，更多地运用代码饱和、意义饱和和信息力等当代饱和概念，并加强定性研究报告的规范性。但不应将样本量本身作为论文拒绝的依据，而应关注研究情境的合理性和方法论论证的充分性。未来工作将把定性分析扩展到全部429篇论文中。

Abstract: Background. Interview based studies are widely used in empirical software engineering to investigate human, organizational, and socio technical phenomena, yet interview sample adequacy and saturation are reported inconsistently across the literature. Aims. This paper investigates how interview sample adequacy and saturation are operationalized in empirical software engineering research. Method. We analyzed papers published between 2016 and 2025 across major software engineering venues, focusing on interview sample sizes, saturation discussions, and sample adequacy justifications. Results. Preliminary findings indicate substantial variation in sample sizes, from highly specialized small sample studies to broader investigations involving large interview datasets. Studies involving fewer than 12 interviewees were common and frequently associated with specialized industrial contexts or constrained organizational access. However, the most recurrent range was 13 to 24 interviewees, suggesting that moderate sized samples represent the most common configuration in empirical software engineering research. Saturation and sample adequacy justification were heterogeneous, with many studies relying on implicit or contextual reasoning rather than explicit methodological discussion. Conclusions. Our findings provide initial empirical insights into methodological reporting practices in interview based software engineering research and contribute to ongoing discussions regarding qualitative rigor and transparency.

</details>


### [71] [Skills That Don't Exist: A Large-Scale Study of Hallucinated Skill Recommendation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12340)
*Weifeng Yuan,Wenbo Guo,Feng Dong,Haoyu Wang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM智能体中的"技能名称幻觉"（skill name hallucination）进行了大规模实证研究，揭示了一种名为"技能名称抢注"（skill name squatting）的供应链攻击：智能体系统性编造不存在的技能名称，攻击者可预先注册这些名称来劫持未来的推荐流程，导致恶意技能安装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Anthropic于2025年12月发布Agent Skills标准、OpenAI迅速跟进，技能（skill）成为LLM智能体扩展能力的新载体，SkillsMP等注册中心已收录超过170万技能。由于目录过于庞大，开发者通常直接询问智能体"该用哪个skill"，由智能体推荐并自动安装。然而，LLM的幻觉问题在此场景下产生了一个全新的供应链攻击入口：智能体可能自信地推荐根本不存在的技能名称。由于注册中心对发布者审核薄弱，攻击者只需批量收集这些反复出现的幻觉名称并预先抢注，就能让未来的受害者通过自己的智能体安装恶意SKILL.md。该研究旨在系统性地量化这一威胁的严重程度。

Method: 研究采用三阶段流水线：(1) 提示构建——构建15,000条提示，分为两类：来自开发者论坛（Stack Exchange、Reddit、Hacker News）的5,000条社区真实问题，以及基于SkillsMP上10,000个真实技能描述构造的技能描述提示；(2) 推荐生成——评估12种配置（4个独立LLM + 8个智能体），涵盖GLM-4.5-Air、GPT-5.4-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.7等模型以及Claude Code、Codex、OpenClaw、OpenCode等智能体框架，多数配置启用网页搜索以排除知识截止日期的干扰；(3) 技能名称验证——采用两级验证，先对照SkillsMP、ClawHub等注册中心，再通过GitHub Code Search检索SKILL.md文件进行扩展验证。研究围绕四个研究问题展开：RQ1流行度（评估12种配置的幻觉率）、RQ2可攻击性（测量幻觉名称的重复性和跨模型收敛性）、RQ3可检测性（评估LLM自审和编辑距离过滤器的效果）、RQ4缓解策略（测试RAG、自我精炼、跨模型投票四种防御方法）。

Result: 关键发现：(1) 流行度方面——所有12种配置均产生幻觉，幻觉率从6.5%（GPT-5.4-mini）到62.0%（OpenCode+Ministral-3 8B）不等，独立LLM平均36.0%，智能体平均36.9%，真实开发者问题场景上升至43.1%，共产生5,669个不同的幻觉名称；GPT-5.4-mini的低幻觉率是因52.5%提示未返回输出造成的假象。(2) 可攻击性方面——Claude Sonnet 4.6的主导幻觉名称在10次运行中平均重复7.8次，53%的提示中10次均返回完全相同的名称；410个名称在两个或以上配置中共享，占全部幻觉推荐事件的15.3%；851个（15.0%）幻觉名称恰好对应PyPI或npm上的真实包；攻击者只需抢注前500个高频幻觉名称即可覆盖57%的幻觉推荐事件。(3) 可检测性方面——LLM自审的平衡准确率仅51%，等同于随机猜测；幻觉名称与真实技能的平均编辑距离为6.85，中位数为6，远超传统typosquatting过滤器的检测范围。(4) 缓解策略方面——RAG将幻觉率从40.8%降至3.2%（最佳防御），但即使最佳防御下的智能体推荐正确技能的概率仅为约六分之一（recall≤16%）；自我精炼效果有限（仅降至33.2%），主要通过抑制输出来"减少"幻觉；跨模型投票（N=2）消除92.8%幻觉但保留的真实技能降至44.8%。通过良性概念验证（PoC）证实了攻击路径的可行性：在隔离的Claude Code沙箱中，攻击者注册一个反复出现的幻觉名称后，智能体能自动搜索、安装该技能。

Conclusion: 技能名称幻觉是一种结构性而非配置性的系统性漏洞，无法通过模型调优、提示工程或解码参数调节来根除。其核心问题是模型倾向于将能力描述（如file-system-operations）当作名称，而非回忆已发布的技能标识符，且网页搜索无法区分"该名称代表某种能力"与"该名称是有效的技能"。即便是最强的模型级防御（RAG）也以牺牲可用性为代价，最佳防御系统推荐正确技能的概率不超过六分之一。解决该问题不能仅依赖模型层防御，需要生态系统层面的结构性变革：(1) 注册中心层面的名称预留（namespace reservation）以阻断抢注；(2) 经过验证的推荐管道，要求只能从已认证的技能索引中检索并拒绝无法解析到具体SKILL.md的名称。技能以自然语言指令而非编译代码为载体，传统软件供应链的artifact级检测信号更弱，这要求技能生态的安全防护必须比传统包注册中心更加显式和严格。

Abstract: LLM agents acquire new capabilities by downloading skills from open registries. Instead of browsing these catalogs manually, developers typically ask the agent to recommend and install a skill. This convenience hides a risk: agents frequently invent names for skills that exist in no registry. We term this flaw skill name hallucination. A fake name may seem harmless, but it opens the door to supply-chain attacks. Because registries rarely verify publishers, an adversary can prompt the agent, collect the fake names it returns, pre-register malicious skills under them, and wait for a victim to install the payload. We conducted the first large-scale measurement of skill name hallucination, evaluating 15,000 prompts across 12 configurations (4 standalone LLMs and 8 agents). We conservatively counted a name as hallucinated only if it was missing from all live registries and GitHub. The results reveal a systemic vulnerability: every configuration hallucinates. Rates average 36.0% for standalone LLMs and 36.9% for agents, rising to 43.1% on real-world developer questions. In total, the systems generated 5,669 distinct hallucinated names. Crucially, these names are not random noise. Agents repeat the same fake names across prompts and models, giving attackers highly reliable targets to hijack. Finally, we tested four model-level defenses and found a severe conflict between security and usability. The strongest, retrieval grounding, cut the hallucination rate from 40.8% to 3.2% but crippled usefulness: even the best-defended system recommended the correct skill only about one in six times. Skill name hallucination is thus a highly exploitable vulnerability requiring minimal attacker effort. Fixing it cannot rely on prompt engineering or model tuning alone. It demands ecosystem-wide structural changes: registry-level name reservations and verified recommendation pipelines.

</details>


### [72] [Detecting Rendering Bugs in Imperative Data Visualization Libraries via Equivalent Mutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12363)
*Weiqi Lu,Yongqiang Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VizDetour是一种针对命令式数据可视化库（如matplotlib、bokeh、plotly）的自动化测试方法，通过"端点保持突变"（endpoint-preserving mutation）将缺失测试预言问题转化为等价性检查问题，从而检测静默的视觉渲染缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 命令式数据可视化库的渲染缺陷通常表现为不正确的视觉输出而非崩溃或异常，难以自动检测。其根本挑战在于缺乏指定任意绘图脚本预期渲染效果的预言（oracle），且对一个图形元素的更新可能意外影响其他元素或属性。现有模糊测试方法只能发现崩溃型缺陷，无法捕捉这些静默的视觉错误，而这类缺陷在主流DataViz库中广泛存在并逃避回归测试。

Method: VizDetour从一个种子绘图脚本出发，追加一段语义等价的API调用序列，该序列临时修改可视化状态后恢复至原始状态。核心组件包括：(1) 从运行时状态构建render-tree以近似内部渲染状态；(2) 均匀采样可追踪的图形元素和可变属性，并生成访问表达式；(3) 三种端点保持突变算子——set-revert（设置-恢复）、redundant-set（冗余设置）、remove-readd（移除-重新添加）；(4) 使用感知哈希（pHash）距离作为视觉预言比较种子脚本与变异脚本的渲染输出。通过38,158次空突变校准异常阈值τ=2。

Result: 在matplotlib 3.10.8、bokeh 3.10.0和plotly 6.8.0上使用官方示例库的2,487个种子脚本进行评估，VizDetour发现了47个先前未知的bug，其中39个已被开发者确认，18个已修复。多数为静默视觉缺陷（34个视觉缺陷、5个崩溃），涵盖视觉编码、注释、布局和比例尺等组件。在matplotlib上，VizDetour代码覆盖率（~37%）高于Atheris和Fuzz4All基线，并检测到3个这两个基线都未发现的历史视觉缺陷。消融实验显示set-revert算子贡献最大，移除后新bug检测下降70.2%、确认bug下降64.1%。

Conclusion: VizDetour通过端点保持突变，将数据可视化库测试的绝对预言问题转化为相对视觉比较，无需第二个实现或共同规范。在三个主流Python DataViz库中发现了大量先前未知的静默渲染缺陷，性能优于领域无关的模糊测试工具，证明了端点保持突变作为单实现可视化库测试策略的有效性。未来工作将扩展到声明式库、其他语言生态系统以及更丰富的语义预言和分类支持。

Abstract: Imperative data visualization libraries construct plots through a sequence of stateful API calls that incrementally create and update graphic elements. Rendering bugs in these libraries often manifest as incorrect visual outputs rather than crashes or exceptions, making them difficult to detect automatically. A fundamental challenge is the lack of an oracle that specifies the expected rendering of an arbitrary plotting script. Furthermore, an update to one graphic element may inadvertently affect other elements or properties, leading to subtle inconsistencies in the final rendered image. This paper presents VIZDETOUR, an automated testing approach for detecting rendering bugs in imperative data visualization libraries via equivalent mutations. The key idea is to transform the oracle problem into an equivalence-checking problem. Starting from a seed plotting script, VIZDETOUR appends a short sequence of semantically equivalent API calls that temporarily modify the visualization state and then restore it to its original state. Although these mutations exercise different execution paths, they should preserve the final rendering. Any visual discrepancy between the original and mutated scripts therefore indicates a rendering bug. To generate such mutations, VIZDETOUR constructs a render tree from the seed script, identifies traceable graphic elements and mutable properties, and synthesizes endpoint-preserving mutation sequences. It then compares the rendered outputs using perceptual hashing. We evaluate VIZDETOUR on matplotlib, bokeh, and plotly using scripts collected from their official example galleries. VIZDETOUR discovers 47 previously unknown bugs, of which 39 are confirmed and 18 are fixed.

</details>


### [73] [Automatic Testing of Interacting Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12452)
*Fabio Cavaleri,Alessio Gambi,Paolo Arcaini,Dejan Ničković,Mauro Pezzè*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EVITA（EVolutionary Interaction Testing Approach），一种基于多目标优化（NSGA-II）的自动驾驶车辆（AV）交互测试方法。该方法通过检测多辆AV的轨迹重规划来识别交互，并利用交互地图分类交互类型，自动生成能够触发多样化、多辆车交互的关键测试场景，从而暴露单AV测试方法无法发现的安全关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于场景的AV测试方法主要针对单辆自动驾驶车辆，使用预编程的NPC（不可玩角色）作为交通参与者，这些方法无法捕捉多辆AV之间因相互反应而产生的紧急行为。多AV之间n路交互引发的组合爆炸问题亟需解决；同时，随着公共道路上AV数量和多样性的增加，多辆AV相互交互导致危险情况的概率显著上升，传统单AV测试方法存在系统性遗漏。

Method: EVITA采用NSGA-II多目标遗传算法，自动生成CommonRoad格式的多AV驾驶场景。核心方法包括：(1) 交互识别——通过比较连续规划轨迹的累积欧氏距离（ED）检测条件C1（轨迹重规划），结合感知模块信息判断条件C2（重规划是否由场景元素引起），涵盖Apollo和FrenetiX两种仿真器；(2) 交互分类——使用交互地图将交互分为前、左、右、后等8个方向类型，构建二维网格量化交互的多样性；(3) 三目标适应度函数——最大化交互数量（fit_int）、最大化交互多样性（fit_div，与已生成场景的距离）、最小化AV数量（fit_numAVs）；(4) 场景验证——确保初始位置可达、安全距离、目标可达性等条件。实验在FrenetiX（5个高速公路/郊区道路网络）和Baidu Apollo（Borregas Ave城市道路）两个仿真平台进行，与现有最优方法DoppelTest对比，重复10次实验并采用Mann-Whitney U检验和Vargha-Delaney的Â12效应量进行统计分析。

Result: 实验结果（5个研究问题）表明：(1) RQ1交互暴露——在FrenetiX场景中，EVITA在交互总数和唯一交互数上均显著优于DoppelTest（大数据效应量），覆盖了更多交互类型；在Apollo城市场景中两者接近，但EVITA的场景复杂度更低；(2) RQ2碰撞暴露——EVITA在唯一碰撞数上显著优于DoppelTest（FrenetiX大数据效应量；Apollo场景生成53次碰撞和14种唯一碰撞 vs 30次/4种），覆盖更广的碰撞点、相对速度和角度范围；(3) RQ3交通违规——EVITA在闯红灯、超速和停车标志违规上均优于DoppelTest；(4) RQ4场景复杂度——EVITA生成场景中大部分含2-4辆车，而DoppelTest以5-8辆车为主，EVITA生成了更简洁的场景；(5) RQ5有效性——无效场景数量可忽略（FrenetiX中3小时生成200-400个场景，每场景27-34秒；Apollo中12小时生成470-570个场景）。

Conclusion: EVITA是AV交互测试领域的重要进展，通过提供精确且可计算的AV交互定义，有效识别多AV交互引发的安全关键场景。其贡献在于：(1) 形式化了系统级的AV交互测试方法学，能够有效过滤不相关交互以提高测试效率；(2) 将测试范围扩展到不同制造商AV之间的交互；(3) 在多种交通条件和静态/动态障碍物下验证了方法的通用性。EVITA为大规模自动驾驶质量保证提供了实用框架，代码和数据已开源以支持复现和后续研究。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) must be thoroughly tested to meet high safety standards and avoid endangering both AV passengers and road users. Scenario-based testing implements driving scenarios in virtual simulation environments as a cost-effective alternative to field testing. Common scenario-based testing approaches set the environment and the surrounding traffic and test a single AV. Recent studies show that the approaches that test single AVs miss critical behaviors that emerge from interactions among multiple AVs. Effective approaches to test scenarios that emerge from n-way interactions must address the combinatorial explosion that the presence of multiple AVs further exacerbates. In this paper, we propose EVITA, an approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to generate scenarios that trigger multiple and diverse AVs interactions, while minimizing the complexity of the generated scenarios, to effectively test multiple interacting AVs and reveal safety-critical scenarios that current approaches overlook. The experimental results that we discuss in this paper confirm that EVITA triggers a higher variety of AVs interactions than state-of-the-art approaches, thus improving the likelihood to reveal safety-critical behaviors.

</details>


### [74] [CodeOwl: Automatic Generation of Tiered Parsons Problems for Introductory Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12460)
*Luca Cisternino,Florian Obermüller,Gordon Fraser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为 CodeOwl 的 AI 驱动工具，用于自动生成分层 Parsons 问题（tiered Parsons problems），以应对编程教育中学生能力差异的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程教育中学生在速度、先验知识和动机等方面存在显著异质性。差异教学（如分层任务序列）能让不同水平的学生在适合的难度下学习，但手工制作这些教学资源耗时耗力。因此，亟需一种自动化方法来生成高质量的分层编程练习。

Method: 提出 CodeOwl 工具：从样例任务或特定编程概念出发，自动生成具有层级结构的 Parsons 问题（将完整代码拆分为可拖拽代码块的题型）。采用混合方法评估框架，包含三个维度：(1) 对 297 个分层序列（每个三层）的复杂度分析；(2) 专家对生成题目陈述的清晰度评分；(3) 教师与学生的用户研究。

Result: 复杂度分析显示，98.7% 的分层序列实现了从 Tier 1 到 Tier 3 的难度递增；专家评定生成的题目表述清晰度高；教师认可工具的实用性，但希望增强对课程对齐（curriculum alignment）的控制；学生反馈积极，但希望改进反馈机制和增加交互方式。

Conclusion: CodeOwl 能够有效自动化生成高质量的分层 Parsons 问题，在复杂度阶梯设计和题目表述方面表现出色。未来工作应聚焦于增强教师对课程内容的定制化控制、丰富反馈机制以及拓展交互模式，从而进一步提升工具的教学适用性。

Abstract: Addressing learner heterogeneity in programming education is challenging due to variations in student speed, prior knowledge, and motivation. While differentiated instruction, such as tiered sequences, allows students to engage at appropriate difficulty levels, manually creating these resources is labour-intensive. This paper introduces CodeOwl, an AI-driven tool that automates the generation of tiered Parsons problems. Starting from a sample task or specific programming concepts, CodeOwl produces tiered sequences of Parsons problems automatically. We evaluated CodeOwl with a mixed-method framework comprising complexity analysis, expert ratings, and user studies. Analysis of 297 tiered sequences (three tiers each) revealed that 98.7% achieved a positive complexity increase, successfully rising in difficulty from Tier 1 to Tier 3. Experts rated the generated problem statements as highly clear. While teachers praised the tool's utility, they identified a need for greater control over curriculum alignment. Similarly, students reported positively but requested enhanced feedback mechanisms and alternative interaction modes.

</details>


### [75] [Understanding before Naming! Enhancing LLM-based Method Name Prediction with Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12467)
*Wei Liu,Weisong Sun,Tingting Xu,Hanwei Qian,Yi Zhao,Chunrong Fang,Xia Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SMNP框架,通过将代码摘要作为语义桥梁,结合面向MNP的摘要生成和思维链(CoT)增强的细化策略,提升基于大语言模型(LLM)的方法名预测质量。研究系统比较了6种传统指标、5种LLM评估器和6位人类评估者的相关性,并在5种LLM和2个数据集(Java/Python)上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法名预测(MNP)研究面临两大挑战:(1)评估方法主要依赖基于token重叠的指标(如F1、Exact Match),无法反映人类对语义质量的判断,导致评估结果与开发者实际期望存在偏差;(2)现有LLM-based MNP方法采用直接代码到名称的映射策略,与人类"先理解后命名"的认知过程不一致,这种不一致对生成名称质量的影响未知。

Method: 研究分为三部分:(1)经验研究——比较6种指标评估器、5种LLM评估器(GPT-4o、DeepSeek、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen、Qwen-Coder)与6位人类评估者的Spearman相关性;(2)策略对比——比较直接生成策略(DGS)与摘要-细化策略(SRS),通过案例研究识别出朴素SRS的三大缺陷:摘要语义不准确(64例)、细化语义不对齐(265例,含动词和宾语两类)和语义分数接近(94例);(3)提出SMNP框架,包含两个核心组件:①面向MNP的摘要生成(任务导向零样本提示,聚焦核心动作、对象和结果)与②CoT增强的细化(三步推理:理解核心目的→识别关键动作→确定目标实体),并在5个LLM和CSN-Java/Python数据集上进行消融实验。

Result: 主要发现包括:(1)LLM评估器(尤其是DeepSeek)与传统指标相比与人类判断相关性最强,Spearman系数显著更高;(2)SRS在多数LLM配置下优于DGS,偏好比例(26%-43%)明显高于DGS(18%-35%);(3)SMNP在所有5个LLM和两种语言上一致优于朴素SRS,Java平均分从3.30-3.91提升至3.51-4.02;对摘要语义不准确问题解决率达78%,语义宾语不对齐解决率76%,语义动词不对齐解决率70%;(4)消融研究表明CoT第2步(识别关键动作)是最关键组件,移除后性能下降最显著;(5)GPT-4o生成质量最佳,SMNP改进在Java上效果更为显著。

Conclusion: 研究证实:(1)LLM评估方法比传统自动指标更适合评估方法名质量,与人类评估相关性更强;(2)模拟人类"先理解后命名"过程的摘要-细化策略能产生更高质量的方法名;(3)通过解决朴素SRS的局限性(摘要语义不准确和细化语义不对齐),SMNP有效提升方法名质量。代码与数据集已开源(https://github.com/software-theorem/SMNP),为LLM-based方法名评估与预测的未来研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Method names are critical to software quality, affecting code comprehensibility, maintainability, and developer collaboration. However, manually designing meaningful method names is challenging. Method Name Prediction (MNP), which automatically generates method names from code snippets, has recently attracted attention. Although large language models (LLMs) show promising performance for MNP, two challenges remain. First, existing evaluations mainly rely on token similarity metrics, which often fail to reflect human judgments of semantic quality. Second, current LLM-based MNP methods usually generate names through direct code-to-name mapping, which differs from the human process of understanding functionality before naming. To address these challenges, we conduct empirical studies on LLM-based evaluation and MNP strategies. We compare 6 metric-based evaluators, 5 LLM-based evaluators, and 6 human evaluators. Results show that LLM-based evaluators, especially DeepSeek-based evaluators, are more consistent with human judgments than traditional metrics. We further compare direct generation and summarization-and-refinement strategies. Results indicate that summarization and refinement generally improve the semantic quality of generated names. Case studies reveal three limitations: inaccurate summaries, semantic misalignment, and close semantic scores. Based on these findings, we propose SMNP, an MNP approach combining MNP-oriented summarization and chain-of-thought enhanced refinement. Experiments on 5 LLMs and 2 datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of SMNP.

</details>


### [76] [Taming the Drift: Context-aware Repair of Dockerfile Drift during Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12541)
*Chengjie Wang,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Tianyue Luo,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Cadre框架，通过构建上下文感知依赖图(CDG)和两步式LLM修复工作流，自动修复Dockerfile与源代码演化之间的不一致导致的CI/CD构建失败问题。同时构建了包含1,040个可复现漂移实例的D³基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dockerfile漂移（Dockerfile与演化的源代码之间的不一致）是导致CI/CD构建失败的主要原因之一，但现有规则式和检索式修复方法孤立地分析Dockerfile，无法处理依赖构建上下文的复杂故障，导致大量构建失败需开发者手动修复数天。核心挑战包括：提取精确构建上下文、推断指令间依赖、定位失败相关的因果信息。

Method: Cadre框架包含四个核心组件：(1) DodeX流水线，从GitHub Actions CI日志中持续挖掘真实漂移实例并捕获完整构建配置；(2) 指令级上下文分析器(Instruction-level Context Profiler)，通过模拟Docker构建过程逐指令跟踪工作目录、环境变量和文件集合，覆盖11种主流构建生态系统；(3) 上下文感知依赖图(CDG)，将指令映射到文件级和跨指令依赖的有向无环图；(4) 上下文精化修复(CRR)，采用两步式LLM代理工作流——先进行上下文查询选择相关文件，再生成针对性修复补丁。

Result: 在D³基准的653个可执行实例上，Cadre实现35.22%的修复率，分别是基于规则的Parfum基线的2.78倍、最佳LLM基线(Vanilla-LLM)的1.24倍。两步式工作流使95.25%的提示词保持在30k token以下，彻底消除了基线方法因提示溢出导致的41-58例修复失败(|F_r|=0)。在跨4个以上构建阶段的复杂场景中，Cadre相对Vanilla-LLM的优势扩大至70%。漂移陈旧化分析显示，在5次以上提交距离时，Cadre修复率25.9%，优于Vanilla-LLM的19.7%，证明CDG的结构信号比diff信号更具鲁棒性。消融实验表明CDG和两步式工作流贡献超加性，组合增益6.74%超出独立贡献之和5.52%。

Conclusion: 上下文结构而非上下文容量决定修复质量。CDG的结构化依赖建模和两步式上下文选择机制相互强化，是提升Dockerfile漂移修复效果的关键。该方法为面向IaC的智能维护工具提供了通用抽象，未来可拓展至其他顺序式IaC语言的上下文感知维护。

Abstract: Docker is widely used to create reproducible build environments, but Dockerfile drift, the divergence between a Dockerfile and its evolving source code, can cause CI/CD builds to fail. Existing rule-based and retrieval-based repair approaches analyze Dockerfiles in isolation and therefore struggle with context-dependent failures. We present Cadre, a context-aware framework for automated Dockerfile drift repair. Cadre uses static analysis to construct a Context-aware Dependency Graph (CDG), which maps each Dockerfile instruction to its file-level and inter-instruction dependencies. Guided by the CDG, Cadre first selects the context causally relevant to a failure and then generates a targeted patch from that context. We also introduce DodeX, a pipeline that mines real-world Dockerfile drift instances from GitHub Actions CI logs while preserving the complete build configurations omitted by static-snapshot datasets. Using DodeX, we construct $D^3$, a benchmark of 1,040 drift instances reproducible locally with the original CI parameters. Across $D^3$, Cadre achieves a 35.22\% repair rate, 2.78$\times$ that of the rule-based baseline and 1.24$\times$ that of the best LLM-based baseline. Its two-step workflow keeps 95.25\% of prompts below 30k tokens and avoids the prompt-overflow failures that prevent competing LLM-based methods from producing patches in 41 to 58 cases per method. Ablation results confirm that both the CDG and the two-step workflow improve repair performance. Cadre's advantage over diff-only approaches also increases as drift ages across commits, supporting explicit dependency modeling for context-aware infrastructure-as-code maintenance. Code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [77] [Evaluation Bias and Epistemic Inequality in Global Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12563)
*Sam Khosravi,Amir H. Payberah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过实地混合方法研究（48名软件工程师，来自卢旺达、乌干达、瑞典、荷兰四国），揭示了全球软件开发中测量绩效与感知能力之间的系统性差距：欧洲工程师系统性低估东非工程师的能力，尽管卢旺达高级工程师的表现在技术任务上可与欧洲同行媲美甚至超越。研究揭示了评估偏见如何在全球软件劳动市场中再现认知不公正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球软件开发日益依赖跨区域协作，但工程师的能力评估受到刻板印象和赤字叙事的影响，构成认知不公正和程序性不公平问题。现有研究主要集中在亚洲、东欧和南美，对非洲（尤其是卢旺达、乌干达）情境下的工程实践和评估缺乏实证研究，亟需填补这一空白以促进公平的全球技术协作。

Method: 采用实用主义混合方法设计：48名软件工程师（每国10-15人，含初级与高级），在四国实地进行60-120分钟的会话。三项技术评估任务：(1) 编程任务（独特配对求和），用CodeBERT嵌入+PCA+K-means聚类分析；(2) 系统设计挑战（URL缩短服务）；(3) 代码审查挑战（含故意漏洞的身份验证系统），用VADER进行情感分析。此外收集开发流程时间估计和半结构化访谈，结合定量分析与定性洞察。

Result: （1）技术能力：瑞典/荷兰工程师编码一致性强，卢旺达高级工程师在安全意识和系统设计上可媲美甚至超越欧洲同行，但初级工程师表现较弱，反映教育与培训的系统性差距；乌干达数据因语言障碍和AI使用问题被排除。（2）沟通风格：东非工程师反馈更支持性（compound score >0.5），欧洲工程师偏好直接批评（荷兰偏负向，瑞典中性）。（3）流程实践：欧洲工程师侧重前期设计与开发（约30%），东非工程师强调部署、验证和审查。（4）感知偏见：欧洲工程师系统性低估东非同行能力（常低于5/10），缺乏对东非生态系统的了解，依赖刻板印象；测量能力与感知能力之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了"外包至非洲意味着质量折损"的假设，指出区域间差异更多源于系统性因素和文化实践而非内在能力差异。北欧工程师强调前期设计和标准化，东非工程师注重协作验证、严格DevOps实践和支持性沟通——这些是互补的优势。然而，感知偏见（包括外部刻板印象和内化的等级观念）对公平协作构成持续风险。研究呼吁重新设计评估和反馈实践以减少偏见，并扩大样本、进行纵向追踪以验证发现。

Abstract: This paper examines fairness and accountability in global software development by focusing on how competence is assessed and valued across unequal regional contexts. We compare software engineers from East Africa (Rwanda and Uganda) and Northwestern Europe (Sweden and the Netherlands), regions that are increasingly connected but embedded in asymmetric technological, economic, and institutional structures. Despite the rapid growth of African technology ecosystems, empirical evidence on everyday engineering practice and evaluation in these contexts remains limited. We present findings from an on-site mixed-methods study with 48 software engineers across four countries. The study combines programming, system design, and code review tasks with semi-structured interviews. Our results reveal consistent gaps between measured performance and perceived competence. Senior engineers in Rwanda often performed at a level comparable to that of their European peers, yet European participants systematically underestimated the competence of East African engineers. We also observed differences in communication styles and organizational practices across regions, reflecting distinct but complementary ways of working.

</details>


### [78] [Hierarchical Fault Localization for Autonomous Driving Systems with Hypothesis Validation and Intent Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12598)
*Rui Zheng,Changwen Li,Yi Ji,Rongjie Yan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HINT，一种面向自动驾驶系统（ADS）的两阶段层次化故障定位框架。Phase I 通过多模态场景抽象与因果假设验证定位故障模块；Phase II 通过动态执行骨架（DES）剪枝与设计/实现意图一致性分析定位可疑代码，无需重仿真。在 Apollo 平台上针对真实与注入故障评估，达到 77.8% 端到端 Class@5 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ADS 测试技术能检测系统级失败或粗粒度归因到模块，但难以定位源码根因。ADS 故障通常表现为行为偏离而非代码异常，且复杂模块（如 Apollo Planning）含数万行代码与数百功能步骤，导致手动根因定位极为困难。以 Apollo 的 PR826 为例，故障源于 Prediction 模块中 FilterLaneSequences 函数的过滤条件错误，删除了有效的高置信度轨迹。

Method: 两阶段框架：
- **Phase I（模块诊断）**：将失败执行记录转换为时间对齐的执行轨迹，使用 STL 监控器定位违规区间，重建 BEV 交通场景并通过多模态 LLM 合成场景描述；然后通过假设生成与验证迭代过程（Algorithm 1），使用可复用的诊断 Skill 进行依赖与语义感知的一致性检查，并进行因果责任分析区分源故障与传播效应。
- **Phase II（代码定位）**：构建场景特定的动态执行骨架（DES）剪枝不可行代码路径；对每个任务采用双流意图不一致分析——设计侧（Architect 通过多阶段 RAG 重构 I_target）与实现侧（Diagnoser 通过信息增益驱动的语义树构建重构 I_impl）——以统一 schema ⟨Goal, Guard, Safety, Assumption⟩ 表示；通过可靠性校准的字段级比较计算怀疑得分，并将得分分配到函数节点。

Result: 在包含 63 个注入故障和 9 个真实 Apollo 缺陷的基准上：
- **模块诊断**：HINT 显著优于 ACAV 与 Oracle 基线（基于 MoDitector），准确识别源故障而非传播异常；
- **代码定位**：在 D_real 上达到 Class@5=88.9%、Func@10=88.9%，明显优于 AutoFL、LocAgent 与 Claude Code；
- **端到端**：D_real 达到 Class@5=77.8%、Func@10=77.8%，优于 Claude Code + Skills；
- **消融实验**：移除 Scenario Abstraction 导致 Phase I 准确率从 88.9% 降至 33.3% 并大量超时；移除 DES 或 Inconsistency Analysis 均显著降低代码定位准确率；
- **跨 LLM 骨干鲁棒性**：在 Haiku 4.5、Sonnet 4.5、GPT-5.4、GLM-5 上均保持稳定，Class@5 在 66.7%–77.8% 之间。

Conclusion: HINT 是首个将 ADS 系统级失败直接关联到工业级代码库源码根因的自动化框架，通过假设验证与意图分析桥接行为异常与实现逻辑。其增益来源于结构化诊断流程本身，而非特定 LLM 骨干或工件访问。框架可推广至其他模块化系统（如同为模块化的 Autoware），前提是具备相应的运行时证据、设计文档与源码。局限在于：(1) 假设单主导故障模块，可能简化多模块级联；(2) 当前未覆盖 Perception 模块；(3) Skill 模板针对 Apollo 定制，迁移到其他平台需适配。

Abstract: Comprehensive testing is essential for the safety and reliability of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS). Existing techniques can detect system-level failures or attribute them to coarse-grained modules, but they often fall short of localizing the root cause in source code. As a result, debugging remains labor-intensive, requiring developers to connect behavioral violations with complex implementation logic. To address this gap, we present HINT, a two-phase framework for hierarchical ADS fault localization based on hypothesis validation and intent analysis. In Phase I, HINT transforms failure-triggering execution recordings into multi-modal abstractions and uses causal reasoning to identify the responsible module. In Phase II, it reconstructs design-side intent and implementation-side behavior, then localizes suspicious code through reliability-aware consistency checking, without costly re-simulation. We evaluate HINT on Apollo across diverse failure modes and modules. The results show that HINT achieves the strongest overall performance across module-level diagnosis and code-level localization metrics, with 77.8% end-to-end Class@5 accuracy on real-world bugs.

</details>


### [79] [Toward Localizing and Repairing Bias in Transformer Attention Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12863)
*Sigma Jahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ROBIN，一种白盒、注意力头级别的公平性调试方法，通过对公平性探针的敏感度排序定位关键注意力头，并在推理时仅移除这些头输出中的低维偏见子空间，而非整头置零。初步实验表明，该方法在四个预训练Transformer模型上均能降低WinoBias差距，同时比整头置零更好地保持语言建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平性测试与修复方法多在输入输出层面或通过重训练进行干预，难以定位模型内部产生偏见的具体组件。近期研究表明偏见行为可能集中在少量注意力头中，但如何定位这些头以及如何修改它们仍缺乏系统性方法。整头置零的简单干预可能误删与一般语言功能相关的有用信息，因此需要一种既能定位又能精细修复的推理时干预手段。

Method: ROBIN分两阶段运行。第一阶段利用公平性探针文本对（仅在受保护属性上不同），计算每个注意力头对损失的梯度范数，结合均值梯度评分和平方梯度评分（源自经验Fisher重要性度量），经标准化求和后排序得到top-k个偏见敏感头。第二阶段对选中的每个头，利用WinoBias对构建差分向量矩阵，通过右奇异值分解保留前r个方向构成偏见子空间V_h；推理时从头输出x_h中减去其在V_h上的投影（x_h' = x_h - V_h^⊤ V_h x_h），保留与偏见正交的部分。实验在DistilBERT、BERT、DistilGPT-2、GPT-2上以k∈{1,2,3,5,8,10,15,20}、r=4进行评估，对比Gradient和SquaredGrad两种整头置零方法。

Result: 在k=20时，ROBIN在四个模型上均降低了WinoBias差距（BERT从45.97降至19.14为最大降幅），同时语言建模损失最多仅增加7.2%（BERT甚至略低于基线）。相比之下，整头置零方法在GPT-2上使语言建模损失增加高达8倍。推理延迟方面，ROBIN在k=20时增加7%~11%开销，随k近似线性增长。StereoSet评分结果不一致，仅作为辅助参考。三种排序方法间top-20重叠15-20个头，Spearman相关系数>0.9，表明关键差异来自修改方式而非头选择。

Conclusion: ROBIN将偏见修复分解为头定位与组件级修改两个独立问题，证明了子空间移除比整头置零在保持模型质量方面更优。论文定位为初步试点研究，未来工作将评估更大模型、更广泛属性、不相交数据划分、生成输出以及下游任务公平性，并研究子空间维度r的选择与迁移性。该方法提供候选定位和可测量的干预，而非因果解释或完整公平性评估。

Abstract: Transformer language models are increasingly used as software components, yet biased outputs remain difficult to localize and repair inside the model. Existing fairness testing and repair methods largely operate at the input-output or retraining level, while recent work suggests that bias-related behavior can concentrate in a small set of attention heads. This paper studies whether attention heads can be localized and repaired through a targeted inference-time intervention. We introduce ROBIN, a white-box head-level fairness debugging method that ranks attention heads using sensitivity to fairness probes and removes a small bias subspace from selected head outputs. In a four-model pilot study, ROBIN reduces the measured WinoBias gap across all models while preserving language-modeling quality better than whole-head zeroing. These preliminary results suggest that head-level bias repair should consider not only which heads are selected, but also how selected heads are modified.

</details>


### [80] [Deep4ge: DNN Training Trajectories for Fault Detection and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12868)
*Sigma Jahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Deep4ge 是一个面向 DNN 训练过程故障检测与诊断的公开基准数据集，包含 14,227 次带标签的逐 epoch 训练轨迹（9,845 次故障运行 + 4,382 次正确基线），源自 59 个 Stack Overflow 上经适配的 TensorFlow/Keras 程序，通过 27 种源码级突变算子注入 7 类已知故障，每个 epoch 记录 4 项评估指标与 26 项动态特征（权重、梯度、激活、趋势、学习率、硬件使用等），支持二分类故障检测、多分类故障诊断及基于部分训练轨迹的早期故障预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN 系统常因细微实现缺陷导致训练过程出现静默症状（如梯度消失/爆炸、优化不稳定、泛化差），传统调试难以发现。研究界已有的 DNN 调试数据集存在程序数量有限、缺乏逐 epoch 轨迹、不可复用、缺乏清晰的故障标注与特征说明等问题，限制了训练时故障的系统化研究与可重复评估。

Method: （1）从 Stack Overflow 筛选 59 个 TensorFlow/Keras DNN 程序（覆盖 FNN、CNN、RNN），手工修复并固定随机种子以确保可重复；（2）基于 27 个源码级突变算子（继承自 DeepCrime 并新增层相关算子）注入 7 类故障（超参数、损失函数、权重初始化、层结构、优化器、激活函数、正则化）；（3）通过 AST 重写应用突变并插入日志回调，每个 epoch 记录 4 项指标 + 26 项动态特征；（4）采用基于 Cohen's d（β=0.5）与 GLM p 值（α=0.05）、n=15 次重训的"kill 准则"筛选有效突变，丢弃等价突变；（5）提供 5 折组感知交叉验证（按 SO 程序分组），对比 final-epoch 与 trajectory 两种特征表示，在 LR 与 RF 模型上评估 F1、Macro-F1、Balanced Accuracy、MCC 等指标。

Result: 数据集共 14,227 次训练运行、719,560 条 epoch 记录（中位 50 epoch/次），各架构突变得分 FNN=0.85、CNN=0.87、RNN=0.83；不同故障类别的训练损失轨迹呈现明显分组（Activation 不收敛、Loss 收敛到不同数值尺度、超参数类约 7 epoch 恢复、权重初始化约 13 epoch 恢复、层/优化/正则类难区分）。基线实验显示：trajectory 特征显著优于 final-epoch——RF 检测 MCC 从 0.150 提升至 0.227（AUROC 0.649→0.716，AUPRC 0.804→0.842），诊断 Macro-F1 从 0.355 提升至 0.381、MCC 从 0.346 提升至 0.401；按类别 Weight 类诊断 F1=0.700 最高，Hyperparameter/Loss 中等（0.52 左右），Activation=0.000、Optimization=0.075 最难，混淆矩阵显示 Activation 易误判为 Optimization、Optimization 易误判为 Layer；跨架构迁移检测 MCC 介于 0.198–0.218 之间，损失较小但 RNN 稍大；单 epoch 早期检测 MCC 范围 0.14–0.19，均低于全轨迹模型。

Conclusion: Deep4ge 作为首个将适配的 DNN 源码、突变派生故障类别与逐 epoch 训练轨迹相链接的公开基准，证明了利用完整训练轨迹（而非仅 final-epoch）可显著提升故障检测与诊断性能；该数据集及其突变框架已开源（CC BY 4.0/MIT，Zenodo DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.20337241），支持可重复研究、跨架构分析、特征重要性研究与未来扩展，但其适用范围限于 TF/Keras 的 FNN/CNN/RNN（不含 Transformer），且突变派生故障不能完全代表真实工业故障分布，正确基线仅验证可执行性而非语义正确性。

Abstract: Deep learning systems often fail due to subtle implementation faults that alter training behavior. Recent work has studied how to detect and diagnose such failures from changes observed across training epochs. However, the software engineering community still lacks a public dataset of per-epoch training runs with documented fault history, feature extraction details, and clear reuse support for fault detection and diagnosis tasks. We present Deep4ge, a controlled benchmark of 14,227 training runs generated from 59 adapted TensorFlow/Keras deep neural network (DNN) programs collected from Stack Overflow. We generated faulty variants using 27 source-code transformations that introduce known faults across seven categories. The dataset contains 9,845 faulty runs and 4,382 correct baseline runs. For each run, we record 4 evaluation metrics and 26 features that measure training behavior at every epoch. These features capture weights, gradients, activations, accuracy and loss trends, learning rate, and hardware use. Deep4ge supports binary fault detection, multi-class fault diagnosis, and early fault prediction from partial training runs. We release the dataset and fault-injection framework at this https URL .

</details>


### [81] [Salesforce Messaging Architecture: Platform Events, Async Sends, and Multi-Tenancy at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12943)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"CRM 原生"的企业消息集成架构范式,主张将消息核心直接构建在 Salesforce CRM 内部(而非作为外部连接器),以 Twilio for Salesforce 生产级托管包为案例,系统评估了六大数据对象模型、平台事件驱动的入站解耦、调用出站治理限额的自链式异步作业、复合键独立的同意状态模型、双路径状态调和以及多租户安全抽象等核心设计模式与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统企业消息集成采用"连接器"模型,消息平台为权威数据源,CRM 仅接收镜像同步,导致结构性延迟、脆弱的同步作业和最终一致性窗口。当 CRM 本身也是业务人员推理客户关系的主界面时,这种跨系统桥接成为工程负担。本文质疑这一默认假设,主张在企业级多租户场景下倒转集成边界,让 CRM 成为唯一的真相源,外部消息网关退化为纯传输层。

Method: 方法上以一个部署在医疗、销售运营、现场服务与客户支持等多行业独立企业组织中的 Twilio for Salesforce 一代托管包为生产实证基础,围绕六大核心对象(Message__c、Campaign_Send__c、Opt_In__c、配置元数据、服务注册表、Team)设计关系模型;出站路径采用"先写盘、后异步 HTTP 调出"两阶段模式,并通过自链式 Queueable 作业(以 95 次调出为切片阈值)规避单事务 100 次调出治理上限,同时嵌入队列拒绝时的 20 秒兜底调度器;入站路径通过非认证 REST 控制器 + 平台事件总线实现 HMAC 校验后 50ms 内返回 202,业务处理完全异步;使用基于批量 SOQL 的集式映射(Map<String,Id>)解决 200 条事件块的多态关联解析与 N+1 问题;Opt-In 状态以"E.164+关键字"复合键独立对象建模,保证幂等;状态同步采用 Webhook 实时通道 + 定时轮询对账的双路径模型,以不可变外部 SID 实现幂等调和;多租户层封装统一 DML 代理、Package 旁路框架,并以纯加性的托管包模式管理 schema 永久性。

Result: 该架构在生产环境中验证了若干关键效果:出站消息在网络/网关故障下仍保留完整本地审计记录,支持可追溯的自动重试;WebSocket/REST 入站响应时间被压缩到 50ms 以下,完全避开网关超时触发的重试风暴与重复写入;集式批量解析在 200 条事件块内仅需对每类实体执行单次 SOQL,消除 N+1 异常;Opt-In 复合键使重复 STOP 命令的幂等处理无需去重逻辑;双路径状态验证在 Webhook 失效时由轮询对账在下个周期捕获漂移,两者同写同一 SID 时无副作用;统一 DML 框架使 FLS/CRUD 权限校验集中于单一切入点;但代价是遗留的通用 Parent ID 字段因托管包 schema 不可删除而成为永久性架构负债。

Conclusion: 论文结论认为,CRM 原生架构消除了复杂外部 ETL,直接复用原生报表、流程构建器与工作流自动化,并具备与 Agentforce 等 AI 编排平台的即插即用兼容性;但要求工程团队接受事务治理限额、复杂异步调试路径与 schema 永久性等约束,主张通过平台事件 Webhook、自链式作业、独立账本式 Opt-In 模型等系统化模式在企业环境中构建高可靠、高容量的通信网络;未来工作将把"治理动作"模式扩展到 LLM 驱动的 AI Agent 平台,要求在 Agent 触发场景下保留集中式 DML/FLS 强制与多租户隔离保障。

Abstract: Most enterprise messaging integrations function as external connectors. They reside outside the Customer Relationship Management (CRM) platform, receive webhooks, write peripheral logs, and consider the process integrated. Consequently, messages are isolated within the messaging platform while CRM records remain strictly inside the CRM, bridged only by fragile sync jobs, rigid field mappings, and eventual consistency windows. This paper outlines an alternative architectural paradigm: constructing the entire messaging core natively inside the CRM. Within this model, every message is treated as a native CRM record,every outbound path is a platform-native transaction, and all state parameters - including delivery status, opt-in metrics, conversation history, and record ownership - are stored as standard database objects. This structural native alignment allows the system to utilize standard reporting pipelines, operate inside native workflow builders, and trigger platform automation seamlessly. Drawing from a production managed package deployed across independent enterprise organizations spanning healthcare, sales operations, field services, and customer support, this study evaluates the core design patterns, platform scaling mechanics, multi-tenant decoupling constraints, and the boundaries of the platform-native architectural design.

</details>


### [82] [Software Supply Chains are Dead: Use-Case-Oriented Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13021)
*Tanmay Singla,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"用例导向再生"(use-case-oriented regeneration)作为一种新的软件采购范式，利用生成式AI代理仅合成下游仓库实际使用的依赖功能片段，从而将软件供应链信任从外部生态转移到本地验证。在180个仓库-依赖对的评估中，该方法在保持99.8%行为一致性的同时，将导出API表面积减少93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件开发过度依赖第三方包，但下游仓库通常仅使用依赖的一小部分功能，由此带来供应链攻击、传递性依赖、维护负担等成本。生成式AI降低了本地实现的成本，使得"重新实现窄功能切片"成为可能，挑战了传统的"构建vs复用"权衡。

Method: 提出用例导向再生范式：以仓库-依赖对(repositry-dependency pair)为基本分析单元，通过智能体工作流识别仓库对依赖的调用上下文，生成仅覆盖被使用行为的本地实现，并通过仓库原有验证构件(测试、构建、lint)进行行为验证。在9个JavaScript/TypeScript依赖(共180对)上使用Claude Opus智能体进行实验评估，从可行性(RQ1)、行为保持(RQ2)、规模与足迹缩减(RQ3)三个维度测量。

Result: 180对中166对实现完美行为保持(99.8%通过率)；生成的实现平均减少93.1%的导出API表面积；简单的实用类库(nanoid、zod)全部成功，复杂框架类(lodash、express、axios)出现语义边缘情况、类身份匹配、深度框架集成等失败模式；共14对失败。论文明确指出该方法仅保证"仓库可观察等价性"，而非全语义等价。

Conclusion: 用例导向再生是可行的软件采购策略，尤其适用于窄、稳定、经过良好测试的依赖使用场景。但应作为"辅助软件工程工作流"而非全自动依赖移除机制。未来的研究议程包括覆盖引导验证、差分测试、再生来源元数据、刷新实践以及AI辅助风险评估。整体愿景是将软件供应链从僵化的上游采用转向灵活的、可验证的、仓库特定的代码生成。

Abstract: Modern software development relies on an increasingly doubtful premise: that the up-front implementation savings from adopting a dependency outweighs the maintenance costs. Two changes are reshaping the build-vs.-reuse calculus: software supply chain attacks have raised the cost of external reliance, while generative AI has lowered the cost of local implementation. We envision use-case-oriented regeneration as a new software sourcing paradigm that shifts the supply chain from external trust to local verification. We evaluate an agentic workflow that synthesizes only the specific slice of dependency functionality that a repository exercises. Our measurements across 180 repository-dependency pairs suggest that this approach is feasible: the replacements preserve 99.8% of repository-observed behavior across baseline validation checks and reduce the exported API surface by 93%. Software sourcing may evolve toward verifiable repository-specific code synthesis, especially when the required functionality is narrow, stable, and well tested.

</details>

### [83] [So Many Opinions, So Many LLMs: Comparing Large Language Models to Traditional Machine Learning for Open- Ended Survey Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11890)
*Abdullah Akinde,Mariam Akinde,Rasheedat Emiola,Ahmed Akinsola*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种大语言模型（LLMs）——包括OpenAI的GPT系列、Twitter-roBERTa-base模型以及Meta的LLaMA——在分析NSSE（全国学生参与度调查）开放式问卷回答中的表现，并与传统机器学习模型在情感分析和主题分类任务上进行对比，评估其模型一致性、分类准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式问卷调查能提供宝贵洞察，但大规模分析极为困难。传统机器学习方法已被用于此类任务，但大语言模型的快速发展为大规模定性分析提供了新可能。本文旨在探究当前LLMs在理解与分析开放式调查数据方面的能力与局限，填补LLMs在教育调查数据分析领域的研究空白。

Method: 研究比较了多类模型的性能：(1) 大语言模型——OpenAI的GPT系列、Twitter-roBERTa-base模型、Meta的LLaMA；(2) 传统机器学习模型（来自先前研究）。评估任务涵盖情感分析与主题分类。评价指标包括三个方面：模型间一致性（model agreement）、分类准确性（classification accuracy）以及推理过程的可解释性（interpretability of reasoning）。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 在分类准确性方面，当前LLMs普遍优于传统机器学习模型，尤其在理解学生回答中复杂的情绪与主题模式上表现突出；(2) 在可解释性与一致性方面，不同LLMs之间差异显著，部分模型在阐明预测依据和应用类别边界时缺乏明确性与一致性；(3) LLMs虽具备更强的预测能力，但在一致性和可解释性方面存在明显短板。

Conclusion: LLMs在大规模定性研究中具有显著优势（更高的分类准确率），但也面临一致性与可解释性的权衡问题。研究为希望在自动化与解释严谨性之间取得平衡的研究者提供了实用建议，强调在选择LLMs用于定性分析时需审慎考虑其推理透明度与类别边界稳定性。

Abstract: Open-ended surveys offer valuable insights, but they are notoriously difficult to analyze at scale. Building on previous work that employed traditional machine learning to classify text ("So Many Responses, So Little Time: A Machine-Learning Approach to Analyzing Open-Ended Survey Data") [1], this study investigates how different large language models (LLMs) understand and analyze NSSE open-ended survey responses. We focus on several cutting-edge LLMSs-OpenAI's GPT series, Twitter-roBERTa-base model, and Meta's LLaMA-and compare their performance to the previous machine learning models in tasks like sentiment analysis and thematic classification. Our research analysis assesses model agreement, classification accuracy, and interpretability of reasoning. The findings reveal that current LLMs routinely beat classic machine learning models in classification accuracy, particularly in understanding complex mood and theme patterns in student replies. While LLMs have superior accuracy, they differ greatly in how explicitly and consistently they justify their predictions and apply category boundaries. These distinctions highlight crucial trade-offs when using LLMs for qualitative analysis: increased predictive strength comes with issues in consistency and explainability. Our findings illustrate the benefits and drawbacks of utilizing various LLMs for large-scale qualitative research, and we provide practical advice for researchers looking to balance automation and interpretive rigor.

</details>


### [84] [A Longitudinal Analysis of Public Discourse on AI Ethics in Education Using Twitter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12295)
*Akriti Bagale,Nafisa Mehjabin,Ali Ünlü,Aditya Johri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对2019-2024年Twitter/X平台上关于AI教育伦理的公众讨论进行了为期五年的纵向分析，使用BERT主题建模和SetFit情感分析追踪了以ChatGPT发布为分水岭的话语演变，发现公众态度总体积极务实，但近期对学术诚信和生成式AI的担忧日益突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和生成式AI快速融入教育领域，理解公众如何感知和讨论教育中的AI伦理问题对于教育者、机构和政策制定者推动负责任、公平的AI整合至关重要。社交媒体作为大规模、实时公共讨论的场所，为捕捉公众舆论演变提供了独特视角。

Method: 采用纵向研究设计，分析2019-2024年Twitter/X平台上五年间关于AI教育伦理的讨论数据。利用BERT-based主题建模识别主导议题，使用SetFit情感分析方法追踪情感随时间的演变趋势，特别关注ChatGPT的发布作为话语转变的关键节点。

Result: 研究发现：(1)在整个观察期内，公众话语以积极情感为主流；(2)消极情感集中于特定的伦理争议事件；(3)近期，围绕学术诚信和生成式AI更广泛影响的焦虑已成为讨论的主导主题；(4)公众话语并非两极分化的对立辩论，而是呈现出务实且总体上接纳AI整合的态度，但同时伴随着对伦理监督和机构问责制的日益强烈的呼声。

Conclusion: 该研究为教育者、机构和政策制定者提供了关于公众对AI教育伦理期望的实证依据，有助于制定负责任、透明和公平的AI教育整合策略。公共话语的务实性和建设性表明，推动AI在教育中的合理应用具有广泛的公众基础，但需建立相应的伦理治理框架来应对日益增长的担忧。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) and generative AI (GenAI) into education presents significant opportunities to enhance teaching and learning, while raising ethical concerns about the responsible use of these technologies in educational settings. Understanding how the public perceives and debates these issues is increasingly important for educators, institutions, and policymakers seeking to integrate AI responsibly and equitably. Social media platforms, where such debates unfold frequently and at scale, offer a valuable lens for capturing large-scale, real-time public reactions to key developments as they emerge. In this study, we analyse five years (2019-2024) of discourse on Twitter (now X) to trace the evolving public conversation around AI ethics in education, paying particular attention to the release of ChatGPT as a pivotal moment that reshaped the nature and tone of that discourse. Using BERT-based topic modelling and SetFit sentiment analysis to identify dominant themes and track sentiment over time, we find that the discourse has been predominantly positive across the observation period, with negative sentiment concentrated around specific ethical controversies. More recently, anxieties about academic integrity and the broader implications of generative AI have come to dominate the conversation. Rather than reflecting a polarized debate, public discourse appears pragmatic and largely receptive to AI integration, though accompanied by growing calls for ethical oversight and institutional accountability. By providing a longitudinal account of public sentiment surrounding AI ethics in education, this study informs educators, institutions, and policymakers an empirically grounded understanding of public expectations, informing the development of responsible, transparent, and equitable approaches to AI integration across educational contexts.

</details>


### [85] [A Comparative Analysis of Institutional and Course Generative AI Policies within Higher Education: Implications for Instruction in Computing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12296)
*Amrita Ganguly,Aditya Johri,Nora McDonald,Areej Ali,Umama Dewan,Aayushi Hingle Collier*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文比较了美国研究型高校在生成式AI（GenAI）使用上的机构层面指导与课程层面实际采用情况之间的差异，发现机构指导更倾向于鼓励使用，但在计算机科学课程中实际采纳仍较为保守，并提出了面向教师的GenAI采纳框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ChatGPT等生成式AI工具在高等教育中的广泛使用，高校已发布相关指导政策。计算机科学课程中GenAI的采用率尤其高，对学生学习影响显著，但目前缺乏对机构指导与课程实际采纳之间的比较分析。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用二次分析法（secondary analysis），对美国研究密集型高校的机构层面指导文件和计算机科学课程大纲（syllabi）中的相关指南进行比较分析。

Result: 研究发现机构层面的指导政策总体上更倾向于支持（pro-use）GenAI的使用，但在课程层面的实际采纳仍然谨慎保守（guarded），存在机构指导与课程实施之间的明显落差。

Conclusion: 文章讨论了该落差所带来的影响，并提出了一个以教师为中心的框架（instructor-centered framework），用以指导未来GenAI在高等教育中的合理采纳。

Abstract: With the increased use of generative AI (GenAI) applications such as ChatGPT, higher education institutions (HEIs) have released a range of guidelines and policies to direct adoption within their institutions. In computer science (CS) courses GenAI adoption is especially high and the implications for student learning are significant. At the same time, instructors have also been forced to address the use of GenAI as students have started to use it for a range of functions. Currently, comparative analysis of guidance provided by institutions and its uptake in instruction is lacking. In this paper we bridge this gap by comparing institutional and computing course level guidance to better understand this terrain. We utilize secondary analysis of institutional and course syllabi guidelines from higher education institutions in the U.S. classified as research-intensive. Our findings reveal that although institutional guidance is more pro-use, at the course-level the uptake is still guarded. We discuss the implications and propose an instructor-centered framework to guide future adoption of GenAI.

</details>


### [86] [AgentSociety 2: An Integrated Research Environment for Executable Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11895)
*Jinghua Piao,Jun Zhang,Haoyu Huang,Keming Zhang,Jing Yi Wang,Songwei Li,Boyuan Sun,Jiayi Chang,Fengli Xu,Chunyan Wang,Fang Zhang,Ke Rong,Jun Su,Tianguang Meng,Yi Liu,Qingguo Meng,Yu Wang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: AgentSociety 2 是一个集成式社交科学研究环境，将 LLM 智能体同时扮演"AI 社会科学家"（协调文献综述、假设生成、实验设计、模拟执行、结果解读与论文撰写）和"硅基参与者"（在可配置社交环境中生成行为响应）两种角色，实现了从假设到可审计的智能体行为、环境规则、干预与测量的端到端工作流程，支持微观、中观、宏观三个层级的社会科学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 科学家系统要么只能辅助孤立的科研任务，要么只能将智能体作为实验对象进行模拟，导致研究工作流与模拟社会相脱节。社会科学的研究对象是涉及情境化参与者、互动情境、干预和结果的综合性社会过程，缺乏一个将研究流程与模拟社会耦合起来的集成化、可控的执行环境。

Method: 提出 AgentSociety 2 集成研究环境，采用双角色设计：1) AI 社会科学家智能体——负责文献 grounding、假设生成、实验设计、模拟执行、结果解读与论文草稿撰写；2) 硅基参与者智能体——在可配置社交环境中生成行为响应。通过优化的智能体-环境交互，支持七大示例研究，覆盖微观社会实验室实验、中观社交媒体动力学与宏观城市场景，并保留人类研究者的顶层决策权（human-in-the-loop）。

Result: 在七个跨层级示例研究中，该系统能够：1) 支持多样化的学科问题；2) 复现先前研究中的主要定性模式；3) 识别具有信息价值的偏离现象；4) 通过优化的智能体-环境交互实现大规模模拟。

Conclusion: AgentSociety 2 在保留人类研究者高层级控制权的同时，将程序性编排委托给智能体系统，为下一代计算社会科学提供了人机协作且可控的基础设施，在可扩展的计算社会实验与 AI 驱动的社会治理平台方面具有更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: AI scientist systems are beginning to automate parts of scientific research, but social science poses a distinct challenge: its objects of inquiry are not merely datasets or laboratory protocols, but integrated social processes involving situated participants, interaction contexts, interventions, and outcomes. Yet a critical link is missing: existing systems either assist isolated research tasks or simulate agents as experimental subjects, leaving the research workflow and simulated society decoupled. Here we introduce AgentSociety 2, an Integrated Research Environment for executable social science. It couples two roles of LLM agents in the same runtime: AI social scientists that coordinate literature grounding, hypothesis generation, experiment design, simulation execution, result interpretation, and manuscript drafting; and silicon participants that generate behavioral responses within configurable social environments. This dual-role design turns hypotheses into auditable agent behaviors, environment rules, interventions, and measurements, thereby supporting an end-to-end workflow. Across seven illustrative studies spanning micro-level social-science laboratory experiments, meso-level dynamics in social media, and macro-level urban scenarios, we demonstrate its capacity to support diverse disciplinary questions, reproduce major qualitative patterns from prior studies, identify informative deviations, and enable large-scale simulations through optimized agent-environment interactions. By preserving human researchers' high-level agency while delegating procedural orchestration to agentic systems, it provides a human-in-the-loop and controllable infrastructure for next-generation computational social science, with broader applications in scalable computational social experimentation and AI-enabled social governance platforms.

</details>


### [87] [Underwriting the Agent Economy: The Blueprint for an AI Insurance Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11999)
*Cristian Trout,Sanmi Koyejo,Sasha Romanosky,Giorgio Ripamonti,Lynn Thompson,Desiree Spain,Alex Taylor,Kevin Casey,Stephen Casper,Matthew Botvinick,Sean McGregor,Miles Brundage,A. Feder Cooper,Patricia Paskov,Adrien Ecoffet,Ben Bucknall,Kevin Wei,Markus Anderljung,Lukasz Szpruch,Bri Treece,Tom Zick,Gabriel Weil,Ugur Ozer,Kevin Kalinich,Jesus Gonzalez,Vitaly Baranov,Moran Koren,Guy Laban,Gil Arazi,Henri Winand,Derek Blum,Toby Clowes,Adam Kleinman,Anita Srinivasan,Tom Fehring,Rune Kvist,Rajiv Dattani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该报告探讨了人工智能(AI)智能体经济所带来的新型风险与保险缺口问题，论证了通过行业协同构建AI保险基础设施的可行性，并提出了涵盖八大组件的保险栈框架以及应对AI灾难性风险的专门机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体经济预计到2030年将处理数万亿美元交易，保险公司面临的风险敞口在现有保险条款中基本未被定价。现有保险既存在"沉默覆盖"(silent coverage)问题，越来越多排除条款又使保障不再适用，且AI能力发展快于可靠性，事故严重性上升；少数基础模型提供商集中度高，存在相关性损失风险；传统精算建模难以跟上AI的快速演进。因此亟需重新设计针对AI智能体的保险产品与基础设施。

Method: 报告借鉴历史上成功的保险与风险管理先例(如美国保险商实验室UL、闭锁索赔项目Closed Claims Project等)，提出由八个相互关联组件构成的AI保险栈(AI Insurance Stack)：(1)事故数据收集；(2)灾难建模；(3)标准制定；(4)合同设计；(5)风险选择；(6)定价；(7)监测；(8)理赔管理。同时对前沿AI导致的灾难性风险(AI CAT)，包括CBRN(化学、生物、放射、核)、关键基础设施崩溃及失控场景，提出专项应对工具。

Result: 报告论证认为：以十亿美元为限额的肯定式AI保险(affirmative AI coverage)在2030年前是可实现的，但前提是开展全行业范围的协调；通过落地八组件保险栈，保险公司能够可持续、大规模地承保与管理AI智能体风险；对于AI CAT尾部风险，则需构建专用工具，潜在方案包括前沿模型开发者互助池(mutual)、巨灾债券(bonds)、定制化责任机制以及政府最终担保。

Conclusion: 该报告的结论是：要使保险市场适配AI智能体经济的规模与速度，行业必须主动构建新型基础设施而非依赖传统保险条款的延伸；同时AI灾难性风险已超出商业保险的承保能力边界，迫切需要公私合作的制度创新，以应对AI可能引发的极端尾部事件。

Abstract: From maritime trade to commercial nuclear power, insurance has been the enabler of major economic and technological developments by pricing risk, limiting downside, and spreading best practices. The emerging AI agent economy, projected to handle trillions of dollars in transactions by 2030, looks to be the next such development. Yet insurers' exposure to AI agent risk currently sits largely unpriced across existing insurance lines; between this silent coverage and growing exclusions, coverage is not fit for purpose. Furthermore, insurability is trending the wrong way: AI agent capabilities appear to be outpacing reliability, leading to rising incident severity; concentration among a few foundation model providers threatens correlated losses; and traditional actuarial modeling will struggle to keep pace with a technology evolving as rapidly as frontier AI. This report argues that affirmative AI coverage with limits in the billions is achievable by 2030, but only with industry-wide coordination. Drawing on successful historical precedents such as Underwriters Laboratories, the Closed Claims Project, and others, we lay out an eight-component AI insurance stack spanning incident data collection, catastrophe modeling, standards, contract design, risk selection, pricing, monitoring, and claims management. Building out this infrastructure is what will enable insurers to cover and manage AI agent risk sustainably and at scale. Finally, we discuss coverage for catastrophic risk from frontier AI ("AI CAT"), including CBRN, critical infrastructure collapse, and loss of control scenarios. Addressing these tail risks will require purpose-built instruments, potentially including a frontier model developer mutual, catastrophe bonds, bespoke liability regimes, and government backstops.

</details>


### [88] [It is not enough to give your moderation rules to ChatGPT: Policy-as-Prompt Moderation and Its Potential Impacts on Community Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12149)
*Anna Neumann,Jasmin Wyss,Ivy Turk,Rebekah Overdorf*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在内容审核中使用"策略即提示"（policy-as-prompt）方法，即将审核策略编写为自然语言提示并输入大语言模型（LLM），分析了该方法在技术和社区治理层面的局限性、风险与危害，最终认为仅依靠编写提示不足以确保有意义的社区治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容审核实践正从中心化的人工专家审核转向依赖志愿者的社区审核模式，LLM被视为减轻审核负担（时间、心理健康、准确性）的潜在工具。然而，将审核政策简单编码为提示输入LLM这一做法在技术和治理层面存在隐患，可能削弱社区自治、逃避机制与问责机制，因此需要系统审视其影响。

Method: 论文采用概念分析与论证方法：1）梳理内容审核领域从中心化到去中心化的演变；2）将内容审核定位为社区治理问题而非单纯的规则应用；3）解析policy-as-prompt的技术机制（包括提示栈的层级优先级、提示注入绕过、防护栏不足等）；4）分别从中心化与去中心化两种审核范式追踪其影响；5）从技术评估、治理结构、问责机制三个维度提出设计考量。

Result: 研究发现：1）系统提示无法可靠保证LLM的对齐、性能与稳健性；2）提示栈的层级结构意味着moderation指令可能受高优先级提示制约；3）在中心化审核中，政策从"翻译给人类"变为"翻译给AI"，损失了人类审核员的判断力和协商机制；4）在去中心化社区中，自治反馈循环被削弱，规则可能为适应AI能力而被改写，社群规范受技术能力反向塑造；5）LLM无法承担问责责任，因此应仅作为辅助或补充而非替代人类决策。

Conclusion: 政策即提示方法不应单独用于内容审核。审核高度依赖语言的流动性、上下文、时间和意图，需要审议、申诉和社区价值观的语境化解读等意义构建（sense-making）操作。将这些机制外包给LLM会产生深远影响，因为LLM的"决策"基于过去语言及其上下文的扭曲镜像，而非当前特定语境中的语言使用。展望未来，必须承认AI只能向后看，这既证明也要求内容审核中必须保留人类驱动的流程。

Abstract: Content moderation practices and governance paradigms are changing rapidly, as fewer human moderators are deployed as `experts' by social media companies in a centralized manner. Instead, the companies are focusing more on community approaches, relying on volunteers to provide accurate information and make correct decisions. In decentralized moderation, communities have always relied on volunteers, updated community guidelines, and internal discussions thereof. For both content moderation paradigms, Artificial Intelligence (AI) seems like it could help ease moderation burdens of time, mental health, and accuracy. One possible way to operationalize AI in content moderation is a `policy-as-prompt'' approach, where the policy is formulated as a natural-language prompt and then passed to a large language model (LLM). This model then aids in moderation tasks. In this paper, we briefly lay out the technical and governance properties of this approach, and argue that its limitations lead to specific risks and harms that have to be addressed. Towards alleviating them, we lay out multiple considerations towards more effective prompt governance, but ultimately find that writing prompts alone is not appropriate for ensuring meaningful community governance.

</details>


### [89] [A Semi-Automated System for Generating Dialogue-Based TTS Lessons Using Large Language Models: An Exploratory Study of Educational Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12235)
*Gendo Kumoi,Fumie Watanabe,Tota Suko,Takashi Ishida,Yuko Kuma,Manabu Kobayashi,Shigeichi Hirasawa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）和文本转语音（TTS）技术的半自动化对话式课程生成系统，并通过245名高一学生的准实验探索性地验证了其教育潜力。该系统采用三阶段人在回路（human-in-the-loop）工作流（LLM生成幻灯片/解说脚本、教师审核、音视频自动整合），并基于认知学徒理论（cognitive apprenticeship theory）创新性地提出了一种"专家-新手"对话式解说的自动生成方法。实验表明：TTS音频并未显著降低学习体验（RQ1）；对话式TTS在理解力、可解释性和认知参与度上显著优于单人TTS，但在音频自然度上存在权衡（RQ2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在翻转课堂等教学模式中，高质量课前视频制作成本高、技术门槛高；现有全自动生成方案缺乏教学细微差别且存在LLM幻觉风险；TTS技术在教育中的可接受性以及对话式教学设计在TTS环境中的效果尚缺乏实证依据。

Method: 系统设计：1）基于Claude 3.5 Sonnet + Marp生成结构化幻灯片；2）生成TTS优化的解说脚本（提供单人模式和"专家-新手"对话模式，对话模式采用七三语量比、五阶段对话模式、五类提问策略）；3）使用Gemini TTS API合成语音（Enceladus为专家、Autonoe为新手），通过MoviePy合成视频。

实验设计：245名高一学生顺序体验三种课程形式（教师授课视频→单人TTS→对话TTS），内容不同。采用两阶段分析：①受试者内Friedman检验+TOST等价性检验（N≤183）；②重复横断面Mann-Whitney U检验（N=229/206），并以先验知识为协变量进行比例优势模型补充分析。评价框架融合ARCS模型、认知负荷理论与学习参与理论。

Result: RQ1（教师语音vs TTS）：三核心指标（理解、集中、整体评价）Friedman检验无显著差异（p>.14），TOST等价性检验（Δ=±0.5）全部落入等价区间，表明TTS音频不显著降低学习体验。

RQ2（单人TTS vs 对话TTS）：
- 对话TTS显著优势：理解（p=.006, q=.025, r=.130）、可解释性（p=.004, q=.021, r=.152）、时间价值感（p=.013, q=.038）、认知参与（p=.019, q=.048, r=.121）、享受度（MWU q=.081未达显著，但OLR控制先验知识后OR=1.65, q=.025）；66.9%学习者偏好对话式为"最愉快"。
- 单人TTS显著优势：音频自然度（p<.001, q<.001, r=-.238，小至中等效应）、易听度（p=.002, q=.015）。
- 协变量分析确认上述差异不归因于先验知识不平衡。
- 矛盾与权衡：对话结构带来教育收益但牺牲音频自然度（增加外在认知负荷）；新手角色的"学习者代理"功能有限（仅39.8%认为新手提问与自己相似），更多通过认知支架而非情感共情发挥作用。

Conclusion: 本研究从理论层面揭示了TTS环境中增加说话人数会降低音频自然度（外在负荷增加），但对话式格式在理解力、自信与认知参与上仍优于单人模式这一权衡模式；拓展了社会代理效应在机器语音下的边界条件；为人机协作的半自动化教育内容生成提供了设计指南与实证依据。然而受限于固定顺序、内容不同的实验设计，研究仅基于自我评估得出结论，尚不能确认课程格式的因果效应。未来需通过随机交叉设计、个体ID链接、前后测知识保持测试等来验证因果效应，并探索添加字幕/头像、扩展至多元学习群体及定量评估教师工作量的方法。

Abstract: This study proposes a semi-automated system for generating dialogue-based lessons using Large Language Models (LLMs) and Text-to-Speech (TTS) technology, and exploratorily examines its educational potential via a practical quasi-experiment. The system augments rather than replaces educators through a three-stage human-in-the-loop workflow (LLM-based slide/narration generation, educator review, automated audiovisual integration), and introduces a novel method for generating Expert-Novice dialogue narration based on cognitive apprenticeship theory. In a study of 245 first-year high school students who sequentially experienced three lesson formats (instructor voice, single-speaker TTS, dialogue TTS; content differed across sessions, limiting format/content separation), we conducted within-subject (Friedman test, N<=183) and repeated cross-sectional (Mann-Whitney U, N=229/206) analyses. TTS audio did not substantially degrade the learning experience versus instructor voice, supported by TOST equivalence testing. Dialogue TTS was significantly superior to single TTS in comprehension (p=.006, q=.025) and cognitive engagement (p=.019, q=.048); enjoyment was non-significant after FDR correction (q=.081) but reached significance after controlling for prior knowledge (proportional-odds model, OR=1.65, q=.025), and these advantages were not attributable to prior-knowledge imbalance. Conversely, single TTS was superior in audio naturalness (p<.001, q<.001, r=-.238), revealing a trade-off between dialogue's benefits and higher extraneous cognitive load. Dialogue format was preferred by 66.9% of learners as most enjoyable (p<.001). These results reflect a fixed-order design; replication is needed before generalizing them as effects of lesson format. This study provides a theoretical and empirical basis for the educational acceptability of TTS audio and for TTS lesson-format design.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [MetaInfer: A Knowledge Only LLM Inference Engine Generator SKILL Toolbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12875)
*Zhenwen Miao,Honglin Wang,Mingheng Mi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MetaInfer 提出了一种 "LLM-as-Compiler" 方法，通过多智能体协作系统和契约知识库(CKB)，仅根据用户指定的运行时约束自动生成紧凑的定制化LLM推理引擎，无需参考开源框架(vLLM/SGLang)的源码，并具备通过知识库演化扩展新模型/新平台的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用推理框架(vLLM、SGLang等)为支持多样化的模型架构、硬件平台、量化方案与并行策略，引入了大量配置项与多层抽象，导致代码复杂度高、维护成本大，且运行时仅走单一执行路径却背负了通用分支的开销。同时现有基于LLM智能体的工作(如VibeServe)严重依赖开源框架源码作为参考，无法应对尚无成熟实现的全新模型(如Qwen3.6-27B的混合注意力架构)或新兴硬件平台。真正的按需生成应当脱离具体源码引用，转而基于可蒸馏的领域知识(计算语义、优化原理、并行策略)。

Method: 系统围绕契约知识库(CKB)组织三个闭环：(1) 零参考生成回路——在CKB覆盖目标时，用户仅提供模型/硬件/部署约束，主智能体调度实现智能体、规范审查智能体、独立验证智能体(角色严格隔离避免自证循环)按阶段(基础执行层→模型组件层→框架集成层→端到端验收层→性能优化层)生成代码，每阶段由固定测试脚本的测试契约把关；(2) 知识库演化回路——当覆盖不足时，探索智能体收集外部信息(含受控源码访问)，实现智能体生成候选，验证智能体判定，知识整合智能体将有效信息写入CKB，关键准则为关闭源码引用后能否重新生成通过(PASS_VERIFIED)；(3) 经验反馈回路——将验证与优化经验反哺CKB。CKB按合同层/框架设计层/模型知识层/并行与算子层/经验优化层/测试契约层组织，而非存储源代码。

Result: 三组实验递进验证：(1) Qwen3-8B构建过程对比显示，源码参考能将构建时间从20.5h降至14.6h、token消耗从6.93M降至3.04M，但即使无参考也能完成全部11个阶段；(2) K100 DCU平台(Qwen3-8B TP=4)零参考生成引擎在3×3矩阵负载下，Eager模式吞吐约24-25 tok/s、TPOT约40ms，开启CUDA Graph后吞吐达50-54 tok/s、TPOT降至18-19ms，相比vLLM在p=11/g=256配置下快约51%(Eager)和22%(Graph)，且单卡显存仅约5.3GB(vLLM约56GB)；(3) Z200平台(Qwen3.6-27B TP=4，含GatedDeltaNet/MRoPE/RMSNormGated等新结构)经过五轮演化(evo-001至evo-005)，最终在关闭源码引用后21/21组件测试通过、24个解码token与HF greedy输出一致达PASS_VERIFIED，TPOT稳定在166-176ms，但受限于16GB/GPU和SDPA math回退，长prompt TTFT达约20s。补充实验显示A800上MetaInfer相对vLLM Eager约+52%吞吐，Apple M5与mlx_lm基本持平(比值0.96-1.03)。

Conclusion: MetaInfer将推理框架的生成从具体源码实现转移到可验证、可演化的契约知识，证明了：(1) 源码参考能显著降低构建成本但并非生成可用引擎的必要条件；(2) 在CKB覆盖的目标上零参考生成的定制单路径引擎在真实平台上具备解码吞吐与显存优势；(3) 当模型与平台超出CKB覆盖时，知识库演化能将新信息蒸馏为可重用约束，使系统在关闭源码引用后仍能再生成功。未来工作将沿两条线推进：一是从项目形态演进为更完整的生成Skill(标准化调用接口与版本管理)，二是构建profiler驱动的自动算子适配与调优机制，使系统从可验证的专用引擎生成迈向持续自优化的推理基础设施生成器；同时承认正确性演化≠性能最优化，目前仍需将系统定位为"可验证生成与知识扩展的闭环"而非无人值守的生产级优化系统。

Abstract: As LLM technology advances, the space of model families, compute hardware, quantization schemes, parallelization strategies, and specialized optimization kernels continues to expand, sharply increasing the code complexity and maintenance cost of general-purpose inference frameworks. Conventional software engineering uses multiple layers of abstraction to support diverse application scenarios, but these abstractions also increase system complexity and may introduce additional performance overhead. This paper presents metainfer, an 'LLM-as-Compiler' approach in which users specify only the runtime constraints of an inference program. An LLM-driven multi-agent collaboration system, coupled with a contract knowledge base, then automatically generates a compact customized inference framework that satisfies these constraints. We evaluate metainfer from three perspectives: the effect of source-code reference, the runtime behavior and performance profile of engines generated under the zero-reference constraint on CKB-covered targets, and knowledge-base evolution for new model and platform scenarios. The results show that metainfer organizes generation constraints, validation feedback, and knowledge consolidation into a continuous closed loop, enabling runnable customized inference solutions to be generated from explicit knowledge. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>

### [91] [GRAFT: Graph-Matched Retrieval and Fusion of Tables in Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12717)
*Daomin Ji,Hui Luo,Zhifeng Bao,Shane Culpepper,Shazia Sadiq*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文提出GRAFT（Graph-matched Retrieval and Fusion of Tables），将表格数据湖中的结构感知表格检索重新建模为查询意图图与异构数据湖图之间的图匹配问题。其核心贡献是设计了一个基于对数行列式的统一奖励函数IGMS，在单一目标中同时耦合语义相关性、结构兼容性与证据多样性；并通过隐式Q学习（IQL）在自生成轨迹上离线训练价值函数，配合三阶段剪枝流水线实现高效搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主数据代理（基于LLM）在解析分析型查询时，需要从大规模、异构的表格数据湖中检索证据并执行join/union等关系操作来整合答案。然而现有方法存在两大局限：(1) 逐点检索（如DTR、Pneuma）独立打分各表格，忽略表格间的可连接性与可联合性，导致返回的证据在结构上碎片化，下游代理无法整合；(2) 贪心扩展方法（MTR）以查询或最近表格为锚点迭代添加，缺乏结构兼容性保证；现有结构感知方法JAR虽然尝试用混合整数规划（MIP）建模，但仅线性组合相关性与连接性，忽略了多样性，且NP难导致扩展性差。此外，分析型查询的正确答案依赖于证据的完整性而非单表相关性，因此检索集合必须同时具备语义相关、结构可整合且非冗余的多样性。

Method: 方法由三个核心组件构成：(1) **图匹配建模**：将自然语言查询通过GPT-5-mini提示转换为意图图𝒢_I（多棵实体-属性-值意图树加跨树join边），将数据湖建模为含表/列/值三种节点与包含/join/union三种边的异构图𝒢_D，检索目标转化为寻找𝒢_D中允许𝒢_I同态的连通子图。(2) **IGMS奖励函数**：构建证据-意图原子的支持矩阵M_Φ（编码语义相关与结构兼容）和证据-证据协方差矩阵C_Φ（通过列集合Jaccard相似度编码冗余），最终奖励为 𝒰_IGMS = (1/a)·log det(I_a + M_ΦᵀC_Φ⁻¹M_Φ)，具有信息论解释（高斯信道互信息）。论文证明了IGMS的单调性、子模性和冗余抑制三大性质，并由此获得(1-1/e)近似保证。(3) **离线IQL+在线三阶段剪枝**：通过规范化压缩算子𝒞（在数据湖图上反演同态，自合成配对的意图图与证据轨迹）生成200K条自监督训练轨迹，在MDP上用隐式Q学习训练价值函数Q_ω。推理时采用锚点可达性（初始化）、谓词可容许性（束扩展）和奖励单调性（懒惰重排序，Cholesky秩一更新将单次评估从O(a³)降至O(a²)）三阶段剪枝，将候选空间从O(M·N^(L-1))压缩至O(BL)。

Result: 在Spider（5,673表）和BIRD（2,753表）改编的表格数据湖以及Open Data（11,345表，用于训练数据增强任务）上的实验显示：GRAFT在L∈{5,10,15}预算下，在Recall、Precision、F1和Sufficiency四项指标上一致优于逐点方法（DTR、Pneuma）、贪心扩展方法（MTR）和结构感知方法（JAR）。相对最强基线JAR，F1提升7.8%，Sufficiency提升10.6%；在训练数据增强任务上，下游RMSE最低达3.65、Accuracy最高达0.748。延迟方面GRAFT为3.5秒/查询，与MTR（4.0秒）相当，远优于JAR（22.4秒）和GRAFT-Exact消融（93.7秒）。消融实验验证了IGMS四特性、中心性采样器和GPT-5-mini意图提取器的不可或缺性；超参研究表明200K轨迹和折扣因子γ=0.8为最优。案例分析显示GRAFT能正确恢复包含桥接表的完整合成T₁⋈T₂⋈(T₃∪T₄)，而所有基线均失败。

Conclusion: GRAFT首次将结构感知表格检索严格形式化为图匹配问题，并通过信息论启发的对数行列式奖励IGMS将语义相关性、结构兼容性与多样性统一于单一目标；通过同态反演的自监督策略摆脱了对人工标注的依赖，结合IQL学习的价值函数与三阶段剪枝实现了结构感知检索的高效执行。论文为表格RAG提供了一种"可整合证据子图"的结构化范式，使下游LLM代理获得可直接join/union的完整证据。未来工作方向包括与下游执行器联合训练以实现端到端综合，以及将该形式化推广到多模态数据湖。

Abstract: Autonomous data agents resolve analytical queries by retrieving and reasoning over evidence in tabular data lakes. Existing methods score tables independently against the query and ignore the joinability and unionability that link them, returning fragmented evidence that downstream agents cannot integrate. We propose GRAFT (Graph-matched Retrieval and Fusion of Tables), structured around two principal contributions. First, we cast table retrieval as a graph matching problem between a query-derived intent graph and a heterogeneous data lake graph, and introduce IGMS, a log-determinant reward that couples semantic relevance, structural compatibility, and evidence diversity in a single objective. Second, we recast subgraph generation as a Markov decision process and learn a value function via implicit Q-learning on self-generated trajectories produced by a canonical compression operator that inverts the homomorphism. We further design a three-stage online pipeline that exploits anchor reachability, predicate admissibility, and reward monotonicity to greatly prune the candidate space before exact IGMS evaluation. On Spider and BIRD adapted to the tabular data lake setting, GRAFT achieves the best Recall, Precision, F1, and Sufficiency among point-wise, greedy-expansion, and structure-aware baselines, with relative gains of 7.8% in F1 and 10.6% in Sufficiency over the strongest baseline, while maintaining high search efficiency.

</details>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Inference Pipelines as Operating-System Objects: Priority Scheduling and Constant-Footprint Streaming for Microcontroller Neural Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12614)
*Dimitrios Kafetzis*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynapticOS 的 Phase 2 推理引擎,将微控制器上的神经网络推理流水线(预处理→加速器调用→后处理)从应用代码提升为操作系统一等对象。基于 Zephyr RTOS,引入静态池管理、规范化阶段排序校验、精确中间缓冲区规划、三级优先级作业调度器(实时>普通>尽力,FIFO 同级),以及基于临时竞技场的常量内存流式处理。在 NXP FRDM-MCXN947 (Cortex-M33 @ 150MHz) 和 qemu_cortex_m3 上进行评估:调度路径仅增加 92µs 延迟(1130 vs 1038µs),人脸检测流水线达 215.8 FPS,2,784 字节常量内存峰值。PowerQuad DSP 加速实际测量为 5.51×(FFT)和 1.66×(矩阵乘),未达到 10× 计划目标,作者如实报告为"未达标"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MCU 推理运行时(TFLM、ExecuTorch、μTVM、CMSIS-NN 等)将推理流水线视为应用代码:每个项目都需手工实现阶段排序、中间缓冲区尺寸计算和完成信号传递。这些是重复出现的、操作系统应解决的问题。常见故障模式包括:阶段顺序错误导致静默数据损坏、缓冲区过小导致内存破坏或最坏时刻失败、临时拼凑的并发原语引发并发缺陷。作者主张将流水线视为 OS 一等公民,获得与文件、套接字、线程同等的地位。

Method: 1. 流水线作为 OS 对象:从 4 槽静态池分配,构建时强制规范排序(pre* model post*),验证完备性,预计算最坏内存估算;2. 尺寸感知阶段缓冲区规划:对 9 个内置处理器用闭式公式精确计算输出容量(如 resize 为 w·h·c 字节),用户自定义阶段采用 max(4n, 64) 字节有界回退;3. 三级优先级作业调度器(realtime>normal>best-effort,FIFO 同级),专用调度线程,每作业完成信号量,定义取消语义,有界作业表,推理路径上无堆分配;4. 常量内存流式处理:所有中间张量存于临时竞技场,每帧 syn_mem_reset_ephemeral() 重置,保证零碎片、恒定内存占用;5. 阶段边界实时性能分析,在引擎内部触发 Phase 1 的四标记分析器;6. PowerQuad DSP 路由与启动时自校准(FFT 增益模型、Q15 已知答案检验)。

Result: FRDM-MCXN947 实测:同步路径总墙钟 1130µs,比 Phase 1 直接 HAL 基线(1038µs)增加 92µs,其中调度派发仅 1µs;30 帧六阶段人脸检测流水线平均 4.63ms/帧(215.8 FPS),恒定 2,784 字节竞技场峰值每帧归零,120 次分配/30 次重置,QEMU 软浮点对照为 31.1ms/帧。PowerQuad DSP 端到端加速(含包装开销):256 点 FFT 5.51×,16×16 Q15 矩阵乘 1.66×,未达 ≥10× 计划目标,如实报告。闪存开销:QEMU 镜像 +3.8KB,FRDM shell 镜像 +20.7KB(含 PowerQuad 路由与校准)。测试套件从 Phase 1 的 61 用例/10 套件扩展至 99 用例/13 套件,全部通过;协同调度确定性捕获了完成路径上的回调/槽重用竞态。

Conclusion: Phase 2 推理引擎成功将流水线提升为 OS 一等对象,在 MCU 上以极低调度开销(92µs 总、1µs 派发)实现规范化流水线管理、优先级调度和常量内存流式处理。PowerQuad 加速未达目标被诚实地标记为"未达标"而非重新定义范围,体现了诚实基线方法论的价值。CI 中基于协同线程的测试套件能确定性捕获真实硬件上将是间歇性 bug 的竞态,这一方法论贡献与引擎本身同等重要。Phase 3 路线图包括:截止时间感知调度、层级粒度抢占、TFLite Micro 模型阶段集成、Neutron 实际 NPU 调用、双 Cortex-M33 核心真正并发、PowerQuad 包装优化(持久配置、批量暂存)以缩小加速比差距。

Abstract: Microcontroller runtimes treat the inference pipeline -- pre-processing, accelerator invocation, post-processing -- as application code: every project re-implements stage sequencing, buffer sizing, and completion signalling around a library call. We argue these are operating-system concerns and present the Phase 2 inference engine of SynapticOS, an open-source Zephyr-based runtime that makes the pipeline a first-class OS object. A pipeline is drawn from a static pool, validated against a canonical stage order, and executed by a priority job scheduler (realtime > normal > best-effort, FIFO per class) with cancellation and a bounded job table; no heap on the inference path. Stage buffers are sized exactly from configuration and tensor geometry for the nine built-in processors (bounded 4x fallback for user stages); all intermediates live in an ephemeral arena reset per frame, so streaming footprint is constant. We evaluate on the NXP FRDM-MCXN947 (Cortex-M33, 150 MHz) and the qemu_cortex_m3 CI target, both running a deterministic stub NPU kernel: engine-overhead baselines, not silicon throughput. On the board the scheduler adds 92 us over the Phase 1 direct-HAL bracket (1,130 vs 1,038 us; dispatch 1 us); a 30-frame, six-stage face-detection pipeline averages 4.63 ms/frame (215.8 FPS, stub model included) vs 31.1 ms under QEMU soft-float, at a constant 2,784-byte arena peak returning to zero each frame. The PowerQuad DSP is routed and self-calibrated for FFT and Q15 matmul; end-to-end speedups are 5.51x (256-point FFT) and 1.66x (16x16 matmul), short of the plan's 10x target -- reported as missed, not re-scoped. Stage-boundary profiling now runs live on the board, closing a Phase 1 gap. The engine adds 3.8 KB flash on QEMU and 20.7 KB on FRDM. 99 tests across 13 ZTEST suites pass 100% under emulation. Released under Apache 2.0 at this https URL

</details>


### [93] [Cross-Core Inference Offload as an Operating-System Service on Dual-Core Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12620)
*Dimitrios Kafetzis*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynapticOS 第三阶段架构,将推理卸载建模为双核 MCU 上的操作系统级远程系统调用:AI 运行时(含 NPU/DSP 与调度器)驻留在具备 FPU/DSP/TrustZone/MPU 的 Cortex-M33 主核,无 FPU/DSP/TrustZone/MPU 的副核仅通过共享 SRAM 中的无锁 SPSC 环形队列访问推理服务,在 FRDM-MCXN947 上实现 15 µs 典型往返延迟、1,913 次零错误双模型浸泡测试以及单向 MPU 故障注入验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商品化双核 MCU(如 NXP MCXN947)在营销中被描述为对称双核,实际上副核往往缺失 FPU、DSP 扩展、TrustZone 与 MPU,导致绝大多数产品将第二核闲置或勉强对称化使用。本文将这种"能力不对称"视为设计输入而非缺陷,主张在 OS 层将推理封装为受保护的跨核服务,使应用核既无需承担 AI 栈的复杂度,又能在硬件允许的范围内获得完整的推理能力。

Method: 1) 非对称 AMP 架构:模型/NPU/DSP/调度器全部限定于主核,副核仅含 IPC 客户端;<br>2) 共享 SRAM 中的无锁 SPSC 环形队列:每方向一环、单写者索引、自由 32 位计数器(无符号回绕安全)、仅以 DMB 数据内存屏障排序,无须跨核原子操作,且索引间隔 64 字节以避免伪共享;<br>3) 推理即远程系统调用:20 字节定长类型化报文携带 Phase 2 调度器优先级类,错误与超时经返回值回传,27 KB 张量在共享交换槽中零拷贝暂存;<br>4) 引导协议:主核先通过 ROM API 的 FLASH_VerifyErase/Read 与向量健全性检查确认副核镜像可执行后才写入 CPBOOT 并释放核心;<br>5) 单向 MPU 防护:运行核在运行时编程 region 7 将副核 RAM 设为只读,故障策略仅中止违规线程;<br>6) 在主线 Zephyr 3.7 之外补齐副核板级移植,统一共享布局头文件并以 BUILD_ASSERT 钉死所有跨核结构偏移。

Result: 在 FRDM-MCXN947(双核 150 MHz)上实测:副核启动 1,514 µs、握手完成 2,554 µs(分别比 100 ms/200 ms 预算低 66×,且 11 次连续上电位级一致);往返延迟 last/min=15 µs、max=81 µs(50 µs 预算,尾延迟源于推理中途到达);环形 push 25 周期、push+pop 41 周期;1,913 次双模型交替推理浸泡测试零错误(约 4,000 条报文);MPU 自测试通过,故障注入后两核继续运行;固件体积 98.9 KB(运行核)/32.4 KB(应用核,RAM 占用 42.6/64 KB);108 个测试用例/13 个套件在 qemu_cortex_m3 上 100% 通过;诚实地报告三项未达标项(NPU 利用率仅约 6% 因演示为 20 Hz 节律、错误传播负路径未在板上触发、跨核优先级抢占未单独设计实验),并完整复盘两项仅硬件能暴露的缺陷(向已擦除 flash 释放副核导致整芯片楔死、Zephyr flash 驱动 Kconfig 静默解除 devicetree 声明的 MPU 保护)。

Conclusion: 把双核 MCU 的能力不对称当作设计输入,可在 OS 层获得比对称化方案更清晰、可审计的服务边界:运行核承载全部 AI 栈,应用核通过 20 字节报文 + 共享槽的"远程系统调用"获得推理能力,而硬件允许的保护被诚实地限定为单向写入完整性。实测表明,在 150 MHz Cortex-M33 上,跨核 IPC 在毫秒级推理服务时间中只贡献微秒级开销,确定性引导与可重现的握手时序在 11 次重启中保持位级一致;同时,主核镜像必须先经 ROM API 验证可执行、运行时编程 MPU 保护必须配套可重复运行的故障自测试,是两个具有跨平台可迁移性的关键工程教训。SynapticOS v0.3.0 以 Apache 2.0 开源。

Abstract: Dual-core MCUs are asymmetric: on NXP's MCXN947, the second Cortex-M33 has no FPU, DSP extension, TrustZone, or MPU. We treat the asymmetry as a design input in the Phase 3 dual-core architecture of SynapticOS, an open-source Zephyr-based runtime: the AI runtime (models, NPU/DSP, scheduler) lives on the capable core, and the application core reaches inference only via a message-based OS service -- a remote system call. The transport is a pair of lock-free single-producer/single-consumer rings in shared SRAM: one writer per index, free-running 32-bit counters, ordering by data-memory barriers alone (the platform has no cross-core atomics). Because ring state is shared, a rebooting application core rejoins unaided. Requests carry priority classes, errors and timeouts propagate to the caller, and tensors stage zero-copy in a shared slot -- a 27 KB frame cannot exist twice in 64 KB of RAM. Measured on the FRDM-MCXN947 (both cores 150 MHz): the application core boots in 1,514 us and completes the handshake in 2,554 us, bit-identical over 11 boots; round trips are 15 us typical / 81 us worst-case (50 us budget); pushes cost 25 cycles; a 1,913-serve two-model soak had zero errors (stub-NPU latencies bracket transport, not silicon). An MPU region on the runtime core guards the application core's RAM (fault-injection verified); protection is one-directional -- the application core has no MPU, and ARMv8-M cannot block privileged reads. Two hardware-revealed defects are reported: releasing the second core into erased flash wedges the whole chip and its debug port (now prevented by a ROM-API blank check), and a Zephyr flash-driver Kconfig silently disarmed the devicetree MPU guard (now programmed at runtime). Firmware is 98.9 KB flash (runtime core) and 32.4 KB (application core, 42.6 of 64 KB RAM); 108 tests in 13 suites pass 100%. Apache 2.0: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [ViHoRec: A Quality-Controlled Vietnamese Hotel Recommendation Dataset and Cold-Start Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12946)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ViHoRec是一个针对越南语酒店推荐的质量可控数据集与冷启动基准，包含18,267条来自Booking.com、Traveloka和Ivivu三大平台的交互数据（6,832名用户与560家酒店）。本文提出了可复现的跨平台实体解析与质量控制流程、基于HMAC的隐私保护方案，以及面向冷启动场景的公开时间留一法评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统研究主要集中于英语市场（MovieLens、Amazon、HotelRec等），越南语酒店推荐领域缺乏公开且具有文档化构建过程的数据资源。构建此类资源面临三大挑战：跨平台酒店名称不一致需实体解析、爬取数据需可审计的定量质量控制、公开发布需保护隐私同时支持真实冷启动条件下的基准测试。

Method: 采用四阶段构建管线：(1) 通过API、HTML爬取或半手动方式采集三大平台数据；(2) 基于规范键（去音、去停用词、词序无关）的跨平台酒店实体解析，将581个原始名称合并为560个规范酒店；(3) 定量质量控制包括去重（精确7条、近重复11条）、完整性（无缺失）、一致性（评分修复、日期校验）以及分层人工验证（248条样本，多数投票准确率100%）；(4) 使用HMAC-SHA256加盐哈希生成不可逆用户伪名。基准测试采用时间留一法划分（k=4，800用户，9,787训练/800测试，稀疏度97.53%），评估Random、MostPop、ItemKNN、UserKNN、BPR-MF和Content-TFIDF等基线模型，并按用户历史长度分层评测。

Result: 在公开划分上，UserKNN表现最佳（R@10=0.134，MRR=0.063），BPR-MF次之（R@10=0.106），Content-TFIDF因仅覆盖52.9%酒店而较弱。冷启动分层分析显示学习模型在最短历史用户上退化最严重（BPR-MF的R@10从≥11历史的0.120降至历史长度3的0.065），UserKNN在所有桶中均最强但仍对冷用户下降。数据中心消融验证：实体解析使Recall@10提升3.9%（0.1288→0.1338）；时间留一法比随机留一法在R@10上高约19%相对值。数据集稀疏度99.52%，69.5%用户为单次交互冷启动用户，项目流行度基尼系数0.599。

Conclusion: ViHoRec是首个具有文档化实体解析与质量控制流程的越南语酒店推荐公开资源，采用CC BY-NC 4.0和MIT许可证发布，并配套完整复现代码。其稀疏性与冷启动主导特性使其成为低资源推荐方法的现实压力测试而非饱和基准——分层评测揭示了饱和英语语料很少暴露的个性化失败模式。主要局限包括规模较小、用户身份因名称空间小仅为近似、内容元数据仅覆盖309家酒店，未来工作方向为扩大规模、加强用户级实体解析与增加多维度属性。

Abstract: Recommender-system research for Vietnamese remains limited by the absence of a public, well-documented hotel interaction resource. Building such a resource is challenging for three reasons: cross-platform hotel names must be reconciled before interactions are comparable; quality must be audited with reproducible metrics rather than ad hoc cleaning; and public release must preserve privacy while remaining benchmarkable under realistic cold-start conditions. We introduce ViHoRec, a quality-controlled Vietnamese hotel recommendation dataset of 18{,}267 interactions between 6{,}832 users and 560 hotels, crawled from this http URL , Traveloka, and Ivivu. Our contributions are: (i) a reproducible construction pipeline with cross-platform entity resolution and quantitative quality control; (ii) a privacy-preserving release with HMAC pseudonyms; and (iii) a public cold-start benchmark with temporal leave-last-one-out split, data-centric ablations, and dependency-free baselines. On the public split, learned models degrade sharply for users with short histories (BPR-MF Recall@10: 0.065 vs. 0.120), while UserKNN remains strongest overall, establishing ViHoRec as a sparse, cold-start-dominated testbed for low-resource recommendation. All data are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [95] [SlimPer: Make Personalization Model Slim and Smart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12281)
*Siqi Wang,Xianjie Chen,Shaofeng Deng,Albert Chen,Romil Shah,Jiawei Huang,Zhaoqin Wang,Zhang Zhang,Yiqun Liu,Meilei Jiang,Anish Dubey,Moyan Mei,Tongxin Wang,Nathan Berrebbi,Misael Manjarres,Armand Sauzay,Shardul Kothapalli,Aryaman Vinchhi,Kevin Johnstone,Juheon Lee,Gufan Yin,Ziheng Huang,Justin Lin,Mert Terzihan,Yilin Qi,Cynthia Yang,Colin Peppler,Qi Ding,Ruohan Sun,Ge Song,Litao Deng,Parichay Kapoor,Matt Ma,Huihui Cheng,Jiyuan Zhang,Yanli Zhao,Yiping Han,Fangqiu Han,Ning Yao,Arun Singh,Jordan Edwards,Zhengyu Su,Abhishek Kumar,Guangdeng Liao,Ankit Asthana*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SlimPer是一种面向工业推荐系统的个性化排序架构，将判别式推荐重新定义为对紧凑、固定大小的<用户,物品>知识库的迭代优化过程。该方法通过固定大小知识库(K=64)、逐层全模态QKV注意力选择、显式点积匹配和请求级优化(ROO)等设计，在保证O(N)逐层复杂度的同时，将模型深度与用户历史长度解耦，已在Instagram Reels和Feed上完成部署并取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
