Abstract: We present an agentic approach to autonomous neural operator discovery based on an AI scientific community, which consists of a swarm of virtual laboratories that interact under a citation-based economy of influence. Highly-cited labs found new labs that follow their research direction and replace non-performing labs. Each virtual lab contains three agents: an LLM planner that proposes an architecture, a numerical worker that trains and measures it, and an LLM reviewer that participates in cross-lab peer review. All labs share a common vocabulary consisting of DeepONet (branch-trunk), Fourier, Transformer (attention), wavelet, and residual convolutional neural operator building blocks. We evaluate the neural operator AI scientific community on five problems, namely piecewise regression, the linear advection and Burgers 1D PDEs, and the Navier-Stokes and Darcy flow 2D PDEs, while repeating the simulation three times for each problem. The results show that the neural operator AI scientific community is capable of discovering high-accuracy, low-parameter-count neural operator architectures. All 9,623 LLM calls are logged and audited, which reveals that the virtual lab LLM planners choose to hybridize in 99.8% of their logged decisions, consistently returning multi-family hybrids. Moreover, we conducted an ablation study by replacing the LLM agents in each lab by rule-based alternatives, which caused the scientific community to collapse to non-hybridized single-family stacks in several cases, showing that LLM agency is needed to preserve diversity. The results suggest a no-free-lunch theorem for neural operators: there is no universal winner. The code, configurations, and the complete LLM transcripts are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [From Geometric Recovery to Causal Validation: A Reproducible Audit of Sparse Autoencoder Features, from Superposition Geometry to Causal Inertness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12166)
*Mohamed Abdessalem Bal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征评估中的余弦相似度指标进行了批判性审计,指出相关性恢复与因果有效性之间存在显著鸿沟。在合成玩具模型中,高达77%的通过余弦≥0.90恢复标准的特征在因果上是惰性的(即匹配原子在特征存在时从不激活),即使在训练良好的SAE中也有9%的惰性比例,且惰性匹配出现在余弦≈1.000的情况。论文还提出了惰性现象的两轴分类法(结构性 vs 竞争性惰性,以及读-惰性 vs 写-惰性),并发布了模型无关的审计工具 sae-causal-audit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SAE评估实践主要依赖相关性指标(余弦相似度)来衡量恢复质量,但这混淆了两个本质不同的主张:解码器几何对齐与编码器激活行为。论文动机是揭示这一评估缺口,论证仅凭几何对齐无法证明该原子在特征存在时确实被激活,即无法确立因果层面的有效性。这一问题在可解释性研究中至关重要,因为基于错误恢复假设的监控或引导系统可能完全失效。

Method: 1. 合成玩具模型:建立完全可控的小型神经网络(32特征→8维瓶颈,s=0.95),已知真实权重。
2. 多种子叠加相图再现:扫描过完备比×稀疏度网格,3个独立种子,通过特征维度D_i和列范数||W_i||²联合分类。
3. L1 vs TopK对比:在相同激活上训练16×过完备字典(128原子),比较恢复精度、重建损失;通过冻结支撑集仅优化幅度来直接量化L1收缩效应。
4. 因果验证电池:对每个匹配特征实施消融(读方向)和带符号校正的引导(写方向)干预,计算消融/引导特异性;同时记录 fired_frac(原子在特征存在时的激活频率)。
5. 确定性可复现管道:单线程、foreach=False、确定性算法强制,实现SHA-256字节级精确(单环境)和语义等价(跨平台,rtol=10⁻⁴)的双重保证。
6. sae-causal-audit工具包:基于运行时可检查协议(encode/decode/W_dec),结构化类型,使符号处理bug不可表达。
7. 真实模型应用:通过SAELens在GPT-2-small的gpt2-small-res-jb SAE上审计83个手工概念,使用差分均值探针方向作为真实方向的代理。

Result: 1. 叠加相图再现:在比例=1的容量充足控制下达到D_i=1.00±0.00;高稀疏度处发现收敛伪影(欠训练伪装为叠加);在极端过完备度(s=0.99,比例=8)处发现未被充分描述的"弥散共享"第三几何区域(D_i≈0.08但||W_i||²≈1.40)。
2. L1 vs TopK:在匹配L0≈4处,TopK达到完美恢复精度(1.00±0.00)而L1仅为0.77±0.11;幅度精化测试证明L1通过将激活幅度+22.5%恢复了91.2%的重建缺口,而TopK仅-0.05%,直接证实L1收缩机制。
3. 因果验证核心结果:在降级SAE中,22个匹配对中17个(77%)消融特异性恰好为0(fired_frac=0,匹配原子从不激活);良好SAE中2个(22个中9%)惰性,均在余弦>0.9999处;余弦与因果特异性的相关性在良好SAE中r=0.859,在降级SAE中r=0.471,表明余弦在退化字典上是"晴雨表式"指标。
4. 惰性两轴分类:按原因分为结构性惰性(反足对叠加几何与正通过编码器结合,良好SAE中也存在)与竞争性惰性(TopK选择病理学,仅降级SAE);按方向分为读-惰性(消融无效果)与写-惰性(引导无效果),五个反足对在两方向上完全分离——同一原子既不可监控又可高特异性引导(143-310),消融效果恰好为0。
5. 真实模型:83个概念中7个通过余弦≥0.5恢复,其中1个(14%)因果惰性;发现原子碰撞信号:少数原子(14149、4504、17413等)在三个独立构建的概念批次中重复作为多个语义无关概念的最近匹配。
6. 可复现性:在固定CI环境(ubuntu-24.04, torch==2.13.0+cpu)实现字节精确SHA-256验证;跨平台(Windows vs Linux)证明浮点最后一位差异无法弥合,提出可复现性应作为"具有明确范围的分层声明"而非单一承诺。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个核心论断并均经实验验证而非简单断言:稀疏特征在几何上精确叠加(足以通过理论应得的答案发现收敛bug,并暴露出弥散共享第三区域);过完备SAE可部分解包叠加,TopK因可被直接测量的L1收缩机制而优于L1;以及几何恢复(即使近乎完美)不等于因果恢复,差距通过干预直接量化(降级SAE中77%、良好SAE中9%惰性,惰性匹配在余弦≈1.000处出现)。该工具及方法学将惰性现象分解为按原因(结构性/竞争性)和按方向(读/写)的二维分类,反足对在两个方向上完全分离,揭示了基于SAE的监控与引导系统的真实可靠性边界。论文同时提出"可复现性声明栈"模型,区分字节精确(单环境)与语义等价(跨平台)两层保证,并警示SAE特征信号在部署系统中应仅作为"更深入审查的路由信号",不可直接替代输出级安全过滤。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are the standard for decomposing superposed neural representations into interpretable features, and evaluation relies predominantly on correlational recovery metrics -- cosine similarity between ground-truth directions and decoder atoms. We show this conflates two distinct claims: decoder-geometry alignment and encoder-activation behavior. We reproduce the superposition phase diagram of Elhage et al. (2022), identifying a convergence artifact at high sparsity and an under-described diffuse sharing regime at extreme overcompleteness. We reproduce the TopK-versus-L1 comparison of Gao et al. (2024), with direct evidence of L1 shrinkage. Our central result is causal: subjecting every recovered feature to ablation and steering, we find up to 77% of features passing a recovery bar (cosine >= 0.90) in a degraded SAE -- and 9% in a well-trained one -- are causally inert: the matched atom never fires when the feature is present, including matches at cosine ~1.000. We package the method as sae-causal-audit, a model-agnostic instrument with a deterministic pipeline. Re-auditing refines the finding: inertness decomposes by cause into structural inertness (antipodal-pair geometry, present in good SAEs) and competitive inertness (a TopK pathology of degraded SAEs), and by direction into read- and write-inertness, which five antipodal pairs dissociate completely -- unmonitorable yet steerable through the same atom, with steering specificities of 143-310 attached to zero ablation effects. We document why byte-exact reproducibility is unavailable by construction, and propose reporting it as a stack of claims with explicit scopes. Applying the instrument to a production SAE reproduces the pattern at small scale (14% inert) and surfaces an atom-collision signal: a handful of atoms recur as the nearest match for dozens of unrelated concepts, replicated across three batches.

</details>


### [145] [Forgetful Attention: A Trainable Support-Vector Memory with Certified Selection and Exact Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12204)
*Vishwajith Ramesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出支持向量注意力（SV-Attention），将上下文键拟合为一类SVM的支持系数作为注意力权重。该方法通过KKT活跃集划分天然获得两个性质：（1）经认证的token选择——储备集token权重严格为零，驱逐之不影响输出；（2）精确遗忘——通过Cauwenberghs-Poggio增/减求解器，在固定箱参数C下可逆删除某token，使求解器恢复到从未见过该token的状态，相当于"完美遗忘"。同时设计了复用KKT逆矩阵的自定义反向传播，以及用于训练的稳定批量近似路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时学习（TTL）类注意力层（线性注意力、DeltaNet、mesa层、Titans等）都维护着密集、无约束的最小二乘型记忆，每个token都保留一定权重，遗忘只能通过指数衰减实现，会留下残差且无法完全清除。这带来两个无解的实际问题：（1）哪些上下文token可丢弃而不影响输出？长上下文推理受内存限制，生产系统使用H2O等打分启发式做KV缓存剪枝，但无保证；（2）如何彻底删除单个token的影响？GDPR删除权和临床同意撤回常要求部署模型按需删除特定记录，传统方法（清缓存、梯度上升、系数缩放）都难以界定残差。此外，H2O类重击手策略在冗余场景下会失效——流行度集中在重复token上，稀有但携带信息的token反而被驱逐。

Method: 给定上下文键$x_i$和值$v_i$，SV-Attention的输出为以支持系数$\alpha_i$加权的核读出，其中$\alpha$求解关于键的一类支持向量数据描述（SVDD）二次规划：$\min \alpha^\top K\alpha - \text{diag}(K)^\top\alpha$，约束$\sum\alpha_i=1$、$0\le\alpha_i\le C$。箱参数$C$是原始求解器超参数，固定不变。KKT条件将token三分为：（1）边界集S（$0<\alpha_i<C$）；（2）误差集E（$\alpha_i=C$，离群键）；（3）储备集R（$\alpha_i=0$，分布内部键）。储备token的权重严格为零，由互补松弛性保证其驱逐绝不改变输出，即为"经认证的选择"。对于精确遗忘，使用Cauwenberghs-Poggio fp64增/减算法，维护边界化KKT矩阵$M$的逆$\mathcal{R}=M^{-1}$；逐步降系数至零即沿原路径逆向删除token，恢复到相同$C$下从未训练该token的状态。自定义反向传播通过隐函数定理复用前向维护的$\mathcal{R}$，无需二次求解。训练则使用另一条稳定路径：FISTA单精度估计活跃集掩码，再以$10^{-3}$岭化的torch.linalg.solve配合autograd求近似系数$\tilde{\alpha}$（非精确删除证书）。混合因果层由长程SV门控记忆+密集局部softmax路径构成，证书仅覆盖长程部分。

Result: 精确遗忘方面：固定$C$下，减量删除与从头重训在SVDD最优点唯一时恢复相同的活跃集划分（98–100%试验匹配率），决策函数偏差中位数约$10^{-9}$（学习键上$10^{-13}$），最差$1.1\times10^{-2}$仅限于病态近似重复流；任何场景下都低于系数衰减偏差（$8.3\times10^{-2}$到$2.4\times10^{-1}$）。删除延迟0.31–1.23 ms，比从头QP重拟合快18–197×。经认证选择方面：在~$56\%$匹配预算下，稀有组召回0.86 vs H2O（oracle查询）0.32；流式因果操作下0.93 vs 0.30；自适应边界集在40个不同键时召回0.95而DeltaNet崩溃至0.06。真实数据演示：在MIMIC-IV 1,465例ICU住院流上，~$33\%$预算下保留0.80恶化小时 vs H2O 0.05；患者级删除零泄漏（衰减下0.29/0.09）；训练回忆模型上外科式遗忘零附带损害，编辑残留为机器精度（衰减为1.15）。训练吞吐量：3.22M参数模型9,125 tokens/s，比MPS softmax慢35.8×。语言建模：enwik8字节级3.22M参数下七种子均值BPC 2.178 vs 匹配窗口Transformer++ 2.383，相对改进8.6%（95% CI [5.2, 12.0]%，配对t=5.95, p=0.001, 配对Wilcoxon p=0.016）；TinyStories三种子方向性正向但未达显著（p=0.057）；10M参数五种子为持平（方差大），32M单跑类似，原因是分块冻结门控优化慢且不稳定。

Conclusion: 将受约束的最大间隔成员加入TTL注意力家族，可赋予层结构化、经证书化而非仅打分测量的性质。对于固定$C$的精确fp64求解，活跃集划分由互补松弛性支撑，是关于哪些token被读出可证明忽略的陈述。重击手驱逐在稀有项上失败并非调参差，而是因为累计注意力质量是流行度统计，在冗余下集中在重复键；一类门控通过构造反转了这一失效模式，在边界上花费预算。在MIMIC-IV上同样是同一机制在未设计数据上的体现——恶化小时是生理离群点，保留离群点的门控即保留了它们。精确遗忘具有法规与技术的双重价值：当删除记录是法律或临床义务时，"残差很小"与"模型被证明在不含该token下重新求解"是不同陈述，后者才是审计员可依据的。证书边界明确：长程门由固定$C$ fp64求解器覆盖，当前分块token属于密集局部路径，通过掩码与重归一化删除；证书不延伸到密集路径。该贡献对求解器层面性质（经认证选择+精确遗忘）独立于语言模型规模，但语言模型优势仅限小规模。

Abstract: Attention can be viewed as an online learner over context, yet existing test-time memories cannot certify that dropping a token leaves outputs unchanged or delete its influence outright. We introduce Support Vector Attention (SV-Attention), a max-margin memory whose weights are support coefficients of a one-class SVM with fixed box parameter C. Its active-set partition gives reserve tokens exactly zero weight, certifying output-preserving eviction; a reversible incremental solver deletes a token to recover the state produced by retraining without it under the same C. In fp64 experiments, decrement and refit recover identical partitions whenever the optimum is unique, and their decision functions match to a median deviation of about 10^-9 (10^-13 on learned keys); the 10^-2 worst case is confined to ill-conditioned duplicates and remains below coefficient decay in every regime. The exact path reuses the maintained KKT inverse in a custom backward. Training uses a separate stabilized batched approximation and does not carry the exact-deletion certificate; it reaches 9,125 tokens/s on a 3.22M-parameter model, while remaining 35.8 times slower than an MPS softmax reference. At matched budgets, certified selection reaches 0.86 vs. 0.32 rare-item recall and retains 0.80 vs. 0.05 deterioration hours on real MIMIC-IV streams. We also demonstrate surgical forgetting, exact editing, patient-record deletion, and a forgettable retrieval memory over real sentence embeddings. On enwik8, the hybrid obtains 2.178 BPC vs. 2.383 for a matched-state sliding-window Transformer across seven seeds (8.6% paired improvement, p=0.001); a three-seed TinyStories result is directionally positive but not significant (p=0.057).

</details>


### [146] [Speculate with Memory: Lossless Acceleration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12236)
*Yu Li,Qinyuan Ye,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种为推测执行加速LLM Agent的方法，通过为推测器（speculator）配备三种在线记忆系统（对比转移动作表、情景记忆、困惑追踪器），使其能够从历史轨迹中学习，从而在环境空闲时更准确地预测下一步动作或观察结果，提升LLM Agent的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM Agent推测执行方法中，推测器是无状态的（stateless），在任务之间丢弃所有信息，导致预测质量无法随经验的积累而提升。作者希望通过引入在线学习机制，使推测器能够从过往轨迹中持续改进预测性能。

Method: 为推测器配备三种在线记忆系统：1) 对比转移动作表（Contrastive Transition Table）：追踪动作序列统计信息；2) 情景记忆（Episodic Memory）：检索上下文相似的轨迹片段；3) 困惑追踪器（Confusion Tracker）：抑制重复出现的错误。在三种推测任务上评估：动作预测、观察预测、链式预测。

Result: 在动作预测任务上获得19-39%的相对准确率提升；在具有重复动作空间的观察预测任务上，准确率提升最高达2.5倍。这些增益随着记忆累积持续增长，并且可以泛化到不同成本的推测器模型。由于推测在空闲时间运行，不增加wall-clock开销，且执行轨迹与非推测执行完全一致，因此是无损的。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的推测执行能够显著提升LLM Agent的效率，通过在线记忆系统实现预测质量的持续提升，同时保持无损执行（零额外时间成本，轨迹一致性），为推测执行提供了一种可扩展、自我改进的解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative execution accelerates LLM agents by using a smaller, cheaper model to predict and pre-launch the next step while the environment is idle. However, existing speculators are stateless and discard all information between tasks, preventing prediction quality from improving with experience. We equip the speculator with three online memory systems that learn from past agent trajectories: a contrastive transition table tracking action-sequence statistics, an episodic memory retrieving contextually similar segments, and a confusion tracker suppressing recurring errors. We evaluate this approach on six benchmarks spanning three speculation types: action prediction, observation prediction, and chained prediction. Memory-augmented speculation yields a 19--39\% relative accuracy improvement on action prediction and up to a $2.5\times$ increase on observation prediction tasks with repetitive action spaces. These gains grow continuously as memory accumulates and generalize across speculator models of varying cost. All speculation is lossless because it runs during idle time at zero added wall-clock cost, and the actor's trajectory is identical to non-speculative execution.

</details>


### [147] [Cluster-Weighted EDMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12243)
*Lorenzo Tomaz,Judd Rosenblatt,Flavio Kicis,Thomas B. Jones,Diogo Schwerz de Lucena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD),通过EM算法联合学习相空间软划分与每簇局部EDMD算子,在匹配多项式提升阶数下,在Lorenz、阻尼摆、Duffing三个系统上的288次配对比较中,258次显著优于全局EDMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局EDMD用单一Koopman算子拟合整个相空间,但当不同区域呈现明显局部动力学差异时效率低下;现有分区方法依赖预定义(流域标签、运行工况指示器)或仅做几何划分,无法让分区追踪"哪里预测得准"而非"哪里数据密"。

Method: 基于Cluster-Weighted Model (CWM)联合密度框架,以EM联合学习G个簇:每个簇含中心cg、协方差Σg、Koopman矩阵Kg(在以cg为中心的多项式提升上拟合);E步责任度r_ig∝πg·N(xt;cg,Σg)·N(Δxg;0,σg²I),即几何邻近×预测残差似然,使分区追踪预测精度;M步用责任度加权最小二乘闭式更新Kg;采用弱共轭先验稳定小簇并支持空簇剪枝;k-means+局部EDMD初始化+多重启;推理时残差项不可用,退化为纯几何选簇。

Result: 288次配对Wilcoxon检验:p<0.05下258胜/4负/26平;阻尼摆/Duffing/Lorenz单步误差中位数比EDMD分别降低57×/2.7×/12×;5秒滚动比EDMD分别降低55×/2.5×/8.3×;4次全败出现在Lorenz小数据配置(N=500,N/G≈42样本/Mq²=400参数);消融显示:失配提升(摆/Lorenz q=2/Duffing q=2)时残差感知E步起决定作用,EDMD饱和时(q=3,5)纯几何分区已足够。

Conclusion: CW-EDMD在匹配多项式提升阶数下一致优于EDMD;其增益可分解为两种机制:分区本身(几何GMM-EDMD vs EDMD)与残差感知责任度(CW-EDMD vs GMM-EDMD);方法模型无关,EDMD预测器可替换为Taylor线性化等局部逼近器;局限性:无定量失效预测规则、Lorenz上非严格支配、仅限低维自治光滑系统、阻尼摆上高阶EDMD仍可达到更低绝对误差。

Abstract: Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) approximates Koopman operators from data, but a single global operator is inefficient when different state-space regions exhibit distinct local dynamics. We introduce Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD), which jointly learns a soft phase-space partition and a per-cluster EDMD operator. Its Expectation-Maximization (EM) objective assigns each transition based on both geometric proximity and prediction residuals, so clusters specialize where local Koopman models are accurate rather than where the data are dense. On Lorenz, damped pendulum, and Duffing systems, across 36 configurations and 10 seeds, CW-EDMD improves matched-degree EDMD in one-step and 5s-rollout prediction. Across 288 paired comparisons, there are significant error reductions in 258 cases, increases in 4, and no differences in 26. Median one-step error reductions are 57x, 2.7x, and 12x on pendulum, Duffing, and Lorenz, respectively.

</details>


### [148] [Proximity Features: Privacy-Compliant Cold-Start Personalization at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12246)
*Wei Jiang,Bin Xu,Hui Gao,Bharathi Thangamani,Weiwei Guo,Sundar Srinivasavaradhan,Tracy Yu,Huiji Gao,Michael Kinoti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Airbnb提出了一种名为"Proximity Features"的隐私合规特征系统，通过基于地理位置（geo-IP）的自适应聚类算法将用户分组为约1000人的"邻近桶"，在推理时无需持久化个人标识符即可为冷启动用户（匿名、首次访问或历史不足用户）提供聚合的用户级特征。该系统已在营销落地页和首页目的地推荐（AutoSuggest）两大场景上线，在线A/B实验显示预订量有统计显著的提升，Never-Booked和Dormant用户群收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Airbnb作为低频高决策成本的双边市场，大量流量来自登出或首次访问用户（尤其在付费渠道落地页），传统基于user_id或cookie的visitor_id的用户级特征不可用或过于稀疏。同时GDPR等隐私法规和第三方cookie限制进一步削弱了基于标识符的追踪能力。论文旨在同时解决冷启动个性化缺失和隐私合规两大挑战。

Method: 1. 核心组件——邻近键（Proximity Key）：通过base64(⌊lat·s⌋, ⌊lng·s⌋, h(ip) mod b)构造，其中s为邻近标量控制地理瓦片粒度，b为IP哈希桶数，h使用xxhash64。2. 自适应聚类算法（两阶段启发式）：Phase 1针对密集位置（用户数≥T且IP数≥n_ip^min），按IP哈希细分为b=⌈n_users/T⌉个子桶；Phase 2针对稀疏位置，通过多遍粗化（e从e_max到e_min），按s=10^e量化经纬度，当满足比例阈值ρ的瓦片用户数≥T时分配。3. 特征计算：每日离线计算三类特征（短期参与、长期预订模式、人口统计），存入分布式KV存储。4. 服务：统一API，实时场景用client_ip，批量场景用user_id（内部解析为最近IP）；未见过IP支持在线即时计算key；软依赖机制（60ms超时）。5. 隐私设计：consent-gated流水线、k-匿名式聚合（目标T≈1000）、不需推理时持久化个人标识符、PII短保留期。

Result: 离线评估：低活跃用户基于邻近特征的目的地预测Recall@5约29-30%，7天浏览特征与365天预订特征效果相当；AutoSuggest V2模型加入邻近特征Top-3准确率相对提升约2%。在线A/B实验：营销落地页全球预订+0.011%、首次预订者+2.0%、有日期搜索者+0.054%；AutoSuggest全球预订+0.16%、首次预订+0.33%。分群分析显示Never-Booked（+0.27%，流量占比65%）、Dormant（+0.33%，占比11%）、Active（+0.15%，占比23%）三个群组方向性正向，New-Guest（<1%流量）不显著（p=0.78）。推荐多样性提升（出现吉隆坡、首尔、济州岛、迪拜等目的地），流行度偏见减少。2023年引导的键分区持续服役至今未重聚类，验证了时间稳定性。

Conclusion: Proximity Features作为隐私合规的特征系统，成功在双侧市场为冷启动用户提供个性化能力，无需推理时的持久个人标识符，对第三方cookie限制和隐私法规具有弹性。在Airbnb多个场景显著提升预订，Never-Booked和Dormant用户收益最大，与设计假设一致。局限包括：稀疏地区桶粒度粗导致特征相关性降低、geo-IP城市级准确率约80%导致部分用户被分到错误地理桶。未来方向：分层邻近键（多分辨率）、版本化键分区、扩展至Nearby Search等更多场景、与营销归因模型集成、实时邻近键更新捕捉日内流量模式。

Abstract: Personalization in two-sided marketplaces relies heavily on user-level features, yet for platforms with infrequent, high-consideration purchases, a large fraction of users lack sufficient history for effective recommendation, spanning both paid and organic channels. At Airbnb, a substantial share of search requests comes from logged-out or first-time users, with this challenge especially pronounced on paid-channel landing pages, leaving traditional user-level features unavailable for a large fraction of traffic. Privacy regulations and increasing restrictions on third-party cookies further limit identifier-based tracking for non-essential use cases. This paper introduces Proximity Features, a privacy-compliant feature system that groups users by geographic proximity using geo-IP data and an adaptive clustering algorithm, producing aggregated user-level signals for groups of approximately 1,000 nearby users without requiring a persistent individual identifier at inference time. Privacy is preserved by design: the pipeline operates on consented, aggregated data only within consent-gated privacy controls. The system is deployed in production at Airbnb, serving multiple surfaces including marketing landing pages and destination recommendation, with engagement emails integration under way. Online A/B experiments demonstrate statistically significant lifts in bookings, with the largest gains observed among users with absent or stale history.

</details>


### [149] [Understanding Structured Health Data through Interaction-Aware Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12255)
*Ji Hwan Park,Ying Ding,Tianjin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于交互感知混合专家模型(I²MoE)的卒中后僵直预测任务，针对同一结构化健康记录构建模型级、数据级和表示级三种多视图方案。结果表明：多视图带来的预测性能提升有限，但路由归因分析揭示了不同视图构造在专家分配上存在系统性差异，且对相似患者具有局部一致性，表明视图构造是可解释性的关键设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化健康数据（如诊断码、程序、生命体征等表格化数据）的现有建模方法通常将其视为单一表格输入，或附加文本/影像等多模态信息，忽略了同一记录内部可构造多种有意义视图的潜力。同时，简单拼接不同视图无法区分视图特有信号、共享信号与协同信号。本文旨在探索：将同一结构化记录转化为多种替代表示并建模其交互，能否在提升预测性能的同时增强可解释性。

Method: 1. **任务**：基于HCUP/NIS 2016-2020年卒中住院数据（129,401例）进行卒中后僵直二分类预测，使用55个ICD-10-CM代码定义僵直标签。  
2. **三种视图定义**：  
   - **模型级**：使用SAINT、NODE、DCN-V2三种表格学习骨干网络编码同一记录，取隐藏表示作为视图。  
   - **数据级**：按临床语义将记录划分为行政、程序、诊断、临床体征、ICD指示符五组，各由MLP编码。  
   - **表示级**：使用冻结的GATv2（图）、NODE（表格）、Qwen3-Embedding（文本）编码器获得表示后融合。  
3. **I²MoE框架**：采用Partial Information Decomposition（PID），包含每个视图的unique专家、一个synergy专家、一个redundancy专家及重加权网络g，联合优化任务损失与弱监督交互损失。  
4. **消融与评估**：对每种视图进行Full/无Synergy/无Redundancy/无两者四种设置，在30个随机种子上评估AUROC、AUPRC、F1，并通过专家路由权重进行全局和局部解释性分析。

Result: **预测性能**：三种视图定义与强基线（XGBoost、GATv2、SAINT、NODE、DCN-V2）相当，但优势取决于具体指标。模型级视图在AUROC（0.7714）和AUPRC（0.7888）上显著优于XGBoost；表示级视图在F1（0.7313）上显著优于XGBoost但AUROC更低；数据级视图与XGBoost无显著差异。整体增益有限，因视图源自同一记录而非独立模态。  
**交互消融**：移除synergy或redundancy专家未造成统计显著的性能下降，说明交互专家非主要预测来源，预测信号主要来自unique专家和重加权网络。  
**全局解释**：不同视图构造产生系统性不同的专家权重分配——模型级和数据级视图将大量权重分配给synergy和redundancy专家（反映视图间存在重叠和互补），而表示级视图更多依赖unique专家（图/表格/文本嵌入保留更多视图特有信号）。  
**局部解释**：在表示空间中，相邻患者的专家权重距离显著小于随机匹配患者，表明专家路由分配具有局部一致性。

Conclusion: 本文证明：对同一结构化健康记录的多视图建模在预测性能上提升有限，因视图本质上是同一记录的替代分解而非独立模态；移除显式交互专家不会显著降低性能，说明协同与冗余建模更适合作为诊断视图使用方式的工具而非性能改进手段。然而，路由分析揭示视图构造选择导致队列级专家分配的系统性差异，且该分配对相似患者具有局部一致性。这些发现确立了基于路由的归因方法作为结构化健康数据患者级解释的有前景的基础。未来工作可聚焦于将专家级路由与更细粒度的临床概念关联，以及开发能从同一记录中提取更具区分性信号的视图构造方法。

Abstract: We study interaction-aware mixture-of-experts for post-stroke rigidity prediction using multi-level views of structured health records. Despite minimal performance gains, routing attribution reveals systematic importance differences across views, underscoring view construction as key to interpretability.

</details>


### [150] [From Many to Meaningful: Feature-Guided Zero-Shot Chronic Kidney Disease Screening Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12260)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Sirajam Munira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种特征引导的零样本LLM框架用于慢性肾病(CKD)早期筛查。该框架利用ML方法筛选的紧凑临床特征子集（排除金标准病理检测），将表格数据序列化后通过指令式提示词输入四个LLM（LLaMA-3、Qwen-3、Mistral、GPT-4o-mini），在孟加拉国、印度和阿联酋三个异构数据集上进行验证，证明精选特征集在零样本设置下能持续提升或保持筛查性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球约8.5亿人受CKD影响，低中收入国家负担尤重，但早期CKD常无症状且现有筛查依赖昂贵的实验室检测（如eGFR、尿白蛋白肌酐比）。传统ML方法需要大量标注数据并对群体/分布偏移鲁棒性差。LLM在零样本推理上表现出强大能力，但在系统性的零样本CKD筛查（尤其基于社区可获取的非金标准特征）方面尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 数据集：使用三个异构CKD数据集（孟加拉国社区队列284例、印度UCI 400例、阿联酋医院56例CKD阳性），均排除金标准病理指标（血清肌酐、尿白蛋白、eGFR、uACR等）；2. 特征选择与协调：基于先前ML分析（10种特征选择方法）从数据集1筛选紧凑临床特征子集，并映射到数据集2和3；3. 提示词构建：将表格记录序列化为列表格式，采用指令式角色条件化提示模板，约束输出空间为二分类（0/1）；4. 零样本推理：使用四个LLM（LLaMA-3 8B、Qwen-3 8B、Mistral 7B、GPT-4o-mini）的token级对数概率进行概率分类，无任何任务特定训练；5. 评估：比较全特征集vs.精选特征集，使用平衡准确率、宏AUROC、宏F1和Brier损失（95% Bootstrap置信区间，配对置换检验）。

Result: 精选特征集在所有数据集和大多数模型上一致且统计显著地提升性能：数据集1上Qwen-3平衡准确率从0.62提升至0.78、AUROC从0.735提升至0.836、Brier损失从0.253降至0.161；Mistral从0.56升至0.77；数据集2上Mistral提升最显著（0.53→0.81，p<0.001），LLaMA-3从0.50升至0.76；数据集3整体性能较高（多为中晚期CKD），Qwen-3从0.93升至0.97，但LLaMA-3完全失败（0%）。零样本性能接近先前监督ML方法（数据集1：78.32% vs 90.4%；数据集2：88% vs 79.33%）。较小开源模型从特征精简中获益更大，而GPT-4o-mini对特征冗余相对鲁棒。

Conclusion: 研究证明LLM可在无数据集特定训练的情况下，利用紧凑、临床协调的特征集实现有意义的零样本CKD筛查。特征引导的提示策略显著增强了零样本推理的可靠性与稳定性，尤其在早期社区筛查和资源受限场景下。紧凑特征集通过减少噪声、引导模型关注显著风险因素来提升性能。局限性包括数据集规模适中、地理覆盖有限、零样本提示词对模型版本敏感、数据集3缺乏阴性对照。未来工作应扩展更大更多样化的队列、探索结构化不确定性估计以及少样本或检索增强扩展。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is essential for preventing irreversible progression; however, many machine learning (ML)-based screening methods remain difficult to deploy in community and resource-limited screening settings due to their reliance on large labeled datasets, resource-intensive pathology tests, or high-dimensional clinical features, and limited robustness to population and distributional shifts. This study examines the feasibility of using large language models (LLMs) for early-stage CKD screening in a zero-shot setting, without dataset-specific training. We propose a feature-guided zero-shot framework that evaluates LLM performance using a selected set of clinically meaningful, readily available community-based features, rather than exhaustive clinical inputs. Feature selection was guided by ML-based analysis to identify a compact, clinically relevant subset of variables. Tabular patient records were subsequently serialized into text using standardized prompt templates to enable zero-shot inference. The zero-shot performance of four LLMs (LLaMA-3, Qwen-3, Mistral, and GPT-4o-mini) was evaluated using both the full feature set and the selected subset. Generalizability was assessed across three heterogeneous CKD datasets spanning three countries. Across models and datasets, the selected feature set yielded consistent and statistically significant improvements in balanced accuracy and probability estimates, achieving performance levels suitable for screening purposes. These findings suggest that LLMs can support clinically meaningful, training-free CKD screening using minimal community-accessible patient features, offering a practical complement to conventional ML methods in real-world screening contexts.

</details>


### [151] [Saturation Makes Quantization Error Additive: A Coverage Model with a Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12266)
*Joshua Hill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"覆盖模型"(coverage model)来解释低比特量化损失的加性结构,挑战了HAWQ、CoopQ等方法依赖的孤立敏感度假设。研究发现,在4比特权重量化中,85-93%的损失方差可由逐层效应解释,序数缺陷不超过2%。基于此提出的覆盖模型与加性模型作为分配器,在30B-355B参数的MoE模型上取得了最低KL散度,并在低于4比特的预算下保持了推理任务的完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前混合精度量化方法(如HAWQ、ModelOpt、CoopQ)依赖一个未经检验的假设:通过隔离测量的逐层或成对敏感度可以重建联合量化配置的损失。然而,在实际部署的4比特权重量化场景下,这一假设从未被直接验证过。如果该假设不成立,将导致量化分配策略的系统性偏差。论文旨在直接测试这一前提,并提出更可靠的理论框架。

Method: 1. 将量化损失变化f(S)视为布尔立方体上的集合函数,通过Walsh变换和Möbius展开两种经典基变换进行分析。2. 引入"饱和"(saturation)概念,提出覆盖模型f(S)=c(1-∏(1-a_i)),其中a_i为各层的"破坏率",c为损失天花板。3. 提供三个核心定理:定理1(加性误差等于高阶Walsh能量)、定理2(序数加性的可检验条件)、定理3-4(覆盖模型的精确Möbius和Walsh谱)。4. 提出无需拟合的Aumann-Shapley路径积分估计器。5. 在30B-355B MoE模型上进行分配器对比实验,使用log-headroom背包问题求解最优分配。

Result: 1. 关键发现:在部署分布下,85-93%的损失方差由逐层效应解释;在精确格点上达到97-99%。2. 序数缺陷(序数加性的违反率)在部署场景下不超过2%,且许多模型中与零不可区分。3. 隔离敏感度过度估计配置损失71-376%(如HAWQ-v2在0.6B模型上376%)。4. 覆盖/加性分配器在30B模型上比ModelOpt基线KL散度低17-21%,在235B和355B模型上一致优于其他方法。5. 低于4比特时,加性分配在30B和235B规模上仍能保持推理任务的终止生成,而梯度敏感度方法产生的分配已无法产生终止输出。6. Aumann-Shapley路径积分恢复最优加性系数及其精确的层级排序,无需任何采样配置。7. 平稳核(GP/RBF)在分布外配置上退化2-5倍,而加性/覆盖模型仅退化1.1-2倍。

Conclusion: 量化损失的结构性特征"饱和"——损失有界且边际损伤递减——解释了为何逐层效应占主导。覆盖模型作为该结构的可拟合表示,仅用L+1个参数即可精确预测配置损失,并提供任何加性方法的可证明误差下界(证书)。该框架不仅在理论上统一了混合精度分配问题,更在实践中以最低的测量成本实现了优于现有生产系统的分配质量,特别是在低于4比特的极低预算下维持了模型的可服务性。论文将所有结果配以"证书",明确报告加性预测何时足够,体现了可验证的科学严谨性。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization must decide which parts of a model to keep at higher precision. A common premise, shared by sensitivity-based methods such as HAWQ and CoopQ, is that the loss from quantizing a set of layers can be reconstructed from per-layer or pairwise sensitivities measured in isolation. We test this premise at the 4-bit weight-and-activation precisions now being deployed, treating the change in loss $f(S)$ from quantizing a layer set $S$ as a set function on the Boolean cube and analyzing it through two classical changes of basis. This analysis yields two findings. First, across configurations drawn from the deployment distribution, 85--93\% of the variance of $f$ is explained by per-layer effects alone. Second, a monotone transform of a sum of per-layer terms reproduces $f$'s ranking of configurations, misordering at most 2\% of pairs. We propose the coverage model $f(S)=c\bigl(1-\prod_{i\in S}(1-a_i)\bigr)$, which reproduces the measured variance profile of $f$ to within a few percent from its $L$ fitted break-rates. This structure supports two predictors of a configuration's loss, each with $L+1$ parameters. The additive model is the optimal first-order predictor. By Parseval's identity its mean-squared error equals the variance of $f$ left unexplained by per-layer effects, which we measure on full lattices, estimate out of sample at full-network scale, and report with every result as a certificate of how well any additive model can do. The coverage model itself is the second predictor. As allocators at matched memory, they attain the lowest KL divergence among the compared allocators on models from 30B to 355B parameters. Below four bits, the resulting allocations continue to solve code and reasoning tasks at budgets where allocations from gradient sensitivities no longer produce terminating generations.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum Port-Hamiltonian Neural Networks: Learning Conservative and Dissipative Dynamics via Measurement-Induced Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12269)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子端口-哈密顿神经网络（Q-pHNNs），一类通过同构哈密顿映射（IHM）以结构保持方式学习经典动力学的参数化量子电路。核心思想是将端口-哈密顿系统的反对称互联矩阵 J 映射为酉门演化，将半正定耗散矩阵 R 映射为通过线路中间测量和经典前馈实现的测量诱导非线性（MINL），使能量守恒和被动性由电路拓扑结构性保证。论文实例化了四种架构（Q-HNN、Q-pHNN v1、Q-pHNN v2、拓扑纠缠的 QGNN），在非线性摆和阻尼谐振子上验证了 1.35% 能量漂移、100% 能量单调性以及 12.1% 阻尼系数无监督辨识精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的结构保持神经网络（HNN、LNN、pHNN）通过罚项或矩阵投影近似地强制执行守恒律和被动性约束，并非真正的结构性保证。同时，量子机器学习存在线性瓶颈——标准量子门均为酉线性操作，无法直接表达耗散非线性。两条研究线之间的精确代数对应关系尚未被建立，阻碍了将量子电路用于学习开放耗散动力学的结构保持方法的发展。

Method: 核心方法为同构哈密顿映射（IHM）定理，建立以下代数对应：J（反对称）↔ 酉门 U_J(θ)=e^{-iĤ_sys t}；R（半正定）↔ 辅助比特 Born 规则投影测量；标量能量 H(x) ↔ ⟨Z⊗Z⟩_θ 期望值；外部端口 ↔ 控制哈密顿门。基于此映射实例化四种架构：（1）Q-HNN 使用两比特 PQC 与参数移位规则（Parameter-Shift Rule）精确计算 ∂H/∂q、∂H/∂p，配合 Störmer-Verlet 辛积分器长程积分；（2）Q-pHNN v1 通过系统-辅助比特纠缠、中间测量与经典前馈构成 MINL 三步协议实现 R 通道；（3）Q-pHNN v2 联合学习能量猜想和阻尼系数 γ；（4）QGNN 将每节点对应一个量子比特，沿耦合边放置 ZZ 纠缠门，构建 N 节点耦合相位子网络。理论贡献包括定理 IV.2（结构性同构）和定理 IV.3（数据编码门上的辛参数移位规则）。

Result: 四个关键实验结果：（1）Q-HNN 在非线性摆上仅用 4 个变分参数和 8 次 BFGS 迭代即达到 1.35% 相对能量漂移（300 步），比一阶 Euler 基线（12.3% 漂移）改善 9.1 倍；（2）Q-pHNN v1 在损失函数中无任何被动性罚项的情况下结构性保证 100% 能量单调递减；（3）Q-pHNN v2 从 200 个均匀采样的向量场快照中无监督辨识阻尼系数 γ=0.264，真值 0.300，误差 12.1%，验证集 q̇ MSE 为 0.268；（4）QGNN 在任意节点数 N 下保持机器精度能量守恒 |Ḣ|=0 和 100% 被动性，且无需架构改动。

Conclusion: Q-pHNN 框架证明了端口-哈密顿结构可通过量子电路拓扑而非正则化来结构性保证，消除了经典 pHNN 所需的矩阵投影开销，并将被动性作为"硬约束"而非软惩罚。IHM 结构性保证具有无标度特性（scale-free），可扩展至 N 体耦合网络。该框架在预测意义上具有广泛适用性：基因调控网络（生物化学反应的耗散结构）、脑机接口（神经群体动力学的低维哈密顿流）、量化金融（均值回复耗散动力学）和心脏电机械建模（Purkinje 纤维网络的耗散端口-哈密顿系统）。同时，MINL 协议与离散时间 Markovian 开放量子系统的 Lindblad 方程存在代数等价，理论上具有更广泛的可解释性。

Abstract: We introduce Quantum Port-Hamiltonian Neural Networks (Q-pHNNs), a family of parameterised quantum circuits that learn classical dynamics in a structure-preserving manner. The framework relies on the Isomorphic Hamiltonian Mapping (IHM): the skew-symmetric interconnection matrix $\mathbf{J}$ corresponds to unitary gate evolution, and the positive-semidefinite dissipation matrix $\mathbf{R}$ corresponds to Measurement-Induced NonLinearity (MINL) realised via mid-circuit measurement and classical feedforward. This ensures conservation and passivity are enforced by construction rather than penalty terms. We instantiate the IHM in four architectures: (1) a Quantum HNN that learns conservative energy manifolds and extracts Hamilton's equations exactly via the Parameter-Shift Rule; (2) a Q-pHNN using Born-rule measurement for dissipation; (3) a Q-pHNN jointly learning the energy ansatz and damping coefficient; and (4) a topology-entangled Quantum Graph Neural Network for $N$-node coupled-phasor networks. Experiments on the nonlinear pendulum and damped harmonic oscillator demonstrate: (i)~$1.35\%$ relative energy drift with a symplectic integrator and scale correction; (ii)~$100\%$ energy monotonicity for the MINL circuit; and (iii)~$12.1\%$ error in damping-coefficient identification from vector-field snapshots with no direct supervision on the damping coefficient.

</details>


### [153] [A hybrid analytical-PINN model for subsurface simulation of geothermal heat exchangers in heterogeneous underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12271)
*Moke Rao,Thomas Hamacher,Smajil Halilovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合解析线源模型(ILS/FLS/MFLS)与物理信息神经网络(PINN)的参数化混合方法,通过将温度场分解为解析基准解与可学习的修正分量,有效解决了含奇异线源(钻孔换热器)的非均质地下热传输问题,实现了可重复使用的通用修正器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层地热能的钻孔换热器(BHE)系统设计需要大量重复的温度场仿真。传统解析模型(ILS/FLS/MFLS)受限于均质假设,而数值方法计算成本过高;现有PINN方法难以处理线源带来的Dirac δ奇异性。因此亟需一种兼顾计算效率、精度且能处理非均质土壤与奇异源的高效替代模型。

Method: 1. **分解策略**:将温度变化分解为解析基线分量(T̃^m,来自ILS/FLS/MFLS)与PINN学习修正分量(T̃_c^m),自然消除奇异性;2. **非均质建模**:采用水平面内线性梯度热导率λ(x)=λ_B+λ_x·x_rel+λ_y·y_rel;3. **修正方程**:将原问题重构为不含奇异源的对流-扩散修正方程,右端项捕捉均质解析解在异质场中的残差;4. **参数化网络**:以相对坐标、距离r、归一化全局坐标指示函数及电导率参数μ=(λ_B,λ_x,λ_y)作为输入,逼近单孔单位热提取率的通用修正场;5. **边界处理**:FLS采用基于距离函数的硬约束层精确满足Dirichlet边界;6. **训练策略**:源中心自适应采样(近源/中间/远场分区)与时间分层采样,结合物理残差损失与有限差分监督数据;7. **叠加原理**:训练后通过空间-时间线性叠加扩展至多BHE场配置。

Result: 在5×5 BHE阵列、30年长周期仿真中,两种电导率场景下的数值测试表明:相对L²误差方面,ILS模型从解析解的~1e-1~2.5e-1降至混合PINN的~5e-3~7e-3(降幅约一个数量级);FLS模型从~4e-2~2e-1降至~1e-2~3e-2;MFLS模型从~3e-2降至~1e-2~2e-2。混合PINN预测解显著消除了纯解析解沿电导率梯度方向的结构化偏差与边界失配,恢复了土壤异质性引入的残差场,且L∞误差保持在~5e-2~8e-2水平,验证了方法对长时序、多BHE、非均质含地下水流动情形的有效性。

Conclusion: 该参数化混合PINN方法通过解析-PINN分解成功规避了奇异源对神经网络的挑战,基于相对坐标系与位置指示函数增强的特征设计有效捕获了局部与全局特性,自适应采样策略缓解了时空尺度失衡。经充分训练的通用修正器可经叠加原理推广至任意BHE配置,适用于BHE系统布局设计、负荷规划及长期运行优化等PDE约束优化问题。未来工作将扩展至孔内换热耦合、更复杂土壤属性场以及神经算子、编码器-解码器等更具表达力的架构,以应对高维参数空间的代理建模。

Abstract: In this paper, a parametric physics-informed neural network for solving the heterogeneous soil thermal problem with borehole heat exchangers (BHEs) as singular sources is developed. There are three novel features in the present framework; namely, (i) the singularity is naturally removed by using analytical line source models; (ii) using the explicit formulation for gradient thermal conductivity enables physics-informed learning of the parametrization featuring the conductivity; (iii) the learned correction is utilized as an efficient universal corrector via superposition principles. We first introduce the decomposition of the temperature change and transform the approximation of the entire heterogeneous response to the correction compensating the difference between the practical solution and idealized homogeneous approximation. In such a way, the delta function singularity is excluded and the bulk heat transfer is captured for the sake of facilitating the effective training of the neural network. The original problem is then reformulated as a governing correction diffusion or advection-diffusion equation subject to a homogeneous initial condition. The linearly varying thermal conductivity is used to model the soil heterogeneity. We propose a physics-informed neural network to approximate a universal corrector with respect to a single borehole with unit heat extraction rate. As a result, the network is trained by minimizing the physics-informed and data-anchored loss function that is evaluated for sampled conductivity parameters on adaptively selected training points. In addition, we include the location indicator function regarding the source as a feature input of network and find that it helps the network to process the local information. We perform numerical tests to exhibit the effectiveness of the proposed method based on three different analytical models.

</details>


### [154] [Gradient Flow Dynamics and Implicit Bias of Diagonal Linear Networks under Infinitesimal Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12332)
*Jiajie Zhao,Jianxing Wang,Junjie Yang,Zhiwei Bai,Yaoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对角线性网络（深度和广义两层）在无穷小初始化条件下的梯度流动力学，通过结构不变流形（SIM）这一新工具，推广了Pesme & Flammarion (2023)的鞍点到鞍点动力学分析，证明了训练轨迹可由算法1刻画，并收敛于修正ℓ₁范数极小化问题的解，从而将隐式偏差推广为修正ℓ₁范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论（VC维等）无法解释过参数化神经网络的泛化现象，因此需要研究隐式偏差（implicit bias）。虽然对角线性网络是研究隐式偏差的简单测试模型，但Pesme & Flammarion (2023)的结果局限于特定的两层网络和特殊初始化方向，尚未推广到深度网络和更一般的两层网络，也缺乏对背后几何机制的理解。Li等人关于交换参数化的研究虽暗示了推广可能性，但这一推广仍是公开问题。

Method: 1. **分析参数模型设定**：考虑F(θ)(x) = Σkᵢ(θᵢ)xᵢ型模型，其中kᵢ满足实解析、唯一临界点在原点等条件；2. **结构不变流形（SIM）理论**：将模型参数空间分解为SIM，研究梯度流在SIM上的不变性。当初始化在原点附近时，SIM诱导两阶段动力学——特征选择阶段和带符号约束的学习阶段；3. **递归算法（算法1）**：通过"距离-速度"论证——特征被激活所需时间正比于初始化决定的"距离"、反比于数据相关性决定的"速度"——构造逐步激活特征的迭代优化过程；4. **极限轨迹分析**：利用Li等人关于动力系统极限轨迹的引理（A.2），分析在s→0极限下的时间映射行为。

Result: **主要定理**：- **定理4.2**：算法1在合理假设下有限步终止，其输出是修正ℓ₁范数极小化问题min Σᵢ(tᵢ⁺kᵢ⁺ + tᵢ⁻kᵢ⁻) s.t. Xk=y的解；- **定理4.5**：深度对角线性网络和广义两层网络在初始化sθ*下的梯度流轨迹Γˢ的极限点等价于算法1生成的{k^(p)}，且迭代极限limₛ→₀ limₜ→∞收敛于k^(pₘₐₓ)；- **推论4.6**：两类网络的隐式偏差为修正ℓ₁范数R(k) = Σᵢ(tᵢ⁺kᵢ⁺ + tᵢ⁻kᵢ⁻)；- **机制理解**：通过SIM证明了符号锁定和增量学习的机理——一旦参数偏离原点，其运行在一条简单曲线上，符号翻转所需时间为O(1/s)量级，与O(1)量级的特征学习时间严重不匹配。**实验验证**：在4维合成数据上，使用10⁻⁶⁰初始化和0.1学习率，验证了算法1输出(k^(1)=(0.8,0,0,0)、k^(2)=(0,0,1.4,0)、k^(3)=(0,0,10/7,-10/49))与实际训练动力学的鞍点位置一致。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将Pesme & Flammarion (2023)的鞍点到鞍点动力学分析从特定两层网络和初始化方向推广到深度对角线性网络与广义两层对角线性网络（定义4.1），建立了训练轨迹与算法1的等价性，并证明算法1收敛于修正ℓ₁范数极小化的解。理论核心贡献是引入结构不变流形（SIM）作为几何机制，揭示了两阶段动力学（特征选择+带符号约束学习）和符号锁定的本质。隐式偏差是修正ℓ₁范数而非标准ℓ₁，意味着不同特征会被赋予不同的稀疏性正则化权重。未来可推广至矩阵分解等其他非线性模型，但当前结果局限于无穷小初始化和连续梯度流，实际中梯度下降下SIM的精确不变性可能失效（曲线型SIM情况），这一差距是理论与实践之间的重要开放问题。

Abstract: We study the gradient flow dynamics of diagonal linear networks for regression tasks under infinitesimal initialization. Extending Theorem 1 from Pesme & Flammarion (2023), we generalize the analysis to both deep diagonal linear networks and a broader class of two-layer diagonal linear networks (as defined in Definition 4.1). Specifically, we demonstrate that the training trajectories of these models can be equivalently characterized by the proposed Algorithm 1. We further prove that this algorithm converges to the solution of a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm minimization problem. As a result, we establish that the implicit bias of both network architectures corresponds to a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm in the regime of infinitesimal initialization. Additionally, we provide insights into the underlying mechanisms governing these dynamics by identifying the Structural Invariant Manifold (SIM) (Zhao et al., 2026) as the key geometric structure that shapes the learning process.

</details>


### [155] [Generating Developable 3D Molecules via Pocket-Conditioned Diffusion and Property-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12349)
*Ruoxi Gao,Jiangweizhi Peng,Ziqi Chen,Frazier N. Baker,David C. Kombo,John L. Kane Jr.,Andrew A. Scholte,Yi Li,Matthew J. LaMarche,Luigi I. Iconaru,Hans-Peter Biemann,Mingyi Hong,Xia Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出conDitar-dev框架，这是一个基于条件扩散模型的结构化药物设计（SBDD）方法，由三个模块组成：msPRL（多尺度口袋表示学习）、conDitar（口袋条件扩散模型）和paOPT（生成时ADMET性质优化）。该框架能够生成具有强结合亲和力和良好ADMET性质的可开发性配体分子，在新构建的CDH基准上超越了现有SOTA方法，并通过PD-L1和CSF1R靶点的实验验证获得了具有纳摩尔级和低微摩尔级活性的化合物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的SBDD方法存在三个主要局限：(1) 口袋与配体表示学习耦合，导致扩散迭代过程中误差累积放大；(2) 仅在原子级别建模口袋-配体相互作用，忽略了残基级别的高阶结构信息；(3) 过度关注结合亲和力而忽视ADMET等可开发性性质。此外，广泛使用的CrossDocked2020基准包含大量非人类靶点，对人类疾病药物开发的转化价值有限。

Method: 框架由三个核心模块组成：(1) **msPRL**：通过自监督去噪预训练学习口袋的多尺度（原子级+残基级）表示，采用GVP（几何向量感知器）结合门控机制融合两个尺度的信息；(2) **conDitar**：以固定的口袋嵌入为条件，通过E(3)等变条件扩散模型同时生成配体原子坐标和类型，建模原子内、原子-口袋、原子-残基三种相互作用；(3) **paOPT**：训练免费的即插即用优化器，将扩散过程中的高斯噪声和Gumbel噪声轨迹作为优化变量，通过零阶梯度估计进行迭代优化以改善ADMET性质。此外，作者构建了仅含人类靶点的新基准数据集CDH。

Result: 在CDH基准上：conDitar的平均Vina D得分达-8.85 kcal/mol，比最佳基线提升7.4%，HA%达62.2%（最佳）。conDitar-dev在五个ADMET性质上比conDitar提升最高达73%（致癌性从0.22降至0.06），同时保持相近的结合亲和力。结构稳定性、3D几何分布和药物发现过滤器通过率均表现优异。**实验验证**：针对PD-L1，两个直接生成的配体SPR测得KD分别为3.49和3.75 μM；针对CSF1R，基于生成的配体进行hit expansion获得IC50低至200 nM的选择性抑制剂，泛靶命中率仅1.1-2.4%（参考化合物为11.1%）。

Conclusion: conDitar-dev成功统一了口袋条件生成与性质优化，将可开发性考量前移到初始药物设计阶段，弥合了结合驱动设计与可开发性优化之间的鸿沟。实验验证表明该框架不仅在计算层面表现优异，还能生成真正具有生物活性的可开发分子。局限性包括：当前仅支持单目标ADMET优化、缺乏合成路径规划、未考虑配体选择性。未来方向包括多目标优化、合成感知生成、化学物理知识融入等。

Abstract: Drug discovery and development is time-consuming and resource-intensive, motivating computational approaches such as diffusion models for de novo drug design. Many such models follow the structure-based drug design (SBDD) paradigm, generating molecules to fit a target binding pocket. However, existing diffusion-based SBDD methods typically couple pocket and ligand representation learning, model interactions only at the atom level, and prioritize binding affinity over other developability properties. Here, we introduce conDitar-dev, a conditional diffusion-based SBDD framework for generating ligands with strong binding affinities and favorable ADMET properties. It consists of three modules: msPRL, a pretrained multi-scale pocket representation learning module; conDitar, a pocket-conditioned diffusion model guided by msPRL representations; and paOPT, a generation-time method for optimizing ligand developability. On a newly curated benchmark of human disease targets, conDitar outperforms state-of-the-art SBDD baselines, achieving an average binding score of -8.85 kcal/mol. Across five ADMET properties, conDitar-dev improves performance by up to 73% over conDitar. To further validate the abilities of conDitar-dev to generate developable molecules, we have applied it to two validated druggable targets: programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) and colony-stimulating factor 1 receptor (CSF1R) proteins. Top-ranked generatively designed molecules and their analogs have been experimentally synthesized and biologically tested. Two molecules generated directly by conDitar-dev for PD-L1 exhibited SPR-derived $K_D$ values of 3.49 and 3.75 $\mu$M, respectively. Hit expansion based on conDitar-dev-designed molecules identified selective CSF1R inhibitors with IC$_{50}$ values as low as 200 nM, while also uncovering opportunities for drug repositioning.

</details>


### [156] [Reducing information dependency does not cause training data privacy. Adversarially non-robust features do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12354)
*Rasmus Torp,Shailen K. Smith,Adam Breuer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"减少训练数据信息依赖性（如互信息、HSIC）可防御模型反演攻击（MIA）图像重建"的主流观点，提出训练数据隐私实际上由对抗性非鲁棒特征（adversarially non-robust features）驱动，并基于此提出新防御方法 AT-AT（Anti Adversarial Training），在多个数据集和架构上取得优于现有方法的防御效果和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流隐私防御理论（如 MID、BiDO、TL-DMI）认为训练数据隐私泄露源于模型对训练输入的信息依赖（信息瓶颈、HSIC 等度量），因此通过减少此类依赖来防御 MIA 重建。但这一理论基础的正确性缺乏系统验证，且近期工作表明对抗鲁棒性与隐私之间存在矛盾关系，因此需要厘清真正驱动 MIA 泄露的因素。

Method: 三项关键实验：(1) 测量主流防御的 HSIC 与防御效果的关系；(2) 在标签置换（permuted labels）下训练完全记忆模型观察其 MIA 鲁棒性；(3) 使用 Lasso 类技术删除 97%+ 训练像素后训练模型，观察 MIA 是否仍能重建。回归分析（OLS 和 Beta 回归）建立了泄露率与对抗鲁棒精度之间的线性关系（R²≈0.93–0.95）。最后提出 AT-AT——通过反向利用对抗训练范式（奖励而非惩罚对抗扰动中暴露的非鲁棒特征），并以随机对照实验（n=10）验证因果性。

Result: 三项反例实验均反驳了信息依赖假说：(1) BiDO 等方法在减少 HSIC 后防御效果反而更差；(2) 完全记忆的模型对 MIA 重建鲁棒；(3) 97% 像素被遮蔽的模型仍可被有效重建。隐私泄露率与对抗鲁棒精度呈强线性相关，泄露率每降低 1pp 对应 ϵ=0.0025 时鲁棒精度下降 0.31pp，ϵ=0.0005 时下降 5.4pp。AT-AT 在 FaceScrub ResNet-152 上将重建率从 84% 降至 6.5%（77× 泄露优势比下降，p<10⁻¹⁶），并在所有数据集/架构/攻击下均优于 7 种基线防御。

Conclusion: 训练数据 MIA 隐私并非由信息依赖驱动，而是由对抗性非鲁棒特征（人类不可感知但可泛化的特征）驱动；现有隐私防御实际上是无意中将模型推向非鲁棒特征以获得表面防御效果。这一发现推翻了该领域长期以来的理论假设，揭示了隐私-鲁棒性之间新的权衡关系，并为隐私学习提供了可解释的机制基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we challenge the prevailing view that information dependency (including rote memorization) drives training data exposure to image reconstruction attacks. We show that extensive exposure can persist without rote memorization and is instead caused by a tunable connection to adversarial robustness. We begin by presenting three surprising results: (1) recent defenses that inhibit reconstruction by Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs), which evaluate leakage under an idealized attacker, do not reduce standard measures of information dependency (HSIC); (2) models that maximally memorize their training datasets remain robust to MIA reconstruction; and (3) models trained without seeing 97% of the training pixels, where recent information-theoretic bounds give arbitrarily strong privacy guarantees under standard assumptions, can still be devastatingly reconstructed by MIA. To explain these findings, we provide causal evidence that privacy under MIA arises from what the adversarial examples literature calls ``non-robust'' features (generalizable but imperceptible and unstable features). We further show that recent MIA defenses obtain their privacy improvements by unintentionally shifting models toward such features. To establish this causal relationship, we introduce Anti Adversarial Training (AT-AT), a training regime that intentionally learns non-robust features to obtain both superior reconstruction defense and higher accuracy than state-of-the-art defenses. Our results revise the prevailing understanding of training data exposure and reveal a new privacy-robustness tradeoff.

</details>


### [157] [Same Loss, Same Noise, Opposite Schedules: Noise Structure and Optimizer Normalization Jointly Determine Whether Learning-Rate Cooldown Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12360)
*Subham Singh,Ashutosh Mishra,Subha Raut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在大模型预训练常用的WSD（warmup-stable-decay）学习率调度背景下，研究一个未解之谜：为什么cooldown（退火）阶段在某些设置下显著降低最终损失，而在另一些设置下毫无帮助。论文给出了一个基于两种性质联合作用的可证明解释：梯度噪声的结构（乘性vs加性）以及优化器是否对更新做归一化。在强凸目标与乘性噪声下，SGD以常数学习率几何收敛，无需cooldown；而相同目标与噪声下，signSGD与归一化方法停留在Θ(η²)的噪声地板上，只有将学习率驱动到零才能到达极小值；任何加性分量都会重新为所有方法引入地板。论文在二次型上精确求解signSGD的平稳律得到闭式地板常数，在(L0,L1)-光滑性下证明局部分离形式，并通过尺度不变性论证扩展到d>1维的归一化SGD，建立了动量与重尾噪声下的鲁棒性，最后在真实分类任务上以实测梯度噪声演示了噪声机制诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSD调度已成为大语言模型预训练的默认选择，但其实践效果——特别是退火阶段是否真正降低最终损失——缺乏理论解释。退火是last-iterate效应（只改善最终迭代而非最佳迭代），这恰好是非凸优化理论中主流的"对迭代取最小"与"随机采样迭代"保证所无法捕捉的。即使是SGD的精细last-iterate分析也只针对加性噪声区域。现有的凸last-iterate界能很好复现实际调度的形状，但同样处理的是加性型噪声下的（次）梯度方法。当损失与噪声保持不变时，仅改变优化器就能改变cooldown是否有用——现有理论无法预测这一点。论文旨在回答一个更狭窄但可获得精确答案的问题：在固定损失与噪声下，优化器本身是否决定cooldown是否有效？若是，决定性性质是什么？

Method: 方法层面包含几个关键设计：(1) 采用Monzio Compagnoni等(2026)定义的仿射方差噪声模型——噪声方差按σ₀² + σ₁²‖∇f‖²界定，其中σ₁²项与梯度成比例并在极小值处消失（对应过参数化模型的插值区域）；(2) 在可分二次型f(x) = ½∑λᵢxᵢ²上严格分析各优化器的行为；(3) 对SGD推导精确的二阶矩递推m_{t+1} = r(η_t)m_t（纯乘性噪声），证明最优常数调度η★=1/(λ(1+σ₁²))；(4) 对signSGD，利用Lemma 1（符号正确率对所有噪声律均与迭代无关）建立显式可解的格点游走模型，通过半格上的细致平衡精确求解其平稳分布，得到闭式地板常数c(φ,p)；(5) 通过(L0,L1)-光滑性局部定理（Proposition 4&5）将分离性推广到非二次凸函数；(6) 利用尺度不变性引理（Lemma 2）将地板扩展到d>1维的归一化SGD；(7) 证明动量下的SGD仍自退火，而带符号/均方根归一化的动量方法保留Θ(1)更新量级，地板持续存在；(8) 验证重尾噪声下signSGD地板Θ(η²)仍成立（无需矩条件）；(9) 在真实数据上通过回归per-microbatch梯度方差对‖∇f‖²的比值直接测量噪声结构。

Result: 主要结果可概括为：(1) SGD自退火：纯乘性噪声下二阶矩服从精确乘性递推，无加性项；最优固定步长为η★=1/(λ(1+σ₁²))，几何收敛，WSD最优cooldown分数为0；(2) 归一化方法不能自退火：signSGD的单步恰为±η，停在Θ(η²)地板，必须退火；任何加性分量（σ₀>0）为所有方法恢复η相关地板；(3) 闭式地板常数c(φ,p)及均匀初始化平均值c̄(p)=1/3 + q/(2p-1) + q/(2p-1)²，与仿真精度达0.7%内吻合；(4) 表格1给出按噪声结构与优化器划分的最优调度分类：乘性噪声下SGD用常数率、归一化方法需退火；加性/混合噪声下所有方法均受益于退火；(5) d=20维各向异性二次型仿真显示：纯乘性噪声下cooldown严格伤害SGD（约6个数量级恶化）、却分别提升signSGD与归一化SGD；(6) 局部(L0,L1)定理确认了分离性在非二次凸函数上的局部保持；(7) AdamW在乘性噪声下也呈η^2.00地板，但ϵ-damping决定其从归一化行为向类SGD自退火行为的转变；(8) 重尾Cauchy噪声下signSGD地板仍呈η^2.00、闭式常数匹配，证明结论对矩条件无要求；(9) 真实数据（digits数据集两层ReLU网络）测量到的噪声log-log斜率≈0.56，落在多乘性区域；SGD最优cooldown=0、cooldown=0.95时损失恶化2.4×；Adam类似（恶化3.5×），仅signSGD受益于退火。表格2的闭式vs仿真常数对比和表格3的WSD扫描cf★与增益数据全面支持理论预测。

Conclusion: 论文得出明确结论：学习率退火是否有用是梯度噪声结构与优化器是否做归一化联合决定的属性。在乘性噪声下SGD自退火偏向常数学习率，而基于符号与归一化的方法停留在η²地板上，只有退火能消除；任何加性分量都会恢复使所有方法都受益于退火的共同地板。分离性在(L0,L1)几何、高维、动量与重尾噪声下均成立，并在非凸网络与真实数据上得到验证。论文明确指出该机制不解释的范畴：实际预训练中观察到的大约0.2-0.35的内部cooldown分数（即非0非1的中间值）超出了平稳分布层面可解释的范畴——指向非平稳性、特征学习或有限数据效应才是其来源。作者把论文定位为"分离而非完整预测"：它清晰回答"是否"问题，将"形状"问题（最优退火曲线）作为开放方向留待未来工作。

Abstract: The cooldown phase of a warmup-stable-decay (WSD) learning-rate schedule, now a default in large-model pretraining, lowers the final training loss in some settings and does nothing in others. We give a provable account of which case obtains, and it turns on two properties together: the structure of the gradient noise and whether the optimizer normalizes its update. On a strongly convex objective with multiplicative (gradient-proportional) noise, stochastic gradient descent contracts geometrically at a constant learning rate, so cooldown has nothing to improve. Under the same objective and noise, sign-based and normalized methods, the standard surrogates for adaptive optimizers, settle on a noise floor of order $\eta^2$ and reach the minimizer only as the learning rate is driven to zero; any additive noise then reinstates a floor for every method. The mechanism is elementary: an SGD step shrinks in proportion to the gradient and so anneals itself, whereas a normalized step keeps unit scale and cannot. We solve the signSGD stationary law on the quadratic exactly and obtain the floor constant in closed form, prove a local form of the dissociation under $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness, extend the floor to normalized SGD in dimension d>1 by a scale-invariance argument, and establish robustness to momentum and heavy-tailed noise. Simulation confirms every prediction, and we demonstrate the resulting noise-regime diagnostic on a real classification task with directly measured gradient noise. The mechanism explains whether cooldown helps; the interior cooldown fraction used at scale lies outside stationary landscape-and-noise geometry.

</details>


### [158] [SinAE: A Single-Architecture Flow-Matching Autoencoder for Cross-Domain Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12380)
*Yuxuan Ren,Fan Yang,Jianhua Yao,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SinAE，一种基于普通Transformer的流匹配自编码器，统一处理分子、晶体、蛋白质三类三维原子结构生成任务。其核心创新是将重建负担从编码器转移至迭代式流匹配解码器，并使用统一的训练目标，无需任何等变、图或领域专用算子。在所有三个领域均达到近无损重建（分子/晶体亚毫埃RMSD，蛋白质约0.01-0.02 Å），并在生成任务上取得了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分子、晶体、蛋白质的3D原子结构生成流程各自独立：分子用等变消息传递网络，晶体需处理周期性边界条件，蛋白质依赖残基级SE(3)帧或三角注意力。各领域数据集规模有限（小分子QM9仅134k，晶体MP-20仅45k），跨域训练可缓解数据稀缺。但现有跨域潜空间自编码器均存在两大缺陷：（1）重建保真度不足（ADiT在QM9上仅94.6%结构匹配率），次埃级坐标偏差会导致键长键角超出物理合理范围；（2）需要为不同领域设计专属损失组合（距离损失、键类型损失、FAPE损失等），扩展领域即面临损失工程问题。作者认为缺乏一个同时具备无损性、架构领域通用性、训练目标统一性三项特性的方法。

Method: SinAE包含两个核心组件：（1）流匹配自编码器——编码器为普通Transformer，将(X, A)（坐标+原子类型）映射为逐token的潜变量z∈R^{N'×d}（周期系统增加3个晶格token）；解码器同样为非等变Transformer，通过条件流匹配以x1预测训练目标迭代重建结构，采用双交叉注意力输出头分别预测坐标和原子类型；（2）潜空间DiT先验——使用标准Diffusion Transformer学习高斯潜空间分布，配合分类器自由引导实现按域采样。训练损失为统一的L_coord + λ_atom·L_atom + λ_KL·L_KL（λ_atom=0.1, λ_KL=10⁻⁶），无领域专属权重调节。随机SO(3)旋转增强提供旋转鲁棒性。解码器RMSD在NFE≈6时即饱和，实际使用NFE=10。

Result: （1）重建保真度：QM9上RMSD 0.0007 Å（联合训练达0.0002 Å），MP-20上0.0041 Å（联合训练达0.0017 Å），GEOM-Drugs上0.0006 Å，分子/晶体匹配率均达100%；蛋白质Cα-RMSD在CASP14/CASP15上为0.010-0.014 Å，比ProteinAE提升10-20倍；（2）生成性能：QM9上PB Valid达0.97，GEOM-Drugs上达0.93（超越ADiT的0.86和SemlaFlow的0.88）；MP-20上S.U.N.=5.4%/M.S.U.N.=30.2%，均为最佳；蛋白质无条件生成在guidance温度0.25下达Designability 0.96+Diversity 278，超越ProteinAE；序列-结构协同设计在长度200时scRMSD=4.44 Å，优于MultiFlow和ProteinGenerator；（3）跨域迁移：QM9+MP-20联合训练严格改善两个领域的重建与生成指标，t-SNE显示两个域在潜空间中自然分离且各域内部聚类具有化学意义；（4）解码器轨迹分析：ODE早期步骤处理原子空间定位（t≈0.8前RMSD即收敛），后期步骤进行局部能量优化（力场能量下降超10个数量级），且能量从未作为监督信号。

Conclusion: SinAE证明：通过将重建负担转移至迭代式流匹配解码器并采用统一训练目标，单一普通Transformer架构即可实现分子、晶体、蛋白质的近无损重建与高质量生成，无需等变、图或领域专用模块。跨域联合训练带来严格的正迁移，证实共享原子潜空间可同时受益于不同化学群体。该方法为跨域原子结构生成提供了一条简化、可扩展的路径。局限：蛋白质评估限于骨架级，全原子建模与跨三域单一checkpoint训练留待未来工作。

Abstract: Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its own graph, equivariant, or frame-based architecture. Cross-domain training would mitigate per-domain data scarcity, but direct generation in 3D coordinate space cannot easily handle the heterogeneous structural priors of all three domains, and no prior latent autoencoder is simultaneously lossless and architecturally general across all three. We introduce SinAE, a single-architecture flow-matching autoencoder for molecules, crystals, and proteins, with vanilla Transformer encoder and decoder and no equivariant, graph, or domain-specific operators. Rather than requiring the encoder to capture fine-grained geometry, SinAE shifts the reconstruction burden into an iterative flow-matching decoder, achieving near-lossless reconstruction across domains and reducing reconstruction errors by orders of magnitude relative to prior latent baselines. The same per-token latent supports a standard Diffusion Transformer prior that reaches strong performance on molecular, crystal, and protein generation benchmarks. Joint molecule--crystal training strictly improves both domains, providing direct evidence of cross-domain transfer through a shared atomic latent. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [159] [Differentiable Clone-Structured Causal Graphs for End-to-End Cognitive Map Learning from Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12382)
*Arash Nikzad,Sasan Sarbishegi,Ali Dasmeh,Muhammad Asif,Parsa Gharavi,Erik Husom,Sagar Sen,Andrew B. Lehr,Olivier Penacchio,Ana Clemente,Tristan M. Stöber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 gradCSCG，将原本基于期望最大化（EM）训练的克隆结构因果图（CSCG）算法重新表述为完全可微的模块，并与 VQ-VAE 感知前端耦合，实现了从原始图像序列端到端学习认知地图的深度学习流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 CSCG 算法作为海马体的规范模型，能够从混叠观测中学习可解释的认知地图，但其依赖于预定义的离散符号字母表和 EM 训练范式，难以与神经网络模块结合，无法处理高维原始图像输入。本文旨在突破这一瓶颈，使 CSCG 成为可组合的深度学习构件。

Method: 1) 将 CSCG 的 HMM 前向算法重写为对数空间中的可微分计算图，使用 Adam 梯度下降直接优化初始状态 logits 和动作条件转移 logits；2) 设计"软发射"前向传播，用 VQ-VAE 编码器产生的码本软后验 ρ_t 替代硬发射 log B，使拓扑目标可通过梯度反向传播到编码器；3) 提出损失平衡机制——长度归一化、权重退火、多样性惩罚和防坍缩保护——以稳定 VQ-VAE 与 gradCSCG 的联合训练；4) 采用三阶段训练策略：VQ-VAE 预热→联合训练→冻结编码器精调。

Result: 在四个强混叠的 MNIST 网格世界（aliased、corridors、room、two_rooms，规模最大达 13×9、87 个位置、151 个潜在状态）中，端到端流水线成功从原始图像中恢复底层邻接图：所有环境下地图召回率均为 1.00，地图 F1 为 0.98–1.00，状态到位置纯度为 0.95–1.00，动作预测准确率为 0.97–1.00。梯度训练在符号化网格世界中也复现了原始 EM-CSCG 的结果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了 CSCG 可作为可组合的深度学习模块：通过可微分重构，CSCG 保留了其稀疏的、解决混叠的克隆结构，同时将感知委托给学习的前端。主要局限在于克隆预算为静态超参数（无法动态招募）、读出依赖 Viterbi 路径最小流量阈值、实验局限于静态 2D 网格世界。未来方向包括动态克隆招募机制、与 RNN 耦合以处理动态信息、以及应用于更丰富的视觉场景。

Abstract: How can an agent build a structured map of its world from nothing but an ongoing sequence of raw sensory input and its own movements, especially when natural variation means exact sensory patterns rarely repeat? The Clone-Structured Causal Graph algorithm (CSCG), a normative hippocampus model, shows how an interpretable map can be learned from aliased observations. However, CSCG requires a predefined discrete alphabet, and its expectation-maximization formulation is not easily combined with existing neural network modules, preventing the end-to-end processing of raw image sequences. We remove this barrier by reformulating CSCG as a single, fully differentiable module, gradCSCG, and coupling it to a learned vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) perceptual front-end. A soft emission forward pass allows the map-learning objective to flow back into perception, while a set of loss-balancing mechanisms mitigates module collapse during joint training. We demonstrate, first, that gradient training reproduces CSCG's results on original symbolic grid worlds by recovering room topology from heavily aliased observations. Second, we show that map recovery remains robust on MNIST image sequences, where each visit to a location yields a newly sampled image of its assigned digit. Across four heavily aliased environments, the end-to-end pipeline successfully uncovers the underlying adjacency graph with high edge precision and recall, directly from visual input. This work provides a proof of principle that CSCG can serve as a composable building block in a deep learning architecture.

</details>


### [160] [ReDiTT: Retrieval Augmented Conditional Diffusion Transformers for Asynchronous Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12391)
*Saiyue Lyu,Zhitian Zhang,Ruizhi Deng,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReDiTT 提出了一种基于检索增强的条件扩散Transformer模型，用于异步时间序列预测（预测下一个事件的间隔时间和事件类型）。通过在潜在空间中从记忆库检索结构相似的序列作为参考条件，通过交叉注意力整合到扩散Transformer中，为长时预测提供全局结构引导，稳定长期预测并提升样本多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步时间序列（事件流）预测在事件驱动系统、医疗监控、金融、用户行为建模等领域至关重要。现有方法面临两大挑战：(1) 经典自回归与基于似然的时序点过程模型依赖强参数假设或马尔可夫动力学，难以处理复杂异步时间序列中的丰富全局结构、长程依赖和混合连续/离散观测；(2) 现有的扩散模型在长时预测中缺乏有效的全局条件信号，预测容易漂移到训练分布中的通用高概率模式，难以保持轨迹特定的时间结构和细粒度事件演化。因此需要一种能提供全局结构引导的非自回归生成方法。

Method: ReDiTT 在VAE潜在空间中工作，结合了三个核心组件：
1. **潜在空间表示**：使用预训练VAE编码器 $E_\phi$ 将填充到固定长度 $N$ 的事件序列编码为潜在表示 $\mathbf{z} \in \mathbb{R}^{N \times d}$。
2. **异步矩阵值插值的条件流匹配（CFM）**：定义 $\mathbf{z}_s = \mathbf{A}(s)\mathbf{z} + (\mathbf{I} - \mathbf{A}(s))\boldsymbol{\epsilon}$，其中 $\mathbf{A}(s)$ 是对角矩阵，其逐事件噪声调度使得后续事件更早注入噪声，促使模型在更强噪声下学习去噪和预测尾部事件。
3. **检索增强条件化**：构建潜在token记忆库 $\mathcal{B}$，训练和推理时均检索每个序列的top-$k$ 近邻，通过扩散Transformer（DiT）块内的交叉注意力模块整合检索到的参考序列，提供全局结构引导。
推理时通过ODE求解器求解生成过程，将已知历史作为初始条件，对未来事件进行去噪采样。

Result: 在七个真实世界数据集上进行了广泛实验，涵盖下一事件预测和长时预测任务。ReDiTT 取得了 state-of-the-art（最先进）性能，显著优于现有方法，包括：(1) 经典TPP模型；(2) 基于自回归的神经TPP模型；(3) 现有的扩散异步时间序列模型（如Add and Thin、ADiff4TPP等）。检索增强的条件化有效稳定了长时预测，提升了样本多样性，且对各种长度的预测窗口均表现稳健。

Conclusion: ReDiTT 是首个将检索机制引入异步时间序列扩散预测的框架，通过在潜在空间中检索相似序列作为参考条件并通过交叉注意力整合，成功解决了长时预测中缺乏全局引导信号的问题。该方法在七个数据集上实现SOTA性能，验证了检索增强条件化对扩散Transformer在异步事件序列预测中的有效性，为非自回归的异步时间序列生成提供了新范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: We present a diffusion based model for asynchronous time series prediction, where the goal is to predict the next inter event time and event type. To address the inherent uncertainty of future events, we introduce ReDiTT, a retrieval augmented conditional diffusion transformer that operates in latent space. ReDiTT retrieves structurally similar latent sequences from a memory bank during both training and inference and incorporates them as reference conditions through cross attention. This retrieval based conditioning allows the model to attend to relevant temporal dynamics and provides global structural guidance for generation. As a result, ReDiTT stabilizes long horizon forecasting and improves sample diversity. Experiments on seven real world datasets demonstrate state of the art performance on next event prediction and long horizon forecasting. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [161] [Mechanical Analysis of Parachute Suspension Line Deployment with Binding Tapes Using PINN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12409)
*Xiang Zhao,Ronghui Quan,Yaqi Xiao,Junlin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的降落伞悬挂绳展开过程力学建模方法，通过傅里叶特征编码与残差多层感知机网络学习时空坐标到力学状态的映射，实现了对任意时空点悬挂绳张力的快速精确预测，并系统研究了约束带数量、位置及断裂强度对悬挂绳动态张力的影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降落伞悬挂绳的拉出和伸直过程是伞衣充气前的关键阶段，该过程极短且载荷变化复杂。现有研究主要采用常微分方程（ODE）数值积分方法计算绳索张力，但该方法无法快速获取绳上任意位置的张力值，且对带有约束带的降落伞系统缺乏全过程的力学分析，难以指导悬挂绳的优化设计，可能导致绳索断裂和伞衣损伤。

Method: 1. 建立单颗粒子力模型：将悬挂绳离散为多个弹簧-阻尼-质量单元，分析弹簧力、阻尼力、重力和气动力；2. 建立连续拉出模型：结合Pflanz公式和高斯过冲模型描述引导伞充气过程，采用变质量微分方程描述绳索拉出动力学；3. 构建PINN架构：采用傅里叶特征编码（96维）和残差MLP（4个残差块，323,335个参数），三分支输出部署状态α、张力T和竖向速度vy；4. 扩展含约束带模型：将6条均匀分布的约束带作为离散约束边界，分松弛拉出（A阶段）和张力断裂（B阶段）两个子阶段交替进行，引入解析max()修正策略补偿神经网络的平滑效应；5. 损失函数采用加权归一化MSE，对张力赋予最高权重20，部署状态权重10，速度权重2。

Result: PINN方法在任意时空坐标的单次查询仅需0.77微秒（可实现每秒超10万次查询），相比ODE方法（154.7秒）具有压倒性的推理速度优势；关键物理量的相对误差均低于1%（引导伞充气峰值张力误差0.5%，峰值猛拉载荷误差0.4%）；约束带数量与伸直冲击力呈近似线性负相关，每增加一条约束带可使速度差Δv减少约0.44 m/s、冲击力降低约50 N；约束带位置对单次断裂张力脉冲幅值影响较小（62 N范围内），但最小间距应不小于2 m；存在临界断裂强度F_crit（约2500–3000 N），推荐工程设计中断裂力设为F_crit的50%–80%（1250–2000 N）。

Conclusion: 该PINN模型能够精确预测悬挂绳任意时空点的部署状态、张力和速度，在关键力学特征位置具有良好精度；增加约束带数量可有效降低伸直速度差并缓解瞬态冲击载荷，但过多或强度过高的约束带会显著增加拉出阻力，阻碍完全展开；含约束带工况下绳粒子张力变化率显著增大，对悬挂绳材料的动态力学性能提出更高要求；未来研究可扩展至整个减速下降过程的张力计算并引入侧风参数。

Abstract: Parachutes are widely utilized in aviation, aerospace and lifesaving missions. As the initial stage of parachute deployment, suspension line extraction and straightening directly determines the smooth implementation of subsequent inflation procedures. This ultra-short process involves intricate dynamic load variations. Most existing studies adopt numerical integration of ordinary differential equations to calculate line tension, yet this method fails to rapidly acquire tension values at arbitrary positions along suspension lines. This paper develops a physics-informed neural network (PINN) algorithm for tension prediction during line extraction and straightening, which outperforms traditional integration methods in both computational efficiency and numerical accuracy. Furthermore, the regulatory law of binding tape parameters on line dynamic tension is investigated. Comparative validations against flight test data and conventional numerical results verify the reliability and effectiveness of the proposed PINN framework.

</details>


### [162] [PolarBM: Complex-valued Boltzmann Machine for Modeling Audio Signals in Polar and Log-polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12417)
*Toru Nakashika,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了PolarBM及其对数极坐标变体LogPolarBM，这是一类在极坐标（幅度-相位表示）下自然处理复数变量的玻尔兹曼机，并通过引入限制性变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM，在音频信号建模任务中取得了优于常规复数RBM和深度学习方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理和工程数据（如音频频谱）天然以复数形式存在，但传统机器学习方法通常丢弃相位信息或将实部虚部视为独立实值变量，破坏了幅度与相位之间固有的结构依赖关系。现有复数BM（如DUBM、CAPBM、复数RBM、GVM-RBM）均存在简化假设，无法准确建模音频信号中相位对幅度的依赖特性。

Method: 1. **PolarBM**：在复数极坐标下定义能量函数，通过对角负项保证概率密度收敛；其条件概率密度使相位服从von Mises分布且其集中参数正比于幅度，幅度服从Rician分布，二者非独立。2. **LogPolarBM**：在PolarBM基础上加入对数幅度项（借鉴GammaBM），得到更灵活的功率加权非中心复高斯（PW-NCCG）分布，其幅度边际分布可退化为Rice、Nakagami、非中心chi及Gamma分布。3. **PolarRBM/LogPolarRBM**：引入二值隐藏单元的二部图结构，隐藏单元可控制可见单元的均值与方差，LogPolarRBM中隐藏单元还控制PW-NCCG的形状参数α。4. 采用复数Adam优化器与对比散度（CD）训练，并提出拉盖尔函数比值的解析近似以高效计算可见单元期望。

Result: 在日语语音数据库（ATR Set B，FTK说话人）的语音重建实验中：LogPolarRBM在PESQ（4.00）、STOI（0.995）、UTMOS（3.93）、MWARP-Q（4.69）等客观指标及MOS（4.51/4.65）主观评分上均达到最优，显著优于CRBM、RBM-DUBM、GVM-RBM、HiFi-GAN、CVAE、VAE和WORLD等基线方法。LogPolarRBM(a)采用近似期望计算后，RTF从86.3降至0.12，性能几乎无损。MOS主观评测与自然语音无统计显著差异（Welch t检验，5%显著性水平）。

Conclusion: PolarBM/LogPolarBM及其限制性变体是复数域玻尔兹曼机的自然推广，通过显式建模幅度-相位依赖关系和引入对数幅度尺度，生成的PW-NCCG分布可统一多种经典分布族。实验证明这些浅层模型在语音表示质量上超越了多种深度学习方法，未来工作包括将模型扩展至深度架构并应用于语音识别、语音合成、源分离及图像处理、通信系统等其他复数数据领域。

Abstract: Although vast amounts of data, such as audio signal spectra, are naturally represented using complex numbers, conventional machine learning methods often simplify complex-domain problems by employing frameworks designed for real-valued variables. While this simplification offers computational benefits, it discards structural information regarding the inherent relationship between amplitude and phase. In this paper, we propose a novel Boltzmann machine (BM), named PolarBM, capable of naturally handling complex-valued variables in the polar coordinate (i.e., an amplitude-phase representation). PolarBM defines a probability density function for complex variables in which the phase explicitly depends on the amplitude, thereby capturing the physically important relationships of complex-valued signals. Furthermore, to process audio signals in accordance with human auditory perception, we propose LogPolarBM, which models amplitude on a logarithmic scale. This extension yields a flexible conditional probability density function, a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian (PW-NCCG) distribution, whose marginal amplitude distribution encompasses the Rice, Nakagami, and noncentral chi distributions as special cases. For practical applications, we also introduce the restricted variants of these proposed models: PolarRBM and LogPolarRBM. Experimental results demonstrate that by explicitly modeling the dependency between amplitude and phase, the proposed RBMs achieve superior modeling accuracy compared to conventional models, including deep neural networks. Although our experiments focus on audio signals, the utility of the proposed BMs is not limited to audio applications; their potential extends widely across various fields of science and engineering that involve complex-valued data, such as wireless communications and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [163] [Fisher Rank Inflation: A Spectral Signature of Memorization under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12438)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Fisher秩膨胀"(Fisher Rank Inflation)现象：在标签噪声下训练深度网络时，最后一层每样本梯度散度矩阵的熵有效秩在记忆化过程中经历"膨胀-坍缩"轨迹。通过谱理论解释了这一现象：损坏标签将协方差质量注入低能量或未使用的特征方向，增加谱熵。推导了一阶留一法(LOO)归属公式，识别了损坏样本贡献更大的谱条件。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和CIFAR-10N上使用SmallCNN、ResNet18和ViT验证了该现象，表明损坏样本在峰值秩检查点的高度贡献样本中被显著富集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在标签噪声下训练时通常先学习干净结构再记忆损坏标签。现有研究多关注损失、准确率或示例遗忘，但较少从梯度谱的角度分析损坏样本如何重塑训练梯度的谱结构。本文旨在通过分析每样本最后层梯度的中心化散度矩阵的有效秩，从谱几何视角揭示记忆化过程的特征。

Method: 1. 定义Fisher梯度散度矩阵S（中心化的每样本最后层梯度协方差），使用熵有效秩er(S)作为核心度量。2. 推导有效秩关于特征值的灵敏度（Lemma 1），识别哪些方向会提升有效秩。3. 建立中心化LOO删除恒等式（Lemma 2）并推导一阶LOO展开（Lemma 3），给出归属分数A_i(S)=ḡᵢᵀB_Sḡᵢ。4. 证明Theorem 1（局部Fisher秩膨胀）：损坏梯度要么在干净梯度跨度外创建新谱方向，要么在已有rank-increasing方向放置足够能量，均可膨胀有效秩。5. 证明Theorem 2：给出损坏样本LOO贡献大于干净样本的谱充分条件。6. 证明Theorem 3：归一化谱稳定性导致LOO信号在后记忆化阶段消失。7. 实验在CIFAR-10/100（对称标签损坏）和CIFAR-10N（人类标注错误）上使用SmallCNN、ResNet18和ViT验证。

Result: 1. Fisher有效秩在训练中表现出一致的"膨胀-坍缩"轨迹，在记忆化过渡期达到峰值。2. 峰值有效秩随损坏严重度单调增长：从干净训练的28.88±1.95增至60%损坏时的97.09±1.78。3. 在峰值秩检查点，损坏样本在top-100贡献样本中显著富集：SmallCNN 96.2%、ResNet18(CIFAR-10) 95.0%、ResNet18(CIFAR-100) 92.2%、ViT 69.2%、CIFAR-10N 94.4%。4. 一阶谱归属分数A_i(S)与精确LOO在卷积模型上高度吻合（Spearman >0.96）。5. 干净难度控制实验表明记忆化的损坏样本（均值0.0751）贡献远超高损失干净样本（-0.0137），证明该现象不是识别困难样本而是识别记忆化的损坏标签。6. 秩膨胀的起始通常早于可观察的测试退化（领先时间约28-43个epoch）。7. NIR（损坏vs干净有效秩比）在所有噪声设置下保持>1。

Conclusion: Fisher秩膨胀被确立为标签噪声下记忆化的谱特征，连接了损坏样本富集、损坏严重度和从结构学习到记忆化的过渡。该现象在多种架构（CNN和ViT）、数据集（含自然人类标注错误）和噪声类型下均稳健存在，提示有效秩动力学为研究深度网络如何从学习结构过渡到拟合噪声提供了有用的视角。理论上为记忆化提供了基于谱的解释，实践上可作为记忆化的诊断信号（但非通用噪声检测器或在线停止规则）。

Abstract: Deep networks trained with label noise often learn clean structure before memorizing corrupted labels. We show that this transition leaves a spectral signature in the centered scatter of per-example last-layer gradients. Its effective rank transiently expands during memorization and contracts after corrupted labels are fit. We call this phenomenon Fisher Rank Inflation. Corrupted labels increase effective rank by injecting spectral mass into low-energy or previously unused eigendirections, increasing the entropy of the gradient spectrum. We derive a first-order leave-one-out attribution formula, identify conditions under which corrupted examples contribute more strongly than clean examples, and explain why attribution signals weaken once the normalized Fisher-gradient spectrum stabilizes. We test these predictions on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CIFAR-10N using SmallCNN, ResNet18, and Vision Transformers. Across settings, Fisher effective rank exhibits a consistent inflation--collapse trajectory aligned with memorization. At peak-rank checkpoints, corrupted examples are enriched among the highest rank-contributing samples, with top-100 noisy fractions from \(69.2\%\) to \(96.2\%\) across five-seed synthetic-corruption experiments and \(94.4\%\pm1.9\%\) on CIFAR-10N. First-order spectral attribution closely matches exact leave-one-out contributions in convolutional models and remains enriched in the Vision Transformer. Peak effective rank increases monotonically with corruption severity, from \(28.88\pm1.95\) under clean training to \(97.09\pm1.78\) at \(60\%\) corruption. In several settings, the retrospectively identified onset of rank inflation precedes observable test degradation. These results establish Fisher Rank Inflation as a spectral signature connecting corrupted-example enrichment, corruption severity, and the transition from structure learning to memorization.

</details>


### [164] [Exploring Zero-Shot Foundation Models for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12454)
*Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Roland Kwitt,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了将单变量时间序列基础模型 TimesFM 以零样本方式应用于工业多变量时间序列异常检测（MTSAD）的可行性，提出并评估了两种策略（每特征预测+阈值检测、嵌入+孤立森林），在 SWaT 基准上均未超越简单基线（F1=0.480 vs 最佳基线 0.833）。核心原因是 FM 过度擅长捕捉时序动态以至于能"适应"异常模式，导致持续性异常与正常行为不可区分；但预测误差在异常边界处可靠地出现尖峰，表明该模型更适合变化点检测而非异常检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MTSAD 方法依赖针对特定应用和数据集训练的专用模型，开发与重训练成本高、难以跨环境扩展；近年来面向单变量时间序列的基础模型已具备零样本泛化能力，但其在工业 MTSAD 上的零样本适用性尚未被充分研究，本文试图回答"未经任务特定训练的单变量时间序列 FM 能否直接检测工业多变量时间序列中的异常"。

Method: 采用 TimesFM v1.2.0 作为零样本单变量时间序列基础模型，在 SWaT 水处理基准（含 51 通道、7 天正常+4 天异常）上提出两种策略：(1) 预测式异常检测——独立地按通道将 FM 作为预测器，对未来 h=32 步进行预测，聚合各通道预测误差并以阈值 τ_error 判定异常；(2) 嵌入式异常检测——将 FM 编码器 g_θ 的中间层表示沿通道拼接为联合嵌入 z_t∈R^{C·d}，在正常数据上训练隔离森林 (IF) 进行离群点检测；窗口长度 w=100，无任何微调或任务特定训练。

Result: 两种策略在 SWaT 上均显著劣于基线：预测式方法 F1 接近零（持续异常窗口内预测误差极低，模型过度适应异常动态）；TimesFM+IF 嵌入式方法 F1=0.480，远低于 L2-norm（0.782）、PCA Error（0.833）等简单基线；PCA 投影显示正常与异常嵌入仅部分分离、重叠严重；然而预测误差在异常段进入和退出边界处出现可靠尖峰，表明 FM 对分布变化敏感，对持续性异常则因局部适应而失效。

Conclusion: 零样本单变量时间序列 FM 不足以胜任工业 MTSAD，根源不在于泛化能力不足，而在于面向预测保真度预训练的模型无法专门区分异常，应优先考虑微调、真正的多变量 FM 或更换下游检测器；同时 FM 预测误差在异常边界处的尖峰特性使其更适用于变化点检测与根因定位等任务，而非逐时间步的异常标注。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is essential for reliability and safety in domains such as industrial process monitoring and financial risk management, yet conventional approaches rely on application-specific models that are costly to train and hard to scale. Foundation Models (FMs), pre-trained on broad data with strong zero-shot generalization, have recently become available for univariate time series forecasting, raising the question of whether they can address MTSAD without task-specific training. We investigate the zero-shot application of a univariate forecasting FM, TimesFM, to industrial MTSAD on the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark, evaluating two strategies: treating the FM as a per-feature forecaster with thresholded prediction errors, and as an embedder whose intermediate representations feed standard outlier detectors. Neither of our proposed setups is competitive with established baselines; embeddings reveal only partial separation between normal and anomalous segments, insufficient for reliable detection. The cause is that the FM is too effective at capturing temporal dynamics, yielding low error even within fully anomalous windows, so persistent anomalies become indistinguishable from normal behavior. However, these observations yield valuable insights: the error peaks at anomaly boundaries, indicating FMs reliably detect distribution changes. We conclude that the proposed naive zero-shot FMs are unsuitable for MTSAD but promising for change-point detection.

</details>


### [165] [Sample Efficient Generative Optimization for Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12488)
*Sarina Kopf,Cristina Nevado,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEGO（Sample Efficient Generative Optimization），一种结合强化学习生成模型（Saturn）与贝叶斯优化代理模型（GOLLuM）的混合分子优化框架。通过锚定代理引导的内部循环生成和SMILES增强优化，SEGO在PMO基准测试中以仅1/10的oracle调用预算达到最优性能，在多参数分子对接任务中以约一半的oracle调用找到10个严格命中分子，显著提升了分子设计的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化在药物发现、材料设计和催化等领域至关重要，但面临化学空间巨大且评估预算紧张的挑战。高保真模拟和实验测量成本高昂，使得样本效率成为优化方法实用性的核心瓶颈。现有方法存在以下局限：纯生成式方法需要数百到数千次oracle评估才能找到高质量候选；纯BO方法受限于固定库和分子表示选择；混合方法要么在连续潜在空间优化面临解码困难，要么样本效率有限。这阻碍了直接基于实验反馈的分子优化活动的开展。

Method: SEGO采用双智能体+GP代理的混合架构：（1）锚定生成智能体π_Agent仅基于真实oracle反馈更新；（2）内部循环智能体π_Inner在每次BO迭代中从锚定智能体重新实例化，针对GP后验均值进行训练以生成动态候选库；（3）基于LLM嵌入的深度核GP代理（GOLLuM）对动态库进行即时特征化；（4）通过采集函数（预期改进EI）选择最有前景的候选。两个核心贡献：锚定代理引导的内部循环实现代理驱动的生成同时保留真实oracle信息；SMILES增强优化利用字符串特征器从每次oracle调用中提取额外信号。实验细节：使用100次oracle调用的严格预算（仅为先前工作的10%），在ChEMBL 33上预训练先验，10个种子运行。

Result: （1）架构消融：锚定初始化策略优于先验和连续初始化策略（Yield 57±22 vs 0和47±19）；增强优化在5倍时饱和（Yield从33±16提升至65±6）；内部循环迭代次数增加改善样本效率（20次迭代Yield达66±9）。（2）PMO基准测试：在PMO-1k上，SEGO以仅100次oracle调用达到top-1 AUC 11.86（最优），超越LICO（11.71）、MOLLEO（11.65）等使用1000次调用的方法。（3）多参数对接任务：SEGO在5个靶点（parp1、fa7、5ht1b、braf、jak2）上找到10个严格命中所需oracle调用比Saturn和GEAM减少一半以上（如parp1: 133 vs 518和743）。（4）多样性分析：SEGO存在同分异构体模式崩溃问题，通过ξ=0.05的EI调节和同分异构体过滤器可将唯一异构体数从12提升至46-52。

Conclusion: SEGO通过结合生成模型与贝叶斯优化的混合框架，在动态生成的库上实现原则化探索，显著提升了开放化学空间中的样本效率。消融实验证实代理引导的内部循环、锚定机制和SMILES增强均对性能有贡献。然而该方法存在同分异构体模式崩溃倾向，需要多样性过滤器和采集函数调节来缓解。未来工作应评估SEGO在更具挑战性的oracle（如基于物理的模拟、严格合成约束）上的表现，并引入多目标Pareto前沿优化和批量采集功能以适应高通量实验活动。尽管存在局限，SEGO所展示的样本效率和命中发现能力是迈向直接实验反馈驱动的生成式分子设计的有希望的一步。

Abstract: Molecular optimization in drug discovery, materials design, and catalysis requires searching vast chemical spaces under tight evaluation budgets, since high-fidelity oracles and experimental measurements are costly. The practical impact of an optimization method therefore hinges on its sample efficiency: how few evaluations it needs to find strong candidates. We introduce Sample Efficient Generative Optimization (SEGO), a framework for Bayesian optimization on adaptively generated molecules. In SEGO, a probabilistic surrogate model forms a hypothesis about where hits lie in chemical space, a generative model is steered to propose candidates in that region, the most promising candidate is selected via an acquisition function, and the resulting oracle call is used both to sharpen the surrogate and to anchor the generator in real reward. SEGO attains state-of-the-art performance on the practical molecular optimization (PMO) benchmark using only one tenth of the oracle calls consumed by other methods, and on a multiparameter docking task it reaches ten hits in roughly half the oracle calls of existing approaches. These gains move molecular optimization closer to campaigns driven by direct experimental feedback.

</details>


### [166] [Adversarial Attacks on Online Handwriting using Salience-based Temporal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12500)
*Yataro Tamura,Brian Kenji Iwana,Jiseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AITE（Adversarial Iterative Temporal Editing）的新型对抗攻击框架，针对在线手写识别（以笔尖轨迹时间序列表示）进行攻击。与传统基于图像的空间加性扰动不同，AITE通过在时间维度上进行点的插入和删除来生成对抗样本，并通过Grad-CAM估计的时间显著性来引导编辑位置的选择。实验表明，AITE在one-shot黑盒迁移场景中显著优于现有的空间扰动攻击方法，同时保持了手写轨迹的视觉自然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗攻击方法主要针对图像输入设计，依赖空间加性扰动。当直接应用于在线手写（本质上是以时间序列表示的笔尖轨迹）时，会引入高频抖动和明显不自然的笔画伪影，违反了人类手写固有的运动平滑性和结构规律性。此外，白盒假设在实际部署中往往不现实，因此需要研究更适合时间序列数据特性、且在黑盒迁移场景下有效的对抗攻击方法。

Method: 1. **时间编辑操作**：定义两种基本编辑操作——时间插入（在相邻两点间插入中点以保持局部连续性）和时间删除（移除非端点的时间步）；2. **AITE迭代框架**：每次迭代执行一次插入和一次删除，保持序列长度T不变，直到攻击成功或达到最大迭代次数K；3. **Grad-CAM显著性估计**：将Grad-CAM从图像领域适配到时间序列，计算每个时间步的显著性得分（不应用ReLU以保留正负贡献），并通过线性插值映射到输入长度；4. **编辑位置选择**：插入位置选择显著性最低的相邻区间中点，删除位置选择显著性最高的非端点时间步；5. **实验设计**：在Unipen（1A/1B/1C）和CASIA-OLHWDB数据集上，以1D CNN-3为代理模型，在白盒和one-shot黑盒迁移设置下评估，对比FGSM、BIM、PGD、CW、MI-FGSM、NI-FGSM和1D-TI-MI-FGSM等基线方法。

Result: 1. **白盒场景**：梯度类攻击（BIM、PGD、CW）效果最强，CW准确率降至0.69%-5.96%（Unipen）和0.71%（CASIA）；AITE的白盒效果相对较弱（如Unipen 1A为25.5%）；2. **黑盒迁移场景**：AITE在几乎所有黑盒列上均取得最低识别准确率，例如在CASIA上将1D CNN-4、BLSTM-2和Transformer的准确率分别降至52.5%、53.6%和47.9%，显著优于最强基线1D-TI-MI-FGSM（90.0%、94.2%、93.5%）；3. **视觉相似性**：AITE在L2距离和SSIM指标上与CW相当，显著优于其他基线方法，生成的对抗样本保留了手写轨迹的整体结构；4. **消融实验**：基于Grad-CAM显著性的选择策略优于基于梯度幅值的策略，攻击效果更强；5. **类敏感性分析**：单笔画字符（"1"、"5"）最难攻击，复杂字符（"6"、"8"、"9"）最易攻击；部分样本仅需单次迭代即可改变分类。

Conclusion: 本文证明时间编辑是针对在线手写识别的一种有效且现实的对抗威胁模型，特别是在one-shot黑盒迁移场景中表现优异。通过Grad-CAM显著性引导的时间编辑生成的对抗样本能够保持手写轨迹的结构和运动平滑性，同时跨模型架构（包括CNN、RNN和Transformer）具有强迁移性。未来工作将扩展到词和句子级在线手写识别，开展人工感知评估，并研究针对时间编辑攻击的防御方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models for online handwriting recognition have been shown effective and are increasingly deployed in practical applications. However, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks is still a challenge. Existing adversarial methods are predominantly designed for image-based inputs and typically rely on additive spatial perturbations. When applied to online handwriting, which is inherently represented as a time series of pen trajectories, such perturbations often introduce high-frequency jitter and visibly unnatural stroke artifacts. In this work, we propose a novel adversarial attack framework for online handwriting recognition based on salience-guided temporal editing. Instead of adding noise, the proposed method generates adversarial examples by inserting and deleting points at time steps selected according to temporal salience, preserving the shape and smoothness of the original handwriting. Temporal salience is estimated using gradient-based activation mapping, which guides edits toward time steps that strongly support the original class prediction. We evaluate the proposed approach on the Unipen and CASIA-OLHWDB datasets under both white-box and one-shot black-box attack settings. Experimental results demonstrate that while conventional image-based attacks achieve strong white-box performance, they exhibit poor transferability across models. In contrast, the proposed temporal editing attack achieves stronger one-shot black-box transferability while preserving the visual structure of the handwriting. These results indicate that temporal editing is a relevant threat model for online handwriting recognition, particularly in one-shot black-box transfer settings.

</details>


### [167] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.
