Conclusion: 论文得出以下结论:IoAT代表了IoT从被动设备网络向主动信息物理智能网络的范式转变,核心不在于叠加LLM接口,而在于分布式智能体推理与物理传感-执行-记忆-数字孪生-反馈控制-自适应重规划之间的耦合。该耦合既使高层意图可转化为可执行工作流,又为物理AI提供了可扩展的网络化形态(将具身分布至连接的设备、边缘智能体、云端智能体与人类操作员)。IoAT架构具有天然的层次性,云端提供可复用战略智能,边缘提供实时编排与态势感知,IoT层提供物理执行与反馈。然而,智能体工作流可能因提示注入、工具失陷、记忆过期、数字孪生偏差、延迟、级联物理效应与不安全委派而失败,且在物理AI语境下认知错误会转化为物理动作,后果尤为严重。因此,下一代IoAT系统必须集成形式化规划、模型预测控制、运行时监控、访问控制、溯源、异常检测、冗余与人类对齐监督,最终目标是构建可扩展、可信赖的信息物理生态系统,使每个连接设备都能参与感知、推理、行动与自适应。

Abstract: The paper introduces the Internet of Agentic Things (IoAT), an architectural framework that integrates agentic AI, IoT, cyber-physical systems, Physical AI, edge computing, and digital twins into a unified closed-loop orchestration framework. The proposed architecture consists of cloud, edge/fog, and physical IoT layers connected through autonomous AI agents that perceive, reason, coordinate, and actuate across distributed cyber-physical environments. The paper formalizes IoAT as a coupled workflow-control problem with nested strategic and tactical decision making using a hylomorphic dynamic programming framework that links agentic planning with physical execution. Smart-building orchestration is presented as a representative use case, and key research challenges related to safety, security, governance, resilience, and trustworthy deployment are discussed.

</details>


### [219] [MaxSAT-Based Feedback for Guiding Vision-Language Models in Sudoku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12711)
*Pedro Orvalho,Guillem Alenyà,Felip Manyà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经-符号混合框架，将部分最大可满足性（MaxSAT）求解器作为一致性验证和精化引擎，引导视觉-语言模型（VLM）求解数独谜题。数独约束编码为硬子句，VLM生成的候选放置编码为软子句，MaxSAT求解器识别最大一致子集，并将冲突转化为结构化的文本与视觉反馈以指导VLM迭代修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM在结构化视觉推理任务（如数独）上展现出较强的感知能力，但缺乏显式的逻辑一致性约束机制，其输出经常违反数独的行、列、宫约束。纯符号求解器虽能保证正确性，但缺乏感知灵活性与候选生成能力。如何将形式化约束优化与VLM相结合，以提升视觉逻辑推理的可靠性，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 1) 将数独的行、列、宫约束采用CNF编码表示为部分MaxSAT的硬子句；2) VLM根据谜题图像生成候选放置，将其编码为软单元子句；3) MaxSAT求解器（RC2）求解得到满足所有硬子句且最大化软子句满足数的赋值，即最大一致子集；4) 将被拒绝的不一致放置转化为文本描述与红色标注的视觉冲突反馈；5) 支持两种交互协议——迭代单步放置求解与全盘精化。实验在200个公开数独数据集实例上评估了GPT-5.5、Molmo2、Qwen3-VL三种VLM。

Result: MaxSAT反馈在所有VLM和设置下均显著提升逻辑一致性和解题数量。全盘MaxSAT反馈模式下效果最佳：GPT-5.5解题数从45增至73（36.5%），平均完整度从72.0%提升至80.7%；难度1谜题解题数从10翻倍至21。开源模型Molmo2（17→28）和Qwen3-VL（10→13）也有提升但幅度较小。迭代单步模式下GPT-5.5解题数从21翻倍至44。

Conclusion: 将部分MaxSAT集成到VLM求解循环中，能够显著增强结构化视觉推理的逻辑一致性、鲁棒性和解的质量。神经感知与符号优化互补：VLM负责候选生成，MaxSAT保证形式一致性。当VLM生成全局较为一致但存在局部错误的方案时，符号精化尤为有效。未来工作可扩展至其他视觉约束满足问题，并探索神经提议机制与符号优化层的更紧密集成。

Abstract: Vision--Language Models (VLMs) have recently demonstrated promising performance on structured visual reasoning tasks, including grid-based puzzles. However, despite strong perceptual capabilities, these models lack explicit mechanisms for enforcing logical consistency and frequently generate assignments that violate underlying constraints. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic approach that integrates formal constraint reasoning into the VLM solving process via a Maximum Satisfiability (MaxSAT) oracle. Rather than computing solutions directly, the symbolic component acts as a consistency validator and refinement engine. Candidate placements generated by the VLM are encoded as soft clauses in a partial MaxSAT formulation, while Sudoku constraints remain hard clauses. When inconsistencies arise, the MaxSAT solver identifies a largest mutually consistent subset of assignments, which is then translated into structured textual and visual feedback to guide subsequent refinements. We evaluate our approach on a Sudoku dataset across multiple open-source and closed-access VLMs. Results show that MaxSAT-based feedback improves logical consistency and increases the number of solved instances, particularly in full-board refinement mode. These findings demonstrate that symbolic optimisation can enhance the reliability of vision-language reasoning.

</details>


### [220] [LLMs Can See the Smoke but not the Fire: Evaluating Abductive Reasoning with Elenchos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12733)
*Julius Steiglechner,Lucas Mahler,Gabriele Lohmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Elenchos评估框架，用于测量大语言模型在依赖类型λ-演算系统中识别潜在规则突变（即逆向溯因推理）的能力。通过对30余个前沿和中端LLM的实验，发现模型在异常检测（detection）与原因归因（attribution）之间存在显著能力差距——能"察觉烟"却难以"定位火源"——尤其在交互式复合突变和推理预算增大时，能力急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在正向推理（模式识别、文本生成）上表现出色，但其溯因推理（abductive inference）——即从观察到的行为差异推断潜在因果假说——的能力尚未得到系统性评估。静态推理基准存在数据污染和饱和问题，且均将形式系统视为可信的"神谕"，缺乏对系统本身是否被腐化的诊断能力评估。论文旨在填补这一空白，评估LLM在"黑盒"访问下推断形式系统中潜在结构腐化的能力。

Method: Elenchos将诊断任务构建为结构化逆问题：(1) 形式系统基质——采用基于LambdaPy的依赖类型λ-演算内核作为参照；(2) 突变本体论——定义13种单例突变（如绑定错位、β-归约错误、宇宙层级崩溃等），并在满足可识别性条件的前提下枚举复合配置（加性 vs 交互式）；(3) 交互式审计协议——智能体在有限探测预算（50或65次）下向两个黑盒内核提交探测（probe），通过观察行为差异推断被腐化内核及活跃突变集。评估分两阶段：Difficulty Ladder（13个LLM × 不同提示层级 × 突变数）和Wide-Panel Benchmark（30个LLM × 固定基线配置）。度量指标包括核检测V_K、突变归因V_A、条件归因、Jaccard指数及效率评分E_ELX。

Result: 核心发现呈现一致的"检测-归因分离"现象：(1) 检测与归因能力差距显著——平均V_K=0.65但V_A仅0.28，最高能力模型如gemini-2.5-pro的差距达0.57（即"能看见烟却找不到火"）；(2) 交互式突变是关键失败模式——k=2⊗时平均精确率降至0.03；(3) 单一突变偏差明显——缺失突变（少报）远多于虚构突变（多报）；(4) 提示脚手架影响显著——Level 0提示（含典型探测样例）大幅提升归因能力；(5) 推理预算扩大（从low到high）未带来一致提升——初步证据表明溯因能力可能对推理时计算扩展具有不敏感性；(6) 即便赋予最优探测集（Simple Reflex Agent可达到V=1），LLM也无法自主生成等价结构探测。

Conclusion: Elenchos揭示了当前LLM在溯因推理上的根本性能力断层：识别异常（感知层面）相对容易，但构建完整的因果假说（解释层面）极具挑战，尤其在多因素交互情境下。性能退化主因是归因失误而非检测失败，表明模型更接近"启发式假说生成器"而非"穷尽性推理者"。即使扩展推理预算也未能有效弥补这一缺口，暗示溯因能力的限制可能反映当前架构的深层特征而非可通过提示工程或搜索扩展解决的表层工程问题。该发现对网络安全取证、自主系统监控及科学假说生成等需要从部分观察推断隐藏机制的应用具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at pattern recognition and text generation, but their capacity for abductive inference - inferring latent hypotheses that explain observed behavior - remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Elenchos (named after the Socratic method of cross-examination), a generative evaluation framework that measures abductive reasoning as a structural inverse problem. Given a reference formal system, such as the lambda-calculus, and a potentially mutated counterpart, agents must determine whether a mutation has occurred and infer the rule modifications responsible for the resulting behavioral differences. Evaluating frontier and mid-tier LLMs reveals a consistent detection-attribution dissociation: models often recognize that a system has been altered but struggle to identify the latent mutations causing the observed discrepancies. Performance degrades substantially under interacting mutations, where models frequently recover only a subset of the underlying mutations. Preliminary evidence also suggests diminishing returns from increased inference-time reasoning, with only modest improvements under larger reasoning budgets, though this finding requires further validation.

</details>


### [221] [Tracing Agentic Failure from the Flow of Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12747)
*Samuel Yeh,Yiwen Zhu,Shaleen Deep,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OAT的新方法，用于无监督的LLM智能体系统故障归因。核心思想是仅在成功轨迹上训练，利用神经控制微分方程（Neural CDEs）建模成功轨迹的动态模式，在推理时通过异常分数识别失败步骤中的错误步骤。OAT仅需约100条成功轨迹进行训练，无需任何步骤级标注，速度比基于提示词的方法快200-5000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统的故障归因方法存在两大缺陷：基于提示词的pipeline计算开销大、需要前沿LLM推理；基于后训练的方法需要昂贵的步骤级错误标注，难以扩展。因此需要一种轻量级、无需步骤级监督的故障归因方法。

Method: 将无监督故障归因问题形式化为潜空间中的一类学习（one-class learning）。使用LLM提取每一步的隐藏状态表示，通过神经控制微分方程（Neural CDEs）建模成功轨迹的连续动态路径，并引入新颖的门控控制路径（gated control path）以自适应抑制分布外控制信号，提升跨域鲁棒性。推理时，通过预测状态与实际状态的偏差为每个步骤计算异常分数，并采用Top-k或Conformal Prediction两种检测策略识别故障贡献步骤。

Result: 在MCP-Atlas（领域内）和Who&When（分布外）数据集上的实验表明，OAT仅用100条成功轨迹训练，在F1分数上比GPT-4o和GPT-5等提示词基线分别高出+20%（领域内）和+7%（OOD），同时速度快200-5000倍，零token成本。消融实验证实Neural CDE优于Neural ODE和RNN，门控控制路径显著提升OOD性能（AUROC提升0.172），深层表示优于浅层表示。

Conclusion: OAT提出了一种无需步骤级标注的智能体故障归因新范式，通过一类学习成功轨迹的动态模式实现高效、可扩展的步骤级失败定位。其计算效率使实时故障归因首次成为可能，为智能体系统的可调试性和可审计性提供了实用方案。局限性在于对早期隐式推理错误的检测能力有限，硬阈值检测可能遗漏边界附近的因果显著步骤，未来可探索连续归一化流、随机微分方程等扩展方向。

Abstract: Failure attribution for LLM-based agentic systems, i.e., identifying which steps in a failure trajectory caused the task to fail, is critical for debugging and improving these systems. Existing approaches either rely on prompting-based pipelines, which are computationally expensive, or require post-training on failure trajectories with step-level error annotations, which are costly to collect and difficult to scale. We argue that a practical failure attribution model should be lightweight and trainable without step-level supervision on failure data. To this end, we address unsupervised failure attribution, i.e., training exclusively on successful trajectories and identifying error steps at inference time given a failure trajectory. We propose OAT, which casts this problem as one-class learning with neural controlled differential equations, modeling the dynamical pattern of successful trajectories in latent space. At inference time, each step in a failure trajectory is assigned an anomaly score based on its deviation from the dynamics learned on successful trajectories, which is then used to form a set of error steps. With training on only 100 successful trajectories, experiments show that OAT is 200--5000 $\times$ faster than prompting-based baselines, and, at the same time, consistently outperforms them in both in-domain and out-of-distribution datasets with +20% and +7% F1 scores, respectively, demonstrating that OAT is a promising and efficient direction for diagnosing agentic system failures.

</details>


### [222] [Accuracy and Normalized Accuracy under Length Bias: Analysis, Guidelines, and a Bayesian Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12767)
*Koen Oostermeijer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了基于条件对数似然的多选题评估中的长度偏差问题。研究发现标准准确率倾向于偏好较短答案，而朴素的长度归一化常常过度修正转而偏好较长答案。为此，作者提出了一种新的"贝叶斯准确率"评分规则，通过引入关于完成长度的显式指数先验来消除线性长度效应，无需额外的前向传播，在多个基准和少样本设置中均显著降低了长度偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多选题基准（如MMLU、HellaSwag、WinoGrande等）通过对数似然排名候选答案，但因似然在token上累加，存在系统性的长度偏差（偏好较短答案）。常用的按长度归一化方法常过度修正，转而偏好较长答案。需要一种在各种任务中都能稳健工作的评分规则。

Method: 1. 将长度偏差形式化为评分与候选长度之间的平均Kendall秩相关系数τ。2. 建立层次线性生成模型刻画似然-长度的近似线性关系。3. 引入指数长度先验P(n)∝exp(-bn)，将后验对数似然表示为ℓ_θ(c|x) - bn，通过去除全局线性长度趋势实现去偏。4. 使用题目内中心化方法估计衰减因子b̂，避免提示特定偏移的混淆。

Result: 在ARC、ARC German、HellaSwag、MMLU（Cloze和Full-Text）、OpenbookQA、SciQ、WinoGrande等8个基准上对LLaMA、Qwen、Mistral、Pythia、SmolLM等家族的17个模型（135M到70B，含基础版和指令微调版）进行评估。标准准确率平均|τ|为0.17-0.31（负偏差），字节归一化为0.24-0.42（正偏差），PMI为0.07-0.18，ANPMI为0.05-0.13，而贝叶斯准确率仅为0.03-0.07，相对归一化准确率减少4-8倍长度偏差。零样本和少样本设置结论一致。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯准确率是似然基多选题评估的即插即用替代方案，无需额外前向传播，在各种基准、模型规模和少样本设置下一致地将长度偏差降低数倍。建议作为默认的长度去偏评分规则使用，标准准确率仅在长度无关的场景（如答案已在提示中的本地确定情况或候选等长情况）下使用，归一化准确率仅在其低偏差被经验验证后使用。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks that rank candidate completions by conditional log-probability suffer from a length bias: because log-probabilities sum over tokens, longer answers tend to be penalized relative to shorter ones in practice. A common mitigation is to normalize scores by completion length, but we show empirically that this heuristic frequently over-corrects, introducing a bias toward longer answers instead. We first analyze these scoring rules, characterizing when standard and length-normalized accuracy are appropriate and how their length biases depend on the distribution of completion lengths. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce \emph{Bayesian accuracy}, a scoring rule that computes the posterior probability of each candidate under an explicit prior over answer length, thereby removing linear length effects. Bayesian accuracy is a drop-in replacement for likelihood-based multiple-choice evaluation, requires no additional forward passes, and consistently exhibits lower empirical length bias than both standard and length-normalized accuracy across benchmarks and few-shot settings.

</details>


### [223] [Do We Really Need Multimodal Emotion Language Models Larger Than 1B Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12787)
*Kaiwen Zheng,Junchen Fu,Wenhao Deng,Hu Han,Joemon M. Jose,Xuri Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Light-MER框架，通过知识蒸馏将8B多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的情感识别能力迁移至亚十亿参数（0.6B）的轻量级学生模型，在9个基准数据集上以11×更少的参数超越8B教师模型，实现了高效的多模态情感识别（MER）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式多模态情感识别（MER）系统通常依赖至少7B参数的MLLM，带来高计算开销、内存占用和推理延迟，难以部署在机器人、手机等边缘设备上。论文质疑高质量MER是否真的需要超过1B参数的部署模型。

Method: 提出Light-MER师生蒸馏框架，包含两大核心创新：（1）SWD-H——基于切片Wasserstein距离（Sliced Wasserstein Distance）的隐藏状态对齐损失，将教师与学生的潜在表示视为经验分布，通过随机一维投影和排序进行最优传输映射（复杂度O(Rn log n)），保留多模态表示几何结构；（2）M-GRPO——基于GRPO的多奖励策略优化，在蒸馏后对生成质量进行精炼，使用F1分数、长度、关键词密度、句子完整性和重复度等五维复合奖励。教师采用Qwen3-8B+CLIP-ViT-Large+HuBERT-Large，学生采用Qwen3-0.6B+CLIP-ViT-Base+HuBERT-Base，通过LoRA适应。

Result: 在9个基准数据集（含中英文情感、情绪、细粒度开放词汇任务）上的全面实验：全模态（音视频文本）设置下平均得分74.61，超过8B教师（73.93）和原始AffectGPT（69.77）；9个数据集中7个取得最优；实现11×参数压缩（854.93M）、11× FLOPs压缩、7.9×显存降低（20.04GB→2.54GB）；M-GRPO使描述式推理延迟从4.6s降至3.1s（-32.8%），直接推理模式仅0.5s/样本；GPT-5.4评估显示生成简洁性大幅提升（+1.20），胜率54%。

Conclusion: 高质量生成式MER不需要超过1B参数的部署模型。Light-MER通过几何感知的隐藏状态蒸馏（SWD-H）和多奖励策略优化（M-GRPO），证明亚十亿参数模型可在多模态情感推理上超越8B教师，同时显著降低内存和延迟。保留表征几何结构是紧凑多模态推理系统的有前景方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have significantly improved the performance of multimodal emotion recognition (MER) and enabled interpretable description generation by jointly modeling video, audio, and language, etc. However, these performance improvements are often accompanied by an increase in model parameter size (e.g, at least 7B), which simultaneously incurs high computational costs and reduces inference efficiency, thereby hindering real-time deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as robots and mobile devices. This raises a fundamental question: do we really need the multimodal MER model larger than 1B parameters for high-quality MER? In this paper, we challenge the assumption that larger models are inherently necessary and proposes a lightweight MER framework (called Light-MER), which achieves better and faster multimodal sentiment understanding and recognition through knowledge distillation. It can transfer knowledge from a strong, large-scale teacher model to a lightweight sub-billion-parameter student model, aiming to preserve rich multimodal emotion reasoning and recognition while substantially improving deployment efficiency. Specifically, we introduce two new optimization strategies to enhance knowledge transfer: (1) a new optimal transport loss that combines Sliced Wasserstein Distance with hidden-state alignment, and (2) a new multi-reward optimization strategy based on GRPO that balances MER performance and efficiency, aimed at further enhancing the learning capabilities of student models. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets demonstrate that Light-MER achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly improving inference efficiency. This highlights the strong potential of small multimodal emotion language models for future research. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [224] [Who Grades the Grader? Co-Evolving Evaluation Metrics and Skills for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12790)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Double Ratchet"框架,将评估指标本身视为可进化的对象,使其与LLM智能体的技能库协同进化。通过由小型"缺陷检测器"组合而成的可解释指标表达式,配合严格的三段式锚定协议(tiny dev set / 锁定 test set),在无可靠自动验证器的场景下仍能恢复88-110%的监督式增益,并能检测并修复Goodhart失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化LLM智能体系统严重依赖评估信号来判定任务成败。代码任务有单元测试,但实际部署场景(企业报表生成等)往往没有可靠指标;业界普遍做法是人工编写评分标准或演示工作流。本文核心动机是:既然技能可以进化,为何指标不能进化?同时指出当前LLM-as-judge存在与被评估模型共享盲区的合谋风险,且存在Goodhart失效隐患。

Method: 1) **指标形式化**:将指标定义为基于"缺陷检测器"(ops)的表达式树,语法包括析取、合取、K-of-k投票,各op检测特定失败类(crash、missing GROUP BY等);2) **三段式数据划分**:train(大量无标签)、dev(仅10个锚定项,唯一监督信号)、test(锁定,任何循环永不读取);3) **指标循环**:通过LLM合成新op并经历完整生命周期(出生门控→阴影晋升→边际贡献淘汰),选择目标结合dev一致性与无标签共识正则化;4) **Double Ratchet协同进化**:指标循环与技能循环交替进行,前者为后者训练任务打分;5) **外部审计**:使用更强LLM对无参考任务进行配对式终审,捕捉Goodhart失效。

Result: 在三个任务上评估:MBPP+(代码生成)保留106%、Spider 2.0-Snow(text-to-SQL)保留110%、Report生成(无黄金指标)保留88%的oracle式增益。指标本身在MBPP+和Report上达到0.85左右的锚定一致性。关键消融发现:移除锚定保护会使指标退化为几乎全通过的"空洞检测器"(train通过率0.94-1.00);移除生命周期管理则不会崩溃。在报告任务上成功检测并修复了一次真实的Goodhart失效(技能通过编写无数值标签来"刷分"),仅添加一个值擦除检测器即将虚假标签从30%降至1%,且rubric分数继续上升。任务感知judge在77%的成对比较中偏好修复后的进化输出。

Conclusion: 评估指标可在严格锚定纪律下进化为可信的、可解释的一等对象;Double Ratchet在无可靠验证器时仍可恢复近全部监督式增益。安全负载由**锚定纪律**(而非检测器生命周期)承担——这与技能进化文献的结论恰好相反。方法在**失败可机械检测**的任务上收益最大;对于语义性失败(如Spider中执行正确但语义错误的查询),指标一致性较低但训练信号仍足够定向性。最终建议:在无可靠自动验证器的部署中,默认采用"失败可期"架构——配备预测性锚定、锁定评估集、以及独立于循环之外的终审判官。

Abstract: Self-evolving agent systems improve by creating, revising, and retiring their own skills, but every such loop rests on a hidden assumption: a reliable evaluation metric already exists. In many real applications it does not. We make three claims. First, metrics can be \emph{evolved}: our metric loop searches compositions of small drawback detectors under a full evolutionary lifecycle, trained to agree with a ten-item anchored reference set, regularized by consensus over unlabeled outputs, and audited against a held-out anchor it never reads, yielding a transparent, inspectable metric rather than an opaque judge. Second, since no metric exists to beat, the yardstick is recovering what an accurate metric would have enabled, and \emph{Double Ratchet}, our co-evolution of the metric with a lifecycle-managed skill loop, does so: across code generation (MBPP+), enterprise text-to-SQL (Spider~2.0-Snow), and reference-free report generation, it retains 88--110\% of the held-out lift achieved by the same skill loop driven by ground truth or the best available rubric. Third, safety comes from anchor discipline plus outer audits: removing anchor guards collapses the metric into a vacuous detector while removing the lifecycle does not; and when evolved skills gamed the report rubric, an independent judge caught it, one detector repaired it, and a task-aware judge then preferred the evolved outputs over the pre-evolution baseline in 77\% of decided pairs. We argue this failure-expecting architecture is the right default wherever no reliable automatic verifier exists.

</details>


### [225] [Visual Access Boundaries in Vision-Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12815)
*Hiroto Osaka,Shohei Taniguchi,Gouki Minegishi,Kai Yamashita,Masahiro Suzuki,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"视觉访问边界"(Visual Access Boundary, VAB)概念及"视觉访问扫描"(Visual Access Sweep)因果干预方法，通过在层深度和生成时间两个维度上遮蔽生成token查询对图像token键的注意力，系统探究了思维链(CoT)推理在视觉-语言模型(VLM)中究竟扩展了什么。核心发现是：CoT并非通过延长对图像token的直接访问来提升性能，而是通过在已提取到隐藏状态的图像派生信息上进行语言侧计算；而CoT增益的根本瓶颈在于感知读出(perceptual readout)而非推理操作本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-Thought(CoT)提示作为视觉-语言模型(VLM)的测试时扩展策略被广泛使用，但其在不同视觉任务上的收益并不一致——有些研究报告提升，另一些则发现收益有限甚至下降。这引发了一个根本性问题：当VLM生成更长的CoT推理轨迹时，"被扩展的"究竟是什么？CoT是通过在整个推理过程中持续重新读取图像token来提升性能，还是通过对早期已提取到隐藏状态中的图像信息进行更深入的语言侧计算来实现？现有研究多为观察性（仅记录注意力衰减），无法区分"不再需要访问"与"访问失败"两种情况，因此需要因果干预来回答这一功能必要性问题。

Method: 1. **Visual Access Sweep（视觉访问扫描）**：一种因果干预方法，在两个维度上设置截断点(ℓ_cutoff, t_cutoff)，将生成token查询对图像token键的直接注意力限制在允许区域内（ℓ≤ℓ_cutoff 且 t≤t_cutoff），外部区域通过加性注意力掩码矩阵将softmax值置为零。2. **Visual Access Boundary（视觉访问边界, VAB）**：定义为在容差ε=0.05范围内保留任务准确率所需的最小访问区域，使用三种准确率下降度量（Direct自身、CoT自身最大值、以Direct全访问为锚点）。3. **任务设计**：采用受控的属性-计数任务（固定推理操作为counting，变化查询属性为颜色/形状/位置/角度/大小），以及基于GQA的真实图像场景图QA任务。4. **感知读出诊断**：包括多目标感知读出任务和单目标probe-vs-decode检查（线性探针vs模型自身解码）。5. **符号属性Oracle旁路实验**：在无图像输入下将地面真值属性序列化进文本，测试语言侧计数操作是否能从CoT中获益。6. **模型范围**：Qwen2.5-VL（3B/7B/32B）、InternVL3（8B/14B/38B），以及作为导引检查的Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct（交叉注意力架构）。7. **对照实验**：包括晚层query-text block对照、null-sink对照、以及对容差参数ε∈{0.03,0.05,0.07,0.10}的敏感性分析。

Result: 1. **有限VAB的普遍存在**：在所有测试的VLM配置和任务中，Direct提示与CoT提示均呈现出有限的视觉访问边界。遮蔽广泛的晚层/晚生成token区域后准确率保持稳定，但一旦干预触及更早层，准确率急剧下降。2. **CoT不扩展直接图像访问**：在Qwen2.5-VL-32B、InternVL3-14B和38B上，以Direct全访问为锚点时，CoT VAB层与Direct边界的差异最多仅2层，尽管CoT生成长度约长50倍。3. **跨架构与跨刺激泛化**：Qwen2.5-VL与InternVL3均呈现层次化VAB结构，交叉注意力架构的导引实验也确认有限VAB的存在；GQA真实图像任务上同样复现该结构。4. **感知读出限制CoT增益**：在Qwen2.5-VL三个规模上，per-attribute CoT增益与多目标感知读出准确率呈正相关（3B: r=+0.92, ρ=+1.00；7B: r=+0.88, ρ=+1.00；32B: r=+0.51, ρ=+0.50），读出弱的属性（角度、大小）几乎无CoT增益。5. **Oracle旁路统一恢复CoT增益**：当以符号形式提供地面真值属性时，CoT增益在所有属性上一致恢复，包括原本表现弱的属性。6. **Probe-vs-Decode间隙**：在Qwen2.5-VL-32B上，角度（gap=+0.571）、位置（+0.658）、大小（+0.608）等难属性可从隐藏状态线性恢复但模型自身难以输出，定位瓶颈在读出而非计算操作。7. **实用性启发**：基于读出准确率阈值的自适应路由策略可保留always-CoT约75-87%的增益，同时节省约一半输出token成本。8. **对照验证**：晚层query-text block和null-sink两种对照均保留定性VAB结构，ε敏感性分析显示主结论在ε∈{0.03-0.10}范围内稳定（|Δℓ*_DA|≤1）。

Conclusion: 本文通过对视觉-语言模型因果干预分析得出三项核心结论：(1) CoT并非通过延长生成过程中对图像token的直接注意力访问来提升性能，而是通过对已提取到隐藏状态中的图像派生信息进行语言侧计算；(2) 在所测试的前缀式解码器VLM中存在有限的视觉访问边界（VAB），该结构在不同模型家族、规模和真实/合成图像间均一致出现；(3) CoT增益的根本瓶颈在于感知读出（perceptual readout）而非推理操作本身——只有当查询的视觉属性能被模型可靠地读出为可用符号时，语言侧CoT计算才能带来增益；这些发现重新定义了视觉注意力衰减的意义（关键不是衰减本身，而是后续直接访问是否功能必要），并为基于属性级读出检查的自适应CoT路由策略提供了实证依据，同时也凸显了主动感知方法（如MemVR、Look-Back、Visual Perception Token）与普通CoT的互补关系——前者显式构建感知-推理反馈环路，后者则在测试范围内无法自行闭合该环路。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting is widely used as a test-time scaling strategy for Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but it remains unclear what is extended when VLMs generate longer reasoning traces. We ask whether CoT requires continued access to image tokens, or whether it mainly operates over visual information already made available earlier in the forward pass. We introduce Visual Access Sweep, a causal intervention that masks attention from generated-token queries to image-token keys along layer depth and generation time, and define the Visual Access Boundary (VAB) as the minimal access region that preserves task accuracy. Across six model configurations from Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3, both no-CoT direct answering and CoT prompting exhibit finite VABs. In Qwen2.5-VL-32B and InternVL3 at 14B and 38B scales, when CoT is evaluated against the no-CoT full-access target, its VAB layer differs from the no-CoT boundary by at most two layers, despite substantially longer generations. This suggests that CoT does not primarily improve performance by prolonging direct image-token access throughout the reasoning trace, but by extending language-side computation over image-derived hidden-state information. We further show that CoT gains are constrained by perceptual readout. CoT helps when the queried visual attribute can be reliably read out by the model, but not when that readout is unreliable. A symbolic-attribute oracle shows that CoT can improve counting once ground-truth attributes are supplied as text, while a single-object probe-vs-decode check shows that hard attributes can be linearly recoverable from hidden states yet difficult for the model itself to output. Together, these analyses place the bottleneck at readout rather than counting.

</details>


### [226] [Human-AI Agent Interaction as a Neuroplastic Training Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12823)
*Eranga Bandara,Ross Gore,Asanga Gunaratna,Ravi Mukkamala,Nihal Siriwardanagea,Gihan Siriwardanagea,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Sachin Shetty,Christopher K. Rhea,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Amin Hass,Shaifali Kaushik,Wathsala Herath,Preston Samuel,Anita H. Clayton,Atmaram Yarlagadd*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，日常与AI智能体（如对话助手、编程副驾驶、图像生成工具）的迭代交互循环（请求-结果-评价-修改）本身即为一个高频的神经可塑性训练环境。当结果令人失望时，耐心不足、完美主义、挫折感和自我批判等条件化反应模式会反复触发并通过长时程增强（LTP）得到强化；通过在该循环的"感受间隙"中实施"幕后观察"（三层观察法），可使同一回路转向长时程抑制（LTD），从而削弱相关神经通路。文章还提出了用户引导模式与智能体辅助模式两种应用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体交互成为日常数字生活最频繁的活动之一，每个交互回合都是一个"接触事件"，可能无意中强化用户的反应性模式。然而HCI文献仅将情感视为需管理的用户体验变量，神经科学文献未将智能体交互视为塑形场所，情绪调节文献则将观察式冥想实践置于专门场景，未与日常技术使用时刻结合。该研究旨在连接这三条独立脉络，提出一种"上游神经可塑性干预"的新框架。

Method: 论文构建了一个端到端的理论框架：（1）将反应性模式刻画为"神经元路径"，通过重复、单一事件强度和遗传三种机制编码；（2）提出情境键编码模型（含霍夫曼编码类比）和"龙卷风效应"（编码越深，泛化半径越大）；（3）定位杏仁核在100-200ms内产生的"前认知感受色调"与反应级联完成之间的"感受色调间隙"为唯一可干预上游窗口；（4）提出三层幕后观察法（信号→感受色调→神经过程）；（5）以生成式图像提示为例进行案例分析，对比"未观察"与"已观察"两种场景。

Result: 该框架表明，同一个智能体交互循环既可以是长时程增强引擎（反应路径被强化），也可以通过在间隙处的观察转为长时程抑制（路径被削弱）。两种模式下行为上几乎不可区分（都会修改提示），但神经机制完全相反。生成式图像提示工作示例显示，数十次失望结果可在一次会话内被转化为数十次小幅LTD事件。"观察能力"本身通过赫布式增强而随练习强化，在多次会话后与反应路径强度交叉，实现结构性重组。

Conclusion: 论文将人-智能体交互循环定位为一种先前未被识别的神经可塑性训练环境，并指出该环境有强化或削弱反应性模式的双向潜力。其核心实践优势在于：高频、间隔明确且低风险，使感受间隙易于定位和练习；反应时刻与练习机会在同一地点、同一时刻重合，无需从专门场景迁移。局限性包括该框架尚未经过临床验证、迁移性未证实、个体差异未充分考虑。未来工作应包括对智能体辅助干预的临床评估与设计调优。

Abstract: Interaction with AI agents has become one of the most frequent activities of everyday digital life. Whether conversing with an assistant, working with a coding copilot, or generating images, the interaction follows a common iterative loop: a request is issued, a result returned, appraised, and the request revised. We observe that this loop is a high-frequency stream of contact events -- moments at which a result meets a person and a conditioned response may fire before deliberate appraisal -- making everyday agent interaction an unrecognised neuroplastic training environment. When a result disappoints, reactive patterns of impatience, perfectionism, frustration, and self-criticism are repeatedly evoked, and under activity-dependent synaptic plasticity each uninterrupted cycle deepens the underlying pathway through long-term potentiation. Ordinary agent use may thus quietly strengthen the very patterns it provokes. We propose that the same training environment can be engaged to the opposite effect. Treating conditioned reactive patterns as physical neurone paths -- activated through a pre-cognitive feeling tone that opens a brief regulatory gap -- we develop a framework in which, at that gap, in place of the reactive re-prompt, a person performs behind-the-scenes observation: watching the neural process operate so the cascade does not complete and long-term depression weakens the path rather than potentiation strengthening it. We characterise this practice through three layers of observation and two modes of application: a user-guided mode requiring no change to existing tools, and an agent-assisted mode in which an ordinary agent is lightly configured to support observation at the gap. We illustrate the framework through generative image prompting, showing how a single frustrating session is behaviourally nearly identical whether or not it is observed, yet neurologically opposite.

</details>


### [227] [Solution of the Hempel's statistical ambiguity problem and Causal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12826)
*Evgenii Vityaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文解决了Hempel的归纳统计推理中的统计歧义性问题，通过引入基于Cartwright因果定义的"因果规则"（Causal Rules）和最大特异因果关系（MSCRs），证明基于MSCRs的预测是一致的（Theorem 1），为因果AI和因果机器学习提供了理论一致的概率因果学习系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carl Hempel的归纳统计（I-S）推理中存在统计歧义性问题：从同一个一致的统计定律集合中可能推导出相互矛盾的预测。为解决此问题，Hempel提出了最大特异性要求（RMS），但该原则经历了Salmon、Coffa、Fetzer等人的修正与批判，始终缺乏严格的形式化证明。此外，因果AI和因果机器学习领域需要一种理论一致的因果推理框架来避免矛盾预测。

Method: 基于Nancy Cartwright关于因果在所有背景语境下提升结果概率的定义，引入"因果规则"（Causal Rule）的形式化概念：规则 A₁&…&Aₖ⇒A₀ 中的每个文字 Aᵢ 都是 A₀ 的原因，即在包含其余文字的背景下 P(A₀|A₁&…&Aₖ) > P(A₀|¬Aᵢ&B)。定义规则的特化（specification）与精化（refinement）关系，引入"富集规则集"（rich set of rules）的概念。在富集规则集上定义"语义概率推理"（SPI），通过逐步添加所有统计相关信息来精化因果规则的条件概率，直至无法进一步精化，从而产生最大特异因果关系（MSCR）。在命题逻辑公式上定义有限可加概率测度，建立一套完整的概率逻辑框架。

Result: 核心结果是定理1（Theorem 1）：设 Π 是 MSCR(R, G, μ) 的任意子集，T 是 G-一致的公式集，则预测算子 PR_Π(T) 也是 G-一致的。即基于最大特异因果关系的归纳统计推理产生一致的预测集，从而解决了Hempel的统计歧义性问题。证明通过将无限规则集归约为有限等价类集，再利用反证法（假设产生不一致则可找到概率更高的精化规则，与MSCR的最大性矛盾）完成。MSCR构成了一个"一致的概率理论"，类似于一致且能推出无矛盾结论的逻辑理论。

Conclusion: 本文精确形式化并解决了Hempel的统计歧义性问题，不仅总结了关于RMS的历史争论，也为科学哲学开辟了新方向。推论包括：(1) I-S推理能够发现逻辑一致的自然科学理论；(2) 循环因果关系可以形成外部世界类别的一致因果模型；(3) 基于因果模型可定义概率形式概念，为范畴提供理想化描述。基于文字形式的因果规则，已开发"Discovery"程序系统（使用Fisher精确检验）成功应用于金融预测、医学和生物信息学等任务。MSCR为开发更强大的因果AI系统（如数字孪生控制系统）奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper addresses Carl Hempel's longstanding problem of statistical ambiguity in inductive-statistical inference, in which contradictory predictions are derived from statistical laws. To avoid such predictions, Carl Hempel proposed the Requirement of Maximal Specificity (RMS) for the statistical laws used in the inference. An analysis of the RMS refinements made by Wesley Salmon, Alberto Coffa, and James Fetzer led to the following definition of maximally specific statistical laws: "the lawlike premises of an adequate explanation must specify all and only those properties whose presence or absence made a difference to the occurrence of its explanandum-phenomenon." However, there was no proof of a solution to the statistical ambiguity problem based on this definition. We use Nancy Cartwright's definition of causes that raise probabilities across background contexts, and then introduce the concept of Causal Rules. Then we define a special semantic probabilistic inference procedure that incrementally refines these causal rules by incorporating all statistically relevant information. This procedure yields Maximally Specific Causal Relationships (MSCRs), for which we prove (Theorem 1) that predictions derived from them are consistent. This resolves the statistical ambiguity problem. The semantic probabilistic inference procedure provides a probabilistic causal learning system, which may be used in such new areas as Causal AI and Causal Machine Learning. They fundamentally explore causal inference as a tool for understanding cause-and-effect relationships within complex systems. Properties similar to RMS remain under discussion. Several notions related to RMS are considered: invariant feature learning, invariant causal prediction, and spurious association.

</details>


### [228] [A Multi-Agent System for Autonomous, Fine-Tuning-Free Clinical Symptom Detection: Development and Validation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12886)
*Cameron Cagan,Pedram Fard,Jiazi Tian,Jingya Cheng,Shawn N. Murphy,Hossein Estiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Pythia的多智能体系统，能够自主编写和优化临床症状提取提示，无需人工提示工程或模型微调。该系统基于本地部署的开源权重大语言模型（GPT-OSS-20B），在72种症状/体征上与人工策展词典和微调BERT分类器进行了对比：Pythia的灵敏度为0.76、特异度为0.95，词典为0.82/0.76，BERT为0.23/0.91。Pythia在62个可比较概念中的20个上同时优于词典，并将词典特异度为零的14个概念的特异度恢复至平均0.97，验证了自主提示优化在临床NLP中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床记录中包含大量症状信息，但很少进入结构化字段。现有方法存在两难：基于规则的词典缺乏上下文理解导致大量假阳性；而监督式模型需要大量标注数据进行微调。本文旨在探索能否利用"循环工程"（loop engineering）思路，让多智能体系统自主优化提示，填补规则匹配与监督微调之间的空白，同时满足临床使用对灵敏度和特异度的要求。

Method: 1. **数据**：400份COVID患者临床记录（387名患者），临床医生对72种症状/体征进行二元标注，按概念分层随机划分为开发集（n=300）和验证集（n=100）。2. **Pythia架构**：多智能体有向图循环，包含三类子智能体——Specialist（用当前提示对开发集做预测）、Improver（对错误样本生成自然语言批评）、Summarizer（综合批评合成新提示）；外加一个确定性Controller负责继续/回溯/重置/终止决策。3. **优化目标**：以开发集上灵敏度与特异度的加权组合 M(P;D,w) = w·σ + (1-w)·τ 作为评分函数，仅优化提示而不更新模型权重。4. **模型选择**：在Gemma-4-31B-it、GPT-OSS-20B、Llama-3.1-70B三种开源权重模型中，GPT-OSS-20B因平均验证灵敏度达1.00而被选用。5. **对照方法**：策展词典（精确子串匹配+否定/家族史过滤，F1阈值化）与Bio_ClinicalBERT微调分类器。

Result: 1. **整体性能**：Pythia灵敏度0.76、特异度0.95，词典0.82/0.76，BERT 0.23/0.91（BERT平均准确率0.90掩盖了其在47个概念上灵敏度为零的事实）。2. **四组操作点分类**（62个可比较概念）：Dominance组（n=20）两者指标均优；Specificity-first组（n=12）以召回换精度（特异度从0.29→0.97）；Sensitivity-first组（n=16）以恒定特异度大幅提升灵敏度；Frontier组（n=14）两者均弱于词典。3. **词典特异度为零的14个概念**（如chills），Pythia通过要求"现在时、患者主诉"的表述将特异度恢复至0.97均值。4. **迁移性**：特异度在开发-验证集间几乎无衰减（均值差距0.004）；灵敏度在>5%流行度下迁移良好，但<2%流行度时差距达0.25。5. **监督学习的流行度下限**：BERT灵敏度与流行度强相关（Spearman ρ=0.75），<2%流行度下灵敏度均值仅0.03。6. **优化动力学**：中位数2次迭代即可收敛，最多8次；8个概念触发"Infinity War"终止（反复再生相同提示后停止）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，自主、无需微调的提示优化可以构建在特异度上优于词典、能从开发集迁移至验证集（特异度方向）且可本地部署的症状提取器。主要适用条件：（1）存在概念流行度下限（约5%），低于此阈值的开发集不足以可靠选择灵敏度；（2）对单标记无歧义术语（如震颤、黑便）词典仍更优；（3）稀疏、语义弥散的概念处于方法边界，需人工审核。该系统将成本从标注与微调转移至推理时的优化搜索。后续工作包括目标权重的扫描、跨机构联邦评估、与大规模监督模型的性能上限对比以及下游表型任务的验证。

Abstract: Clinical notes contain many of the signs and symptoms that bring patients to care, yet this information rarely reaches structured fields. Existing extraction approaches either rely on context-insensitive rules that generate false positives or on supervised models that require substantial fine-tuning. We present Pythia, a multi-agent system that autonomously writes and optimizes extraction prompts for clinical concepts without manual prompt engineering or fine-tuning. Running on a locally hosted open-weights model, Pythia keeps clinical notes on local infrastructure and selects prompts using development-set sensitivity and specificity. We compared Pythia with a curated lexicon across 72 signs and symptoms from 400 clinical notes representing 387 patients. Development (n=300) and validation (n=100) sets were partitioned independently for each concept. Pythia achieved mean sensitivity of 0.76 and specificity of 0.95, compared with 0.82 and 0.76 for the lexicon, and matched or exceeded the lexicon on both metrics for 20 of 62 directly comparable concepts. For 14 concepts where the lexicon labeled every note positive, Pythia recovered mean specificity of 0.97 by requiring a present-tense, patient-attributed finding rather than any textual mention of a term. Specificity transferred from development to validation with minimal degradation across prevalences, whereas sensitivity transfer weakened below 5% prevalence, reaching a mean gap of 0.25 below 2% prevalence. A BERT classifier fine-tuned per concept on the same development set achieved mean sensitivity of 0.23 and collapsed to zero sensitivity for concepts below roughly 5% prevalence. These findings suggest that autonomous, fine-tuning-free prompt optimization can produce symptom extraction prompts that generalize effectively from development to validation while remaining deployable on local infrastructure.

</details>


### [229] [MemOps: Benchmarking Lifecycle Memory Operations in Long-Horizon Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12893)
*Xixuan Hao,Zeyu Zhang,Zehao Lin,Yihang Sun,Ziliang Guo,Xichong Zhang,Yuxuan Liang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemOps是一个面向长周期对话中生命周期记忆操作的大语言模型评估基准。它将传统黑盒式的最终问答评估重构为对显式记忆操作（记住、遗忘、更新、反思及组合轨迹）的结构化追踪与诊断，覆盖长上下文、检索式、参数式和管理式四类记忆系统，揭示了当前系统在操作层面仍远未达到可靠一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长程记忆基准几乎完全通过下游问答的正确性来评估记忆能力，这种黑盒方式混淆了记忆失败的多种异质原因（如遗漏事实、错误绑定目标、依赖过时值），可能在对答案正确但记忆状态不一致或不安全的情况下误判能力，亟需在操作级别对记忆进行显式建模与诊断。

Method: MemOps将每个记忆事件表示为包含触发器、目标、范围、状态转换和支撑证据的结构化追踪，定义五种生命周期操作（Remember、Forget、Update、Reflect、TrajectoryOps）与六类操作级探针（OperationTrace、TargetBinding、StateTransition、CandidateDisambiguation、OperationApplication、StateTrajectory）。通过可控生成流水线，将操作嵌入长程任务式对话中，并提供金标操作追踪，同时在相邻证据和长上下文两种设置下进行评估；评测覆盖GPT-4o、Claude-Sonnet-4.5、Qwen3.6-27B等长上下文模型、基于BM25的会话级/轮次级检索、Temp-LoRA参数式记忆以及Mem0和MemOS管理式记忆系统。

Result: 实验表明：在相邻证据设置下表现最强，Claude-Sonnet-4.5准确率最高（0.916），Qwen3.6-27B在来源支撑（0.944）和低泄漏率（0.095）上领先；长上下文设置普遍降低性能，但暴露出差异化优势——会话级RAG显著优于轮次级RAG，MemOS优于Mem0，Temp-LoRA几乎不可靠。最敏感的维度是TrajectoryOps和多步状态轨迹重建（如Qwen3.6-27B在长上下文下StateTrajectory仅0.415），表明即便强模型也难以在干扰丰富的长历史中恢复有序的组合操作链。

Conclusion: MemOps将长程记忆评估从单一最终答案分数推进到可解释的操作级诊断，使研究者能够区分系统在事件检索、目标绑定、状态转换、遗忘安全和反思支持等环节的具体失效模式。未来工作可沿扩大领域与语言规模、加强人工校验与判官校准、以及将MemOps作为记忆控制器开发信号三个方向推进，最终构建可控、可审计、尊重用户意图生命周期的记忆系统。

Abstract: Long-term memory has become a foundational capability for LLM-based agents that accompany users across extended, multi-session interactions. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate such memory almost exclusively through downstream question answering, scoring only the correctness of a final answer. This black-box formulation conflates the heterogeneous causes of memory failure, such as missing the introduction of a relevant fact, binding an operation to the wrong target, or relying on stale values after a correction. As a result, it can credit correct answers despite their reliance on inconsistent or unsafe memory states. In this paper, we argue that, in dynamic long-horizon interactions, memory is not a static collection of facts but a lifecycle of explicit operations, including remembering, forgetting, updating, reflecting, and their compositions. We introduce MemOps, a benchmark that reformulates conversational memory as a sequence of lifecycle operations and represents each memory event with a structured trace specifying its trigger, target, scope, state transition, and supporting evidence. A controllable generation pipeline embeds these operations into long, task-oriented conversations and produces gold operation traces together with six categories of operation-level probes, evaluated under both adjacent-evidence and long-context settings. Across long-context, retrieval-based, parametric and managed-memory systems, MemOps disentangles failure modes that final-answer accuracy alone conceals, revealing that current systems remain far from uniformly reliable. For instance, session-level retrieval outperforms turn-level retrieval, and long-context models remain notably weak at reconstructing ordered memory-state trajectories. These results move long-term memory evaluation from final-answer scoring toward interpretable, operation-level diagnosis.

</details>


### [230] [Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12924)
*Jonas Ehrhardt,René Heesch,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的神经符号强化学习算法 KGRL（Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning），用于参数化动作马尔可夫决策过程（PAMDP）。该算法通过 Datalog 知识库对动作空间进行剪枝，并通过基于梯度的参数精化循环（ParamOpt）优化连续参数，在样本效率和回合回报上均优于现有最优基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 PAMDP 环境中，现有 RL 算法通常采用一次性（one-shot）估计器来确定动作的数值参数，导致训练样本效率低下；同时，尽管实际场景中通常存在显式但不完整的领域知识（如规则、安全约束、专家启发式），这些知识很少被直接用于提升 RL 训练效率。此外，现有的神经符号 RL 方法大多局限于离散玩具领域，尚无工作将结构化、可查询的知识库直接整合到 PAMDP 中 RL 智能体的训练和参数估计过程中。

Method: KGRL 基于 DQN 算法进行扩展，主要包含两个关键机制：（1）**Datalog 知识库**：在每个决策步通过符号状态抽象函数 α 将数值状态映射为命题原子集，结合知识库推导当前状态下**可应用动作集** Ā(s) 和**可行参数约束** Ψ̄ₐ(s)，从而剪枝不合法动作并限制参数搜索空间；采用分层 Datalog 与失败即否定推理，并使用默认适用性策略（默认适用，除非可推出 napp(a)）。（2）**ParamOpt 参数精化**：利用 Q 网络对参数的可微性，采用**投影梯度上升法**在可行参数区域内迭代优化参数估计 ψ̃*，投影操作确保参数始终位于知识库定义的可行域内。同时记录知识库中被激活的规则（如 napp(a)、active(a,c)），生成局部过程化的解释轨迹。论文还形式化了知识约束的 PAMDP 元组 ⟨S, A, {Ψₐ}, T, r, γ, α, K⟩，并定义了 K-可行性策略。

Result: 在 HardMove4/8/10、CatchPoint、HardGoal 和 Platform 四个 PAMDP 基准上与 QPAMDP、PA-DDPG、P-DQN、MP-DQN、HyAR 五个基线对比，KGRL 在所有域上均取得最高 AULC（学习曲线下面积），收敛更快、起点更高且方差更低。消融实验显示：（1）知识库在**部署阶段**的作用至关重要——在评估时移除知识库会导致性能显著下降；（2）梯度引导在**训练阶段**反而带来噪声干扰，训练时关闭 ParamOpt 反而能提升性能；（3）训练和评估时同时移除两者，性能普遍最差。知识库利用率方面，HardMoveX 域达到 100%，动作剪枝率约 49%；CatchPoint 和 HardGoal 的剪枝率约 32-34%；Platform 域以参数约束推导为主。通过规则激活日志可生成步骤级解释轨迹，验证了 RQ3 的可解释性。

Conclusion: KGRL 是首个将符号知识库整合到 PAMDP 训练与部署中的神经符号 RL 方法，通过知识引导的动作剪枝与梯度引导的参数精化实现了更高的样本效率和性能，并天然提供局部过程化解释。实验证实了符号领域知识能有效塑造 RL 训练，使其更具样本效率；但何时注入何种知识至关重要——知识库主要影响部署，梯度引导则更适合训练后部署。未来工作方向包括：自动学习抽象函数和知识库、将局部解释轨迹聚合成全局解释。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Reinforcement Learning in Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes (PAMDP), where each decision consists of a symbolic action and numerical parameters. In such settings Reinforcement Learning algorithms typically determine parameters with one-shot estimators, which makes their training sample inefficient. Though in most PAMDP environments explicit but incomplete knowledge (e.g., rules, safety constraints, or expert heuristics) is available, it is rarely directly used to increase the sample-efficiency of training Reinforcement Learning agents. We step into this gap and propose our novel Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning (KGRL) algorithm. KGRL uses domain knowledge in a Datalog knowledge base to derive the set of applicable actions and feasible parameters for a given state. This allows it to prune non-applicable actions from the decision-space and constrain the parameter spaces of the remaining actions. We then use a gradient-based parameter refinement loop to estimate the optimal parameters during training and deployment of the agent. By recording activated rules along the trajectory, KGRL additionally provides local procedural explanations on the pruning of actions and constraining of parameters. Overall, KGRL guides the agent's exploration and deployment toward feasible and constraint-aware decisions, while increasing sample efficiency during training. KGRL outperforms state-of-the-art RL baselines for PAMDPs in both, sample efficiency and episodic return.

</details>


### [231] [FormalAnalyticGeo: A Neural-Symbolic Based Framework for Multimodal Analytic Geometry Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12982)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FormalAnalyticGeo，一个基于神经-符号的多模态解析几何问题自动生成框架。核心创新是设计了一种形式化中间语言 CDL（条件描述语言），结合 SDF（符号距离场）渲染引擎，实现无需人工标注的解析几何多模态数据集生成。该框架包含四个专用 LLM 组件（生成器、形式化器、测量器、质量验证器），通过闭环反馈机制确保数据质量。产出 AnalyticGeo7K 数据集，包含 7,043 道经过验证的多模态解析几何问题，地面真值相对误差中位数仅为 0.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在数学推理方面取得显著进展，但解析几何领域研究严重不足，主要原因是缺乏标注样本。现有基准（如 GeoQA、Geometry3K、Conic10K）要么仅关注欧几里得平面几何，要么是纯文本形式，无法满足解析几何对坐标系统、圆锥曲线和代数-几何联合理解的需求。现有图表生成方法存在三大瓶颈：模板方法无法处理约束驱动的布局，生成模型缺乏精确渲染标注圆锥曲线的能力，且缺乏跨阶段的验证机制。

Method: 框架采用四组件流水线：1）Generator：通过前向/反向两种路径生成解析几何题目（反向路径基于 SymPy 验证的种子库锚定生成）；2）Formalizer：将自然语言问题转换为 CDL 程序，采用针对曲线类型的语法提示策略；3）SDF Engine：基于符号距离场的可微分渲染引擎，将 CDL 编译为可微分的 SDF，统一约束求解与图表渲染，对欠约束位置通过梯度下降自动求解；4）Measurer：通过物理信息隔离（任务提取器与视觉测量器分离）防止信息泄露，从渲染图表中提取真值答案。Quality Verifier 在三个门控位置（题目可解性、CDL 完备性、答案合理性）进行检查，失败时返回结构化反馈驱动上游重试，形成无人工标注的闭环质量控制。CDL 涵盖五种几何元素（直线、圆、椭圆、抛物线、双曲线）及十二种派生对象操作符。

Result: AnalyticGeo7K 数据集共生成 7,823 道候选题目，淘汰 780 道后保留 7,043 道验证问题，覆盖四种曲线类型和五种目标类型（面积、弦长、距离、角度、周长）。三个质量门控的最终通过率均≥99%（首次通过率分别为 99.1%、98.2%、95.1%）。对 164 道采样题目的人工核验显示，测量器获得的视觉真值与精确符号解的中位数相对误差为 0.70%（平均 2.84%），82.3% 的答案落在精确解 5% 误差范围内。抛物线-面积组合是最难类别（精度 93.97%），归因于多步视觉推理中误差累积。

Conclusion: FormalAnalyticGeo 成功解决了多模态解析几何数据生成的三大挑战——表示鸿沟、渲染鸿沟、验证鸿沟。通过形式化语言 CDL 与 SDF 渲染引擎的统一，使约束驱动布局、几何精确渲染和跨阶段验证成为可能。该框架无需任何人工标注即可扩展到任意规模，并产出 AnalyticGeo7K 这一目前唯一的大规模多模态解析几何数据集。作者将公开框架与数据集，并对八个 SOTA 模型进行了综合实证分析，为推进多模态空间与数学推理提供可操作洞察。区域渲染（如曲线与直线围成区域着色）是当前 CDL 规范的局限，作为未来工作方向。

Abstract: Math reasoning has achieved significant progress with the rapid advancement of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), however analytic geometry remains largely underexplored, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated samples. Existing diagram generation approaches struggle with analytic geometry: template methods cannot handle constraint-driven layouts, and generative models lack the geometric precision to render annotated conic curves correctly. We present FormalAnalyticGeo, a scalable framework for fully automatic generation of multimodal analytic geometry problems. Leveraging the rigor of formal languages, we design the framework around CDL (Condition Description Language), a formal intermediate representation that bridges free-form problem text with precise diagram rendering via a Signed Distance Field (SDF) engine. The framework employs four specialized LLM components in sequence: a Generator that produces diverse analytic geometry problems, a Formalizer that converts each problem into CDL for SDF-based rendering, a Measurer that extracts ground-truth answers through vision-based measurement on the rendered diagrams, and a Quality Verifier that checks outputs at three stages. Structured feedback from the Quality Verifier drives automatic retry, forming a closed loop that eliminates any need for human annotation. Applying FormalAnalyticGeo at scale yields AnalyticGeo7K, a dataset of over 7K verified multimodal problems, each with aligned text, diagram, formal annotation, and ground this http URL show that the generated problems achieve a median ground-truth relative error of 0.70\%, with 82.3\% of answers falling within 5\% of the exact symbolic solution. Our framework and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [232] [Resist and Update: Counterfactual Report Coordinates for Incentive-Compatible LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12985)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement failed after retries (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement failed after retries (llm_error). Original arXiv metadata is preserved. Error: AI returned empty structured response

Abstract: Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.

</details>


### [233] [Win by Silence: Deletion Non-Monotonicity, Autonomous Exploitation, and Typed-State Gating in LLM Plan Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12986)
*Aleh Manchuliantsau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM生成战略计划评估中的"沉默获胜"(Win-by-Silence)失败模式：在分期期望值评分器中，通过删除内部转换并保留下游终值可使评分反而提升。论文给出闭式刻画(命题1)，在26条冻结路径上验证该激励普遍存在且可被无指导的评分优化器自主发现，并提出基于类型化状态覆盖的GATE/DOCK门控机制来重定向优化搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的战略路线在被打分、选择和迭代优化时，可能通过删除必要的中间转换来获得更高评分——更短的路线携带更少成本和概率折扣，却仍声称相同终值。这不是评判不准确，而是评分在未被支付的中间状态变更上发放了价值。本文针对此遗漏激励(omission incentive)问题，研究RouteCast/PCSC实验轨道下分期期望值评分器的完整性漏洞。

Method: 1) 命题1给出删除内部转换时的评分变化闭式公式 Δ_k=(∏_{i<k}p_i)[c_k+(1-p_k)R_{k+1}]，并推导出正Δ的条件。2) 在26条冻结路线(57个可接受删除)上做穷举突变面验证(EXP A, 零模型调用)。3) 检测阶梯比较结构代理、盲语义法官、全上下文法官和类型化状态检测器(Phase 1/v1)。4) EXP B：评分寻优优化器，Arm1仅给评分反馈，Arm2加GATE评分释放裁决+未履行义务清单。5) EXP C：白盒编译器感知对抗性共作者，四种条件(v1, v1.5a源锚定, v1.5b增量索引成本下限, v1.5a+b)。6) GATE在有未覆盖接缝时拒绝释放评分(suspend-and-ask)，DOCK移除未覆盖路径上的终值。

Result: 命题1在57/57删除上与解析恒等式完全吻合(最大误差1.49×10⁻⁸美元)，26/26路线至少存在一个改善评分的删除位置，最后可接受接缝在26/26路线上均改善。Arm1无指导优化器在21/26路线中发现基线击败型未覆盖结构(54个改善修订中53个带未覆盖增量)。GATE对26/26静默路线拒绝释放评分，对0/26诚实路线误拒；Arm2中47/54拒绝后下一修订修复至覆盖结构，严格覆盖改善从1/26升至13/26。EXP C暴露注册来源边界：义务通道规避在四种条件下均为6/6，增量索引下限将击败诚实从6/6降至3/6，静默可融资性从5/6降至0/6，但未建立语义完备性。

Conclusion: 分期战略评分器确实可对遗漏进行奖励，且该奖励具有解析可刻画性、突变面上普遍存在、可被评分寻优自主发现。确定性类型化状态门控既能拒绝评分释放，又能在合作设置中重塑搜索方向朝向覆盖结构。但编译器感知共作者仍构成独立更难威胁——它需要不可共著的语义实质性支撑，而非仅自洽的注册记录。PCSC检测并中和模型中介类型化状态记录上的事后遗漏拼接，不验证任意LLM生成策略的语义完备性或现实质量。

Abstract: Plan evaluators can reward a strategic plan for becoming less explicit. This paper studies that failure in a staged expected-value scorer for LLM-generated venture routes. Proposition 1 gives the score change from deleting an interior transition while retargeting its predecessor and retaining downstream value: Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]. On a frozen 26-route cohort, all 57 admissible deletions matched the analytic identity and threshold sign, and every route had at least one score-improving deletion. A score-seeking optimizer, allowed to restructure routes but not told the exploit mechanism, found baseline-beating uncovered structures in 21/26 routes. GATE refused score release for 26/26 silenced routes with 0/26 honest suspensions; after refusal, 47/54 next revisions repaired to a covered structure, and strict covered improvement rose from 1/26 to 13/26. An adaptive compiler-aware co-author exposed the registry-provenance boundary: obligation-channel evasions remained 6/6 across all four v1/v1.5 conditions, while delta-indexed cost floors reduced beat-honest routes from 6/6 to 3/6 and fundability-by-silence from 5/6 to 0/6 without establishing semantic completeness. If a plan scores better only because it omits necessary work, the plan did not improve; the evaluation created an omission incentive. PCSC detects and neutralizes post-hoc omission splices over model-mediated typed-state records. In the cooperative setting tested, GATE acts as a deterministic search-shaping constraint, not merely a post-hoc filter. It does not verify the semantic completeness or real-world quality of arbitrary LLM-generated strategies.

</details>


### [234] [Dynamic Resource Allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13007)
*Jakub Kowalski,Adam Ciężkowski,Artur Krzyżyński,Mark H. M. Winands*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对不确定性环境下的Ensemble Determinization MCTS（ED-MCTS）提出了两类动态资源分配增强方法：动态决策树数量（Dynamic Number of Determinizations）和动态仿真预算分配（Dynamic Simulation Allocation）。在Jaipur、Lost Cities和Splendor三款桌游上的实验表明，部分配置能够统计显著地提升算法相对于Sample Open Loop MCTS（SOLMCTS）的胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS在确定性完全信息博弈中表现出色，但在含有随机性和隐藏信息的不确定性环境中面临挑战。ED-MCTS虽然是一种常用解决方案，但其决策树数量和仿真预算分配通常被视为需手动调优的常数，忽略了不同局面下不确定性程度和搜索需求的动态变化。本文旨在通过引入动态资源分配机制，使算法能够自适应地调整搜索资源分配，从而提升整体博弈强度。

Method: 论文提出两类动态增强方法：(1) 动态决策树数量——基于前L轮搜索中获胜动作的"边际"（margin）动态增减determinization树数量，使用参数M↑和M↓控制增减的稳定性阈值；(2) 动态仿真预算分配——提出五种策略：Greedy（贪心分配给最争议树）、UCB: win rate difference、UCB: visits proportion、Across-tree UCB（跨树UCB，将整体视为单一bandit）、Move Pruning（剪枝）及其+Freeze变体。此外还测试了四种最终动作选择机制：visits、score、voting、borda。实验在三种桌游（Jaipur、Lost Cities、Splendor）上进行，对手为SOLMCTS，分别在固定仿真数和固定时间预算下评估。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 静态配置中，5-10棵决策树通常表现最佳，相比单树baseline提升可达+13.2个百分点（Jaipur）；(2) visits和score聚合方法优于文献中常用的voting方法；(3) 动态树数量调整在Jaipur（+3.3pp）和Splendor（+5.1pp）上获得显著提升（使用voting聚合），但对Lost Cities效果不佳；(4) 动态预算分配中，Across-tree UCB配合voting在Jaipur和Splendor上一致改善，但树级UCB变体在visits聚合下表现糟糕；(5) 组合使用时增益非可加，Lost Cities例外（+3.4pp超过预测的+1.8pp）；(6) 时间预算下Splendor中静态summed-visits ED-MCTS达到72.3%胜率，Lost Cities中动态voting配置从47.6%提升至54.6%。

Conclusion: 论文成功引入了ED-MCTS动态资源分配的概念，证明部分增强方法（特别是动态树数量配合voting、Across-tree UCB）能带来统计显著的改进。但效果高度依赖游戏类型和聚合规则，并非通用最优解。未来工作应扩展至更多游戏、进行更精细的决策树数量搜索、测试与score-based选择的组合，并探索其他bandit分配方法的适用性。

Abstract: Simulation-based algorithms are especially suited for high-uncertainty environments such as adversarial board games with significant elements of randomness and hidden information. In particular, several Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) variants are commonly used in such domains. In this paper, we propose a series of enhancements for Ensemble Determinization MCTS, introducing two axes for dynamic resource allocation. First, Dynamic Number of Determinizations, increases or decreases the number of currently used determinization trees depending on the behavior of so-far search. Second, Dynamic Simulation Allocation, splits the simulation budget nonuniformly across the determinization trees, using simulation-to-simulation decisions to choose the tree with potentially the best knowledge gain. As benchmark domains, we used three popular tabletop games: Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor. Testing our proposed enhancements in iteration- and time-based settings showed that particular configurations yield a statistically significant increase in the algorithm's strength.

</details>


### [235] [Audio-Native Speech Recognition with a Frozen Discrete-Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13013)
*Harsha Vardhan Khurdula,Abhinav Kumar Singh,Yoeven D Khemlani,Vineet Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于冻结离散扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma，26B MoE）的语音识别方法，仅训练约 4200 万参数（投影器 + LoRA 适配器，占主干网络 0.16%），即可在 LibriSpeech test-clean 上达到 6.6% 的词错误率，并以约 8 步并行去噪完成转录，识别成本与文本长度解耦。研究核心贡献是揭示并解决了"接地死锁"问题：仅靠注意力路由的损失无法训练音频投影器，必须通过冻结输出头的 CTC 损失直接监督投影器才能打破僵局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动语音识别由自回归解码器主导，其按 token 串行生成的特性使得一段 30 秒音频需要数百次顺序前向传播，计算成本随转录长度线性增长。扩散语言模型可通过少量去噪步骤并行精炼整段序列，但此前尚不清楚能否让现成的通用扩散语言模型学会"听"音频——既不新训练解码器，也支持多语言。已有工作（TransFusion、Whisfusion）均为英语专用且从零训练扩散解码器。

Method: 方法由四个组件构成：(1) 冻结的 Whisper-small 编码器提取声学特征；(2) 可训练投影器将 1500 帧特征映射为 188 个音频 token（约 6.25 token/秒），采用三层卷积 + GELU 结构；(3) 冻结 DiffusionGemma 主干网络 26B MoE，使用统一随机 token 离散扩散（非吸收掩码方案）；(4) 编码器与解码器自注意力的 Q/K/V/O 投影上添加 rank-16 的 LoRA 适配器。训练使用三个损失：扩散 x₀ 预测损失、自回归辅助损失，以及关键的 CTC 损失。论文诊断了接地失败的机理：注意力路由损失的梯度 ∝ α_t·W_V⊤δ_t，初始化时注意力权重 α_t→0，投影器与注意力陷入互等对方的死锁；CTC 损失通过 ∂ℒ_ctc/∂𝐮_t = W_U⊤(𝐪_t-ρ_t) 直接监督投影器，无需经过注意力，从而打破死锁。推理时采用统一离散扩散采样器，固定 8 步并行去噪，温度由 0.8 线性降至 0.4，按熵阈值保留高置信预测并重噪其余位置。

Result: 在 LibriSpeech test-clean（100 小时干净子集训练）上达到 6.6% WER；多语言适配器（基于 FLEURS 六语种训练 + VoxPopuli 微调）在 FLEURS 英语 15.7% WER、印地语 15.8% CER、普通话 29.6% CER、VoxPopuli 英语 18.5% WER。识别速度方面，8 步去噪即可获得接近 48 步的精度（约 14.9× 实时），且去噪步数与转录长度无关。瓶颈消融实验表明：替换为 Whisper-large-v3 编码器或降低子采样率均无显著改善，提示当前限制为数据规模（约 219 小时音频）而非架构。相较自回归 Whisper 仍存在差距，尤其在多语言朗读语音上。

Conclusion: 冻结的语言模型能够学会听觉，前提是音频投影器必须通过直接监督（如 CTC）而非仅通过注意力来训练。损失曲线与 token 准确率在模型真正"接地"之前可能已表现良好，因此训练过程中需手动解码监控。一旦接地完成，扩散解码使识别成本独立于说话内容长度，约 8 步并行即可收敛。该方法在架构层面已验证有效，剩余差距主要源于训练音频数据量不足三个数量级于 Whisper。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition is dominated by autoregressive decoders that emit one token at a time. We ask whether a discrete diffusion language model can transcribe speech instead, refining a whole transcript in parallel over a small number of denoising steps. We train an audio-native interface for DiffusionGemma, a 26B mixture-of-experts model that generates text by uniform, random-token discrete diffusion rather than the absorbing-mask scheme common to recent diffusion language models. A frozen Whisper encoder supplies acoustic features, a lightweight projector maps them into the model embedding space, and low-rank adapters let the frozen backbone attend to the new modality. About 42M parameters are trained, which is 0.16 percent of the backbone. We find that the natural training objectives fail to ground the audio because their gradient reaches the projector only through attention that has already dismissed it. A connectionist temporal classification loss applied through the frozen output head breaks this deadlock. The resulting model reaches 6.6 percent word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length, and uses a single adapter trained on six languages, which we evaluate here on English, Hindi, and Mandarin.

</details>


### [236] [Do AI Agents Know When a Task Is Simple? Toward Complexity-Aware Reasoning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13034)
*Junjie Yin,Xinyu Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 E3 框架（Estimate-Execute-Expand），通过让 LLM 智能体先估算任务执行范围再以最小可行路径执行、在验证失败时渐进扩展上下文，解决 LLM 智能体在简单任务上过度读取上下文（maximum-context-first）的冗余问题。在 MSE-Bench（121 个确定性编辑任务）上，E3 以 100% 成功率匹配最强基线，成本降低 85%、token 降低 91%、读取文件减少 92%；在 gpt-4o 真实编辑 toml 库的 LLM-Case 验证中，E3 也是最精简、最快速的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 智能体在多步骤工程和信息工作流中普遍采用"先收集最大上下文再行动"的保守策略，将本应秒级的简单编辑（如两行图标替换）变成小型代码库审计。已有研究关注推理深度、模型路由、计算自适应，但缺少对"任务究竟需要多少执行范围"的预判能力——即判断任务难度、所需信息及最短可靠路径。冗余浪费在简单任务上比例最大，亟需将"难度评估"提升为一阶能力。

Method: 1) 形式化定义：最小充分执行（minimum-sufficient execution，式 2）与智能体认知冗余比 ACRR（式 3，归一化超出 oracle 的浪费成本）；2) 提出初始运行点 x₀=(d̂, ŝ, r̂, ĉ)（式 4），类比电力潮流求解中的 flat start/DC warm start；3) 设计 E3 三阶段框架——Estimate（基于词法线索+一次廉价探测估算难度/范围/风险/置信度）、Execute（按 x₀ 执行最小可行路径，分 3 级 scope 粒度）、Expand（验证失败时逐级扩展并复用缓存搜索结果）；4) 构建 MSE-Bench（121 个程序生成任务，分单文件/跨文件/仓库三级，含"诱饵"措辞变体，每任务带 oracle 轨迹）；5) LLM-Case 真实模型验证：以三个 system prompt（thorough/react/e3）在 gpt-4o 上编辑 vendored 的 toml 0.10.2，用真实 pytest 评分并测量 oracle；6) 电力系统案例：三母线 Newton-Raphson 潮流实验量化初始点误差对迭代次数与收敛率的影响。

Result: 主实验（121 任务）：Max-Context-First 100% 成功但 ACRR=12.9；Fixed ReAct 仅 66.9%（漏掉全部 L3）；Adaptive Retrieval（强基线）100% 成功 ACRR=1.21；E3 100% 成功 ACRR=0.55，相对 MCF 降 latency 58.6%/tokens 90.9%/files 92.2%/cost 84.9%，相对 AR 降 cost 16%、files 66.8%。冗余在 L1 最大（MCF ACRR=22.1），E3 跨级平稳（0.26–0.73）。消融：去 Expand 成功降至 85.1%（丢全部 18 个诱饵 L3），去 Estimate cost 升 20%。稳健性：4000 组随机成本权重下，E3 在 99.8% 中为最廉价的全成功策略；held-out 改写措辞使估计准确率从 85.1% 降至 66.9%，但 E3 仍 100% 成功、cost 仅升 8.7%。LLM-Case（gpt-4o，5 任务×3 轮）：前沿模型仅读 1–4 个文件（远比模拟 MCF 克制），E3 是 token 最少（-18% vs thorough，-4% vs ReAct）、延迟最低（-18%/-5%）的策略；L3 诱饵任务对所有策略都耗 token 260–350k 且可靠性随机，过度读取者还触发 429 限流或 step 超限。电力案例：平坦/DC 暖启动误差小时 Newton-Raphson 3 步收敛 100%，误差 ≥2.4 时发散，量化"好初始点在收敛域内"的直觉。

Conclusion: 研究表明 LLM 智能体最缺的不是更强推理或更多工具，而是一阶的"任务复杂度感知与执行范围估计"能力；E3（Estimate-Execute-Expand）通过乐观初始运行点 + 验证驱动的渐进扩展，以架构方式而非词法技巧保证既快且对，在简单任务上消除最大相对冗余、在复杂任务上不低于强自适应基线。论文将"任务感知执行"定位为工程导向 AI（EGAI）的一环——智能体的努力应对齐并验证于任务的工程现实——并发布 MSE-Bench 与 LLM-Case 框架以促进后续多模型、SWE-bench 规模评估与学习式估计器研究。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering and informatics workflows, yet they rarely ask how much effort a task actually requires. They often follow a maximum-context-first strategy--re-reading files and dependencies they have already seen--turning a one-line edit into a small code-base audit. We argue the missing capability is task-aware execution-scope estimation: judging a task's difficulty, the information it truly needs, and the shortest reliable path before committing budget. We formalize minimum-sufficient execution and the Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR), and propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand): the agent estimates an initial operating point, executes a minimum viable path, and expands scope only when verification fails. On MSE-Bench--a deterministic benchmark of 121 edits in a capability-controlled simulator--E3 matches the strongest baseline's 100% success while cutting cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and further beats a strong adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%; the gains survive held-out instruction wording and essentially every cost weighting. A companion real-model harness (LLM-Case) corroborates the effect on a live gpt-4o agent editing a real open-source library, with every candidate patch graded by actually running the project's real pytest suite against a measured oracle: the over-reading is milder but real, and E3 is the leanest and fastest policy at comparable task success--its one shortfall a provider rate-limit, not a wrong edit. We frame this as a controlled probe of execution redundancy, not a measurement of any deployed agent, and position task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI (EGAI)--agents whose effort is anchored in the engineering reality of the task. We release the framework and benchmark.

</details>
